Abstract: Content-based image retrieval (CBIR) in neuroimaging enables the identification of structurally similar brain scans, supporting diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment planning; however, existing methods are often limited to small datasets, single brain regions, or coarse class labels, thereby restricting their clinical utility and generalizability.
  Here, we present NeuroCBIR, a framework for fast and flexible retrieval of both whole-brain and region-specific 3D T1w MRI scans. A total of 103 cortical and subcortical regions are extracted to enable both whole-brain and region-level queries.
  NeuroCBIR leverages latent representations learned by a variational autoencoder (VAE) combined with contrastive learning, producing scan-specific embeddings that capture anatomical patterns. These embeddings were evaluated for subject re-identification, zero-shot age prediction, and zero-shot multi-class pathology stratification. Re-identification performance was high across both whole-brain and brain-region levels (mean average precision across the top-5 retrieved images (mAP@5) >= 98.4%), with robust generalization across datasets and acquisition conditions. While NeuroCBIR is not trained for age prediction or pathology stratification, zero-shot evaluations for these two tasks demonstrate that the embeddings encode meaningful information for downstream tasks.
  Embedding extraction on a 4-core CPU required approximately 18.7 s per scan, whereas similarity search was effectively instantaneous (less than 0.01 s).
  NeuroCBIR is publicly available for brain MRI with more than 26,000 precomputed T1w MRI embeddings. It supports reproducible research, region-specific flexibility, and clinically meaningful personalized diagnostic support. The software is available at https://github.com/minnelab/NeuroCBIR.

</details>


### [209] [Harmful Content Generation in Text-to-Image Models: Capabilities and Moderation Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06503)
*Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Manos Schinas,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了五个开源文本到图像模型在有害内容生成上的风险，发现标准与社区微调模型均存在高比例的有害图像生成，且现有审核与检测系统存在明显漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型能生成高度逼真图像，但也带来被滥用的风险。尽管已有安全机制，但对其在真实攻击下有效性的系统性评估仍然有限，因此需要全面衡量模型的安全防护能力。

Method: 作者构建自动化流水线，将合法新闻标题转化为针对色情、暴力/血腥、有害刻板印象、自残和仇恨言论的不安全提示词，评估五个开源文本到图像模型，包括带内置安全机制的标准模型和绕过限制的社区微调变体。随后对1,500张生成图像进行人工评估，并进一步测试自动审核系统与合成图像检测器。

Result: 有害内容生成率很高：血腥类89.2%、色情类47.6%、有害刻板印象43.6%、仇恨言论46.0%、自残类34.5%（主要为暴力画面）。社区微调变体尤其易受色情提示攻击；有害提示下图像质量基本保持，FLUX.1-dev有30.9%的案例生成清晰逼真的有害图像。自动审核系统存在大量漏检，仅在良性数据集上训练的合成图像检测器对露骨内容表现更差，而多样化训练数据可改善检测。

Conclusion: 当前生成模型的防护、审核系统与合成图像检测均存在明显局限，亟需更强的防御手段以应对大规模滥用风险。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models can produce highly realistic imagery but also raise concerns about harmful misuse. While safety mechanisms exist, systematic evaluations of their effectiveness against realistic attacks remain limited. We present a systematic evaluation of harmful content generation across five open text-to-image models using an automated pipeline that transforms legitimate news captions into unsafe prompts targeting sexually explicit content, violence/gore, harmful stereotypes, self-harm, and hate speech. We evaluate both standard models with built-in safety mechanisms and community fine-tuned variants that bypass content restrictions. A human evaluation of 1,500 generated images shows high harmful-content generation rates: 89.2% for gore-related prompts, 47.6% for sexually explicit content, 43.6% for harmful stereotypes, 46.0% for hate speech, and 34.5% for self-harm, predominantly through graphic violence. Models show substantial capability for generating violent and stereotypical content, while community fine-tuned variants are particularly vulnerable to sexually explicit prompts. Generation quality is largely preserved under harmful prompting, producing imagery of sufficient fidelity to pose risks for disinformation and abuse; FLUX.1-dev produces clearly realistic harmful images in 30.9% of cases. We further evaluate automated moderation systems and find substantial detection gaps that allow unsafe images to evade filtering. Finally, we assess synthetic image detectors and show that models trained only on benign datasets perform worse on explicit content, while more diverse training data improves detection, highlighting semantic distribution gaps in current approaches. These findings expose limitations in current generation safeguards, moderation systems, and synthetic image detection, highlighting the need for stronger defenses against misuse at scale.

</details>


### [210] [A General Pipeline for Dense Illuminant Estimation via Physically Based Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06508)
*Luca Cogo,Gianmarco Corti,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从物理渲染3D场景中提取稠密光源色度图的通用流程，生成74,321张合成图像用于预训练光源估计模型，单光源和多光源估计性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光源估计是计算摄影的基础问题，但基于学习的方法受限于缺乏大规模且具有准确光源真值的数据集，数据获取门槛高。

Method: 提出通用且可复用的流程，通过改造已有的3D场景集合，在可控光照条件下生成像素级光源标注，构建大规模合成数据集（74,321张图像），用于单光源和多光源估计模型的预训练。

Result: 在多种先进架构上的实验表明，合成预训练可持续提升性能：单光源估计最高提升28%，多光源估计最高提升57%，尤其在数据稀缺情况下效果显著。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成流程为光源估计方法的预训练提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Illuminant estimation is a fundamental problem in computational photography, as it enables the correction of color shifts induced by varying lighting conditions. While learning-based methods have demonstrated strong performance, their progress is hindered by the limited availability of large-scale datasets with accurate illuminant ground-truth. In this work, we propose a general and reusable pipeline to derive dense illuminant chromaticity maps from physically based 3D-rendered scenes. By repurposing an existing 3D scene collection, our approach enables the systematic generation of pixel-wise illuminant annotations under controlled lighting conditions, effectively lowering the barrier to data acquisition for learning-based illuminant estimation. Using this pipeline, we generate a large-scale synthetic set of 74,321 images, which we employ for pre-training both single- and multi-illuminant estimation models. Extensive experiments with state-of-the-art architectures show that synthetic pre-training consistently improves performance, with gains of up to 28% for single-illuminant estimation and up to 57% for multi-illuminant estimation, particularly in data-scarce regimes. These findings demonstrate that synthetic data generation pipelines offer an effective and scalable solution for the pre-training of illuminant estimation methods.

</details>


### [211] [BrainTRACE: Tracing Longitudinal, Multimodal, and Volumetric Evidence in Brain MRI Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06571)
*Qizhen Lan,Mengchen Fan,Hang Zhang,Jingwei Duan,Moule Lin,Jialin Chen,Baocheng Geng,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainTRACE是一个基于报告的纵向脑MRI基准，用7,273个VQA实例审查视觉语言模型能否追溯证据链，发现现有模型只能识别孤立视觉线索，难以形成有依据的纵向解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI解读本质是纵向临床推理：需对比序列研究、整合多序列信息、在容积解剖中定位病灶，并转化为有报告依据的评估。现有医学VQA与3D影像基准多以孤立图像、静态容积或无依据的报告式答案评估脑MRI，掩盖了支撑临床有效性的证据链中的失败。

Method: 构建BrainTRACE基准，包含来自1,778名纵向患者、7,299次MRI检查、约29k个共配准3D MRI序列容积的7,273个计分VQA实例。基准按五个临床推理层级组织（从采集识别到病例级综合），并按证据需求分类（纵向比较、报告依据引用、多序列整合、容积空间证据）。支持兼容标准VLM接口的渲染输入、3D证据条件，以及分解式病例推理赛道，审查纵向证据链的六个步骤。

Result: 对20种VLM配置的评估显示，当前系统能识别孤立的视觉线索，但很少将其组合成有依据的纵向解读。

Conclusion: 发布基准规范、评估列表、评分实现、评分细则与审计记录格式，以支持脑MRI VLM评估的可复现进展。

Abstract: Brain MRI interpretation is a longitudinal clinical reasoning problem: radiologists compare serial studies, integrate information across MRI sequences, localize findings within volumetric anatomy, and translate this evidence into report-grounded assessments. Existing medical VQA and 3D imaging benchmarks capture important parts of this workflow, but often evaluate brain MRI through isolated images, static volumes, or ungrounded report-style answers, thereby obscuring failures in the evidence chain that support clinical validity. We introduce BrainTRACE, a report-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether vision-language models can trace the evidence structure required for longitudinal brain MRI interpretation. BrainTRACE contains 7,273 scored VQA instances derived from 1,778 longitudinal patients, 7,299 MRI studies, and approximately 29k co-registered 3D MRI sequence volumes. The benchmark is organized by five levels of clinical reasoning, from acquisition recognition to case-level synthesis, and by evidence demands covering longitudinal comparison, report-grounded references, multi-sequence integration, and volumetric spatial evidence. BrainTRACE supports rendered inputs compatible with standard VLM interfaces, a 3D-evidence condition, and a decomposed case-reasoning track that audits six steps in a longitudinal evidence chain. Evaluation of 20 VLM configurations shows that current systems can identify isolated visual cues but rarely compose them into grounded longitudinal interpretations. We release the benchmark specification, evaluation lists, scoring implementation, scoring rubrics, and audit-record format to support reproducible progress in brain MRI VLM evaluation.

</details>


### [212] [FrontVeg V2: A Training-Free Software Framework for Foreground-Aware Zero-Shot Plant Trait Segmentation in High-Resolution Images of Trellised Crops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06575)
*Abdoul Djalil Ousseini Hamza,Herearii Metuarea,Corentin Lothod{é},Morgane Roth,Jacem Ben Hamden,Eric Duch{ê}ne,Lionel Ley,David Alletru,David Rousseau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrontVeg V2 是一个开源、免训练的前景感知零样本分割框架，用于高分辨率棚架作物图像中植物性状的分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高分辨率棚架作物图像中进行植物性状分割时，邻近植株行会产生大量误检，且现有方法往往需要针对特定任务重新训练模型。为此，作者希望构建一个无需任务特定重训练、可复用、前景感知的零样本分割框架。

Method: 该流程结合了单目深度估计、基于谷值感知深度阈值（Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding）的自动前景提取、分块零样本分割、基于图的掩码组装（Graph-Based Mask Assembly）以及几何感知融合。当前实现集成了 Depth Anything V2（DAV2）与 SAM3，并支持命令行批量处理和 Napari 图形界面两种使用方式。

Result: 该框架能够分割植物器官和病害症状，同时减少由邻近植被行引起的误检，实现了前景感知的零样本分割效果。

Conclusion: FrontVeg V2 提供了一个可复用的框架，支持多作物、多性状的数字表型分析，且无需任务特定的模型重训练。

Abstract: FrontVeg V2 is an open-source, training-free software framework for foregroundaware zero-shot segmentation of plant traits in high-resolution images of trellised crops. The pipeline combines monocular depth estimation, automatic foreground extraction using Valley-Aware Depth Thresholding, tiled zero-shot segmentation, Graph-Based Mask Assembly, and geometry-aware fusion. This design enables plant organs and disease symptoms to be segmented while reducing detections arising from neighboring vegetation rows. The current implementation integrates Depth Anything V2 (DAV2) and SAM3 and can be used through both command-line batch processing and a Napari graphical interface. FrontVeg V2 provides a reusable framework for multi-crop, multi-trait digital phenotyping without task-specific model retraining.

</details>


### [213] [Keepsake: Selective Spatial Memory for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06588)
*Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Kunyang Li,Yuzhang Shang,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Keepsake，一种在线、免训练的空间记忆控制器，通过"相对可替代性"动态评估并管理固定容量记忆，在保持生成一致性的同时大幅压缩存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序可控视频生成依赖持久记忆维持场景一致性。现有方法存在两类问题：全历史方法存储与检索成本无界；选择性构建方法仅做一次性保留决策，不再随记忆库演化重新评估。二者共同忽视了一个核心问题——随着生成历史演变，哪些已存观测仍应保留？作者的关键洞见是：观测的价值并非固定，而是关系性的，取决于当前记忆库中可替代项的数量。

Method: Keepsake 在每次更新时，对保留的与新生成的观测构建"位姿-外观图"，结合相机位姿邻近性与视觉相似性。设计保留优先级，同时刻画强替代项的数量与最近替代项的相似度，从而持续重估记忆价值：保留缺乏替代支持的观测，驱逐高度可替代的观测。该方法仅修改持久记忆的更新规则，不改变宿主生成器、去噪调度与检索规则，且无需训练。

Result: 在 MemCam 与 WorldMem 上，Keepsake 在固定记忆预算下提升了 FVD 与 LPIPS。在 180 秒的 MemCam 轨迹中，仅保留 5,397 帧中的 32 帧，同时将 FVD 降低 35.1%。

Conclusion: 记忆价值应被视为随记忆库演化的关系量而非固定属性。基于此的在线、免训练固定容量记忆控制策略，可在显著压缩存储的同时提升长时序视频生成的一致性质量。

Abstract: Long-horizon camera-controlled video generation relies on persistent memory to maintain scene consistency. Existing systems follow two strategies to achieve this consistency. Full-history approaches retain all generated observations, causing unbounded storage and retrieval costs. Selective-construction approaches reduce redundancy, but make one-time retention decisions that are never revisited, even as an observation's value changes with the evolving memory bank. Both strategies leave a shared question unresolved: as the generated history evolves, which stored observations should still remain in memory? Our key insight is that the value of a stored observation is not fixed, but relational: it depends on the alternatives currently available in the memory bank. A view supported by many geometrically and visually similar substitutes can be relinquished with little loss of coverage, whereas an observation with few viable alternatives should remain regardless of age. We introduce Keepsake, an online, training-free controller for fixed-capacity spatial memory. At each update, Keepsake constructs a pose-appearance graph over retained and newly generated observations, combining camera-pose proximity with visual similarity. A retention priority jointly captures the number of strong substitutes and the similarity of the closest alternative, allowing Keepsake to continually reassess memory value, preserve observations with little alternative support, and evict highly replaceable ones under a fixed budget. The controller modifies only the persistent-memory update; the host generator, denoising schedule, and retrieval rule remain unchanged. Across MemCam and WorldMem, Keepsake improves FVD and LPIPS under a fixed memory budget. On 180-second MemCam trajectories, it retains only 32 of 5,397 frames while reducing FVD by 35.1%.

</details>


### [214] [VGGT-Bridge: Beyond Sequential Pose Graphs via Coarse-Stride Skip Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06594)
*Sungjae Choi,Hanna Bae,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VGGT-Bridge，通过长程跳连边与反向漂移校正，无需重训练即可显著降低分块对齐式前馈三维重建的累积漂移，在多个数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈视觉几何变换器（如 VGGT）可单次前向重建稠密三维结构，但二次注意力复杂度使其难以扩展到数千帧长序列。分块对齐框架通过重叠分块建位姿图缓解该问题，然而现有方法仅连接序列相邻分块，逐帧小误差沿链累积形成大尺度漂移。

Method: 提出 VGGT-Bridge：在分块对齐框架中引入长程跳连边，直接约束非相邻分块，无需重训练；对稀疏采样的粗分块运行 VGGT，使每个粗分块将远端细分块桥接为单一直接约束；将 VGGT 的首帧尺度偏差转化为漂移校正，通过反向输入选定粗分块实现；并提出回路感知策略，使该反向操作与既有回路闭合兼容。

Result: 相较 SwiftVGGT 基线，VGGT-Bridge 在 KITTI Odometry 上 ATE 降低 28.3%，Virtual KITTI 降低 18.8%，Waymo Open 降低 10.0%，在所有分块对齐方法中取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过长程跳连边与反向漂移校正，VGGT-Bridge 有效抑制了链式累积漂移，在不重训练的前提下提升了长序列前馈三维重建精度，优于现有分块对齐方法。

Abstract: Feed-forward visual geometry transformers such as VGGT reconstruct dense 3D structure from images in a single forward pass, simplifying multi-view 3D reconstruction. However, their quadratic attention complexity makes them difficult to scale to long sequences with thousands of frames. Chunk-and-align frameworks address this by splitting a long sequence into overlapping chunks and stitching their local reconstructions into a pose graph. Yet existing methods connect only sequentially adjacent chunks, so small per-frame errors accumulate along the chain into large-scale drift. To move beyond sequential edges, we propose VGGT-Bridge, which adds long-range skip edges that directly constrain non-adjacent chunks without retraining. By running VGGT on sparsely sampled coarse chunks, each coarse chunk bridges distant fine chunks into a single direct constraint. We further turn VGGT's first-frame scale bias into a drift correction by feeding selected coarse chunks in reverse, and a loop-aware policy keeps this reversal compatible with existing loop closures. VGGT-Bridge reduces ATE by 28.3% on KITTI Odometry, 18.8% on Virtual KITTI, and 10.0% on Waymo Open over the SwiftVGGT baseline, achieving the best performance among all chunk-and-align methods.

</details>


### [215] [Analysis of SWIR Imaging Detection Performance Under Adverse Environmental Conditions for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06596)
*Rohan Mehra,Alexandre Riffard,Yannis Loumouamou,Mathieu Labussière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了RGB与短波红外(SWIR)在RASMD数据集上的目标检测性能，并提出传感器优势挖掘框架，发现SWIR在眩光、雨滴、低对比度和远距离等安全关键场景下具有互补优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWIR成像被视为自动驾驶有前景的模态，但其相较RGB的实际收益在多样条件下仍缺乏充分的量化刻画。

Method: 在RASMD数据集上进行配对RGB-SWIR目标检测对比，覆盖四种天气条件与两种实时检测架构，并采用多种微调策略对标统一真值；同时提出结合多模型一致性与定向人工检查的传感器优势挖掘框架，利用基本无标注的配对数据识别某一模态更可靠的场景。

Result: 总体而言RGB在多数场景下表现相当或更优，而RF-DETR在多变条件下鲁棒性更强；分析显示SWIR在四种安全关键情形中优势明显：挡风玻璃眩光、玻璃上水滴、低对比度目标可见性以及远距离车辆检测。

Conclusion: SWIR应被视为一种互补模态，用于在罕见但具挑战性的条件下增强感知。数据集可应请求获取，代码与训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Short-wave infrared (SWIR) imaging has emerged as a promising modality for autonomous driving, yet its practical benefits over RGB remain poorly characterized across diverse conditions. This paper presents a systematic comparative study of paired RGB and SWIR object detection on the RASMD dataset, covering four weather conditions and two real-time detection architectures, with various fine-tunings evaluated against a unified ground truth. Overall, RGB demonstrates comparable or superior performance in most scenarios, while RF-DETR exhibits greater robustness across varying conditions. Beyond aggregate metrics, we propose a sensor-dominance mining framework that combines multi-model agreement with targeted manual inspection to identify scenarios where one sensing modality provides more reliable detections using largely unannotated paired data. This analysis reveals that SWIR offers clear advantages in four safety-critical situations, including windshield glare, water droplets on the windshield, low-contrast object visibility, and long-range vehicle detection. The findings suggest that SWIR should be viewed as a complementary modality that enhances perception in rare but challenging conditions. The datasets will be available upon request, and all code and trained model weights are publicly released at https://github.com/comsee-research/swir-adverse-env-analysis.

</details>


### [216] [Multitask Conditional Generative Adversarial Network Enables Automatic Whole Knee Cartilage and Menisci Segmentation and Reliable T1\r{ho} and T2 Quantification Without High-Resolution Morphological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06602)
*Ahmed Tahseen Minhaz,Richard Lartey,Zhiyuan Zhang,Jeehun Kim,Kunio Nakamura,Mingrui Yang,Jiasen Zhang,Weihong Guo,Naveen Subhas,Carl S. Winalski,Xiaojuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MT-cGAN，从qMRI回波图像直接合成DESS样图像并分割软骨与半月板，性能优于现有cGAN，且显著减少扫描时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期骨关节炎的定量MRI（qMRI）检测依赖准确的软骨和半月板分割，但传统方法需要耗时且昂贵的3D高分辨率DESS MRI扫描，限制了临床推广。

Method: 开发多任务条件生成对抗网络（MT-cGAN），从qMRI回波图像同时合成DESS样图像并分割组织。回顾性研究纳入三个队列共508个膝关节MRI体数据（361名受试者，平均年龄40.4±12.2岁，179名女性）。以DESS图像经预训练模型加人工校正生成真值分割掩膜，并从MAPSS回波图像计算T1ρ和T2图。以Dice分数评估分割精度，以变异系数（CV）评估T1ρ和T2定量。

Result: MT-cGAN分割性能最佳，所有软骨和半月板分区平均Dice为0.84（范围0.80–0.86），显著优于带迁移学习的最新条件GAN模型（平均Dice 0.82，p<0.001，Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。在弛豫定量方面，MT-cGAN与参考DESS方案一致性最高，CV最低（T1ρ: 1.84%，T2: 1.81%）。

Conclusion: MT-cGAN能直接从回波图像准确分割软骨和半月板，并提供可靠的T1ρ和T2定量。该流程无需单独的形态学DESS扫描，缩短扫描时间，有助于推动qMRI的临床转化。

Abstract: Early osteoarthritis detection through quantitative MRI (qMRI) requires accurate cartilage and meniscus segmentation, traditionally necessitating time-consuming, costly 3D high-resolution Double Echo Steady-State (DESS) MRI scans. This study developed a multi-task conditional generative adversarial network (MT-cGAN) to simultaneously synthesize DESS-like images and segment tissues directly from qMRI echo images. This retrospective study evaluated 508 knee MRI volumes from 361 subjects (mean age: $40.4 \pm 12.2$ years; 179 female) across three cohorts. Ground truth segmentation masks were generated from DESS images using a pretrained model with manual correction, and $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ maps were computed from magnetization-prepared angle-modulated partitioned $k$-space spoiled gradient echo snapshots (MAPSS) echo images. MT-cGAN was trained to jointly synthesize DESS-like images and segment cartilage and meniscus directly from echo images. Model performance was evaluated using Dice score for segmentation accuracy and coefficient of variation (CV) for $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ quantification. MT-cGAN achieved the highest segmentation performance, mean Dice score 0.84 (range: 0.80--0.86) across all cartilage and meniscus compartments and significantly outperformed the state-of-the-art conditional GAN model with transfer learning (mean Dice, 0.82; $p < 0.001$, Wilcoxon signed-rank test). For relaxometry quantification, MT-cGAN demonstrated the highest consistency with the reference DESS protocol, yielding the lowest CV ($T_{1ρ}$: 1.84%, $T_2$: 1.81%). The proposed MT-cGAN accurately segmented cartilage and menisci while providing reliable $T_{1ρ}$ and $T_2$ quantification directly from echo images. By eliminating the need for separate morphological DESS scans, this workflow reduces required scan times to facilitate the clinical translation of qMRI.

</details>


### [217] [Lens3D: Target-Conditioned Visual Foveation for Fine-Grained 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06611)
*Junming Huang,Zini Chen,Shuaiying Hou,Chi Wang,Qiang Dai,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lens3D，通过外部视觉辅助与知识蒸馏提升 3D 大语言模型对细粒度属性及小目标的物体理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 大语言模型常忽视细粒度属性和视觉显著性较低的物体与部件，即使场景视频中存在相关证据也难以捕捉。

Method: 包含 LensUnd 与 LensDistill 两个流程：LensUnd 采用 3D 定位为外部 2D 视觉语言模型选择信息量大且互补的视角，支持细粒度物体描述、小目标定位与细粒度问答；LensDistill 通过详细描述监督将细粒度知识迁移至 3D LLM。同时构建 LensBench 评估集。

Result: 在 Video-3D LLM 与 3DRS 上的实验表明，LensDistill 显著提升细粒度物体描述能力，同时保持原有定位与场景级问答性能。LensBench 包含 2,068 个物体，每个物体有 3 条银标准参考描述。

Conclusion: 结果证明将外部获取的细粒度知识迁移至原生 3D 大语言模型是可行的。

Abstract: Existing 3D large language models often overlook fine-grained attributes and less visually salient objects and parts, even when relevant evidence is present in scene videos. We introduce Lens3D to improve fine-grained object understanding through external visual assistance and knowledge transfer. Its LensUnd pipeline adopts 3D localization to select informative, complementary views for an external 2D vision-language model, supporting fine-grained object captioning, small-object grounding, and fine-grained object question answering. LensDistill transfers the resulting fine-grained knowledge to 3D LLMs through detailed caption supervision, enabling captioning from native inputs without external VLM calls. We also construct LensBench, a held-out evaluation set of 2,068 objects with three silver-standard reference descriptions per object. Experiments with Video-3D LLM and 3DRS demonstrate that LensDistill substantially improves fine-grained object captioning while preserving existing grounding and scene-level QA performance. These results establish the feasibility of transferring externally acquired fine-grained knowledge into native 3D LLMs.

</details>


### [218] [Video Encoders Built on Image Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06616)
*Jusheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang,Longqi Cai,Liangzhe Yuan,Yuxiao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"先保留帧内表示、再联合分配、最后时序交互"的紧凑视频编码路径，在仅用约28%-35%视觉token的情况下达到全图像路径的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码器的设计决定了帧间何时交互以及哪些帧级视觉证据对语言模型可见。原生视频路径在视觉编码阶段就耦合相邻帧；图像路径保持各帧独立表示，但转发全部图像token时视觉token开销巨大。作者因此提出核心问题：能否用图像表示构建一个紧凑的视频编码器。

Method: 将常被耦合的三种操作解耦：逐帧表示、跨帧token分配、时序交互。冻结的图像编码器首先生成帧级候选；问题感知选择器依据相关性、多样性和跨帧对应关系，在帧间分配固定token预算；随后轻量级学习refiner读取邻帧上下文，仅对保留的锚点写入残差更新。该设计保持源位置不变，并将视觉输出维持在固定预算。

Result: 在13个基准、3个视觉-语言骨干上，该路径以约28%-35%的视觉token匹配全图像聚合性能。在Qwen3-VL-8B上，13基准宏平均62.75、1,535视觉token，对比全图像路径62.58（4,424 token）、原生Conv3D 59.49（2,212 token）。在Qwen3-VL-32B上宏平均66.28，对比图像路径66.09，并带来2.16倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 紧凑视频编码不必然需要早期时序混合：可以先生成帧级证据，再进行联合分配，并在选择之后做时序上下文化。

Abstract: The design of a video encoder determines when frames begin to interact and which frame-specific visual evidence remains accessible to the language model. Native video pathways couple neighboring frames during visual encoding, whereas image pathways preserve independently computed frame representations but incur a much larger visual-token cost when all image tokens are forwarded. We ask a basic question: whether a compact video encoder can instead be built on image representations. To answer this question, we separate three operations that are often coupled: per-frame representation, cross-frame token allocation, and temporal interaction. A frozen image encoder first produces frame-specific candidates. A question-aware selector then allocates a fixed token budget across frames using relevance, diversity, and cross-frame correspondence, after which a lightweight learned refiner reads neighboring-frame context and writes residual updates only to the retained anchors. This preserves source positions and keeps the visual output at the fixed budget. Across 13 benchmarks and three vision-language backbones, the resulting pathway matches full-image aggregate performance while using only about 28%-35% of its visual tokens. Specifically, on Qwen3-VL-8B, it achieves a 13-benchmark macro-average of 62.75 with 1,535 visual tokens, compared with 62.58 for the full Image pathway at 4,424 tokens and 59.49 for native Conv3D at 2,212 tokens. On Qwen3-VL-32B, it reaches a 13-benchmark macro-average of 66.28, compared with 66.09 for Image, while providing a 2.16x end-to-end speedup. These results show that compact video encoding does not require early temporal mixing: frame-specific evidence can be preserved first, allocated jointly, and temporally contextualized after selection.

</details>


### [219] [Talk Like You: Imitating How You Speak in Real-Time Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06658)
*Baiqin Wang,Zhixing Ding,Jijie Li,Jiankuo Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TalkLikeYou框架，在运动空间建模个体说话习惯，用单步Flow Matching实现实时、可定制的说话头生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话头生成方法视觉逼真、唇形同步好，但忽视用户个性化定制，难以建模和捕捉高度细粒度、个体间相似的口型运动习惯，导致面部运动过于统一，缺乏个体化发音模式。

Method: 在运动空间建模说话习惯；采用Flow Matching，推理时仅需一步采样实现实时生成；采用两阶段模仿学习策略捕捉习惯间的细微差异；用户可通过数据集预设风格或参考视频指定目标习惯；提出新指标PLAD，将嘴部运动投影到代表性发音轴以评估模仿准确性与生成多样性。

Result: 大量实验表明，TalkLikeYou能实时生成高质量说话头，并相比先前方法显著提升说话习惯模仿效果；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了个性化说话习惯建模与实时生成的问题，为定制化说话头生成提供了新方案。

Abstract: In daily life, each person exhibits unique speaking habits, leading to subtle yet consistent lip-shape variations even when pronouncing the same word. Although recent talking head generation methods have achieved impressive visual fidelity and lip synchronization, they largely overlook user-specific customization, especially the motion patterns that characterize individual speaking habits. These habits are difficult to model and capture, as their motion patterns are highly fine-grained and often similar across individuals. As a result, many approaches produce overly uniform facial motions and fail to capture diverse, person-specific articulation patterns. To address this, we propose TalkLikeYou, an efficient framework that imitates how a target person speaks in talking head generation. Our method models habit in motion-space and achieves real-time performance through Flow Matching with only one sampling step during inference. We further adopt a two-stage imitation learning strategy to capture subtle distinctions between habits, allowing users to specify a target habit through either a preset style from the dataset or a reference video. In addition, we introduce a new metric PLAD that projects mouth motions onto representative articulation axes to evaluate imitation accuracy and generation diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TalkLikeYou generates high-quality talking heads in real-time and significantly improves speaking habit imitation compared with prior methods. The code is available at: https://github.com/BQ-Wang0511/TalkLikeYou

</details>


### [220] [Cross-dataset harmonization for robust endoscopic image analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06663)
*Romil Imtiaz,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对内窥镜图像分析中因不同内窥镜色彩分布差异导致模型跨数据集泛化能力下降的问题，提出一种简单的基于参考的图像协调方法，在CIE-Lab空间进行统计通道变换，使目标图像向参考数据集外观靠拢，从而显著提升跨数据集息肉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在来自不同内窥镜的图像上表现通常下降，主要原因是不同设备的图像传感器与光源不同，造成图像色彩分布差异。此前研究虽指出该挑战，但尚未明确解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出基于参考的图像协调方法：从选定的参考数据集在CIE-Lab色彩空间中提取全局颜色统计量，并对每张目标图像应用按通道的统计变换，将其外观映射到参考数据集的风格，从而减小数据集间全局外观差异。评估采用柔性结肠镜和胶囊内镜数据集的息肉检测场景，并使用数据集级交叉验证协议。

Result: 所提出的协调方法在跨数据集性能上持续提升，最高提升达30.7%，优于相关基线和最新方法，表明跨数据集性能下降的很大一部分来源于低层外观差异，且无需重新训练即可缓解。

Conclusion: 数据集间的低层外观差异是泛化能力下降的重要来源，通过简单的参考式图像协调即可有效缓解，为内窥镜图像分析中的跨域泛化提供了一种无需重训练的实用解决方案。

Abstract: A significant problem in endoscopic image analysis is that the machine learning (ML) models used for this purpose usually underperform when applied on images acquired from endoscopes that are different from those used to acquire the images of their training set. The main difference of the images originating from different endoscopes is their color distributions, which depend both on the image sensors and the light sources used. Although previous studies have highlighted this challenge, to the best of our knowledge it has not been previously explicitly tackled. This study focuses on this problem and proposes very simple but impactful method. It implements a reference-based image harmonization that reduces global appearance differences between endoscopic datasets. Specifically, it extracts global color statistics from a chosen reference dataset in the CIE-Lab color space and applies a statistical channel-wise transformation to map each target image toward the appearance of the images of the reference dataset. The method is evaluated in the context of polyp detection in both flexible colonoscopy and capsule endoscopy datasets using a dataset-level cross validation protocol. The results indicate that the proposed harmonization consistently improves cross-dataset performance up to 30.7%, outperforming relevant baseline and state-of-the-art methods. The results indicate that a substantial part of the generalization gap is driven by low-level appearance variation that can be mitigated without retraining.

</details>


### [221] [VideoTapestry: Query-Adaptive Memory Refinement for Multi-Agent Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06672)
*Yucheng Liu,Yufei Yin,Mingxiao Feng,Jiajun Deng,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoTapestry是一个免训练的多智能体框架，通过由粗到细、查询驱动的分层视频记忆精炼，提升长视频理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对记忆需求巨大，答案常需检索跨长时间跨度的信息。现有方法分两类：查询驱动探索对定位错误敏感，查询无关记忆构建可能遗漏问题特定细节。

Method: 提出免训练多智能体框架VideoTapestry，适配预构建的分层视频记忆。记忆分三层：全局叙事上下文、事件级时间结构、细粒度关系证据。每层分配专门智能体，在尺度特定上下文中完成检索与精炼；在查询引导下重访相关视频区域并丰富各层记忆，最终按原层级组装为查询自适应复合记忆。

Result: 相比直接GPT-5.5推理，在LVBench、LongVideoBench(Long)、Video-MME(Long)、EgoSchema上分别取得17.2%、14.9%、9.8%、7.0%的绝对准确率提升，达到当前最优结果。

Conclusion: 通过分层记忆与多智能体协作的查询自适应精炼，VideoTapestry在保持全局上下文的同时保留细粒度证据，显著提升长视频问答性能。

Abstract: Long-video understanding places substantial demands on memory, as answering questions often requires retrieving information distributed across extended temporal spans. Existing approaches broadly follow two paradigms: query-driven exploration, which is sensitive to localization errors, and query-independent memory construction, which may omit question-specific details. We introduce VideoTapestry, a training-free multi-agent framework that adapts a preconstructed hierarchical video memory through coarse-to-fine, query-driven refinement. The preconstructed memory organizes video content into three levels, capturing global narrative context, event-level temporal structure, and fine-grained relational evidence, respectively. To support coarse-to-fine localization and observation, we assign a specialized agent to each level, keeping retrieval and refinement within a scale-specific context. Guided by the query, these agents revisit relevant video regions and enrich layer-wise memories with targeted multimodal observations. Their refinements are assembled according to the original hierarchy into a composite query-adaptive memory, preserving global context in a compact form while retaining fine-grained evidence along query-relevant branches for final reasoning. Compared with direct GPT-5.5 inference, VideoTapestry achieves absolute accuracy gains of 17.2%, 14.9%, 9.8%, and 7.0% on LVBench, LongVideoBench (Long), Video-MME (Long), and EgoSchema, respectively, achieving the state-of-the-art results among all competitors.

</details>


### [222] [ChronoWorld: Camera-Controlled Consistent 4D World Generation via Spatiotemporal Cues and Geometric Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06687)
*Xiaoyu Zhou,Dingwei Xian,Zhenyu Wang,Yajiao Xiong,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChronoWorld框架，通过"观测-状态-反射"机制实现全局一致的自由视角4D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控的视频生成模型虽能生成视觉上吸引人的序列，但在保持内在4D时空连贯性方面仍具挑战。

Method: 提出ChronoWorld的"观测-状态-反射"框架，利用时空因果线索与重建先验。核心包括：(1) 时空对极因果注意力机制，在生成过程中施加多视角对极约束和时间因果性；(2) 重建驱动的几何反射流水线，配合4D检索策略，实现对生成输出的动态自评估与校正。

Result: 大量实验表明，ChronoWorld在时空一致、电影级质量的4D场景生成上达到状态最优性能，在多样场景中具有强泛化能力与高保真几何。

Conclusion: ChronoWorld通过融合时空因果线索与重建先验，有效提升4D场景生成的时空一致性与几何精度，实现自由视角的高质量4D场景生成。

Abstract: While existing camera-controllable video generation models can produce visually compelling sequences, preserving intrinsic 4D spatiotemporal coherence remains challenging. To address this limitation, we propose ChronoWorld, an "Observation--State--Reflection" framework that leverages spatiotemporal causal cues and reconstruction priors to generate globally consistent, free-view 4D scenes. Given a context video, we introduce a Spatiotemporal Epipolar Causal Attention mechanism that enforces multi-view epipolar constraints and temporal causality throughout the generation process. In addition, we develop a reconstruction-driven geometric reflection pipeline with a 4D retrieval strategy to enable dynamic self-assessment and correction of generated outputs, improving consistency and accuracy. Extensive experiments show that ChronoWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance in spatiotemporally consistent, cinematic-quality 4D scene generation, with strong generalization and high-fidelity geometry across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [223] [GS-Pool: Object-Level Change Detection in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06688)
*Boaz Keren-Gil,James Gain,Patrick Marais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Pool通过将SAM2掩码提升到3D高斯并引入摄影载体等证据，比较同一空间两次独立3DGS重建中的对象，实现变化检测并返回变化对象及其掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂、博物馆和测绘人员在不同时间拍摄同一空间，需要知道哪些对象发生了变化。但3DGS训练具有随机性，即使空间未变，两次重建也不会重合，且第二次扫描照片往往远少于第一次，因此直接比较两次重建无法回答该问题。

Method: GS-Pool输入同一空间两个独立重建的高斯场，将每次访问照片的SAM2掩码提升到渲染它们的高斯上并合并为对象池，从而每个决策在3D中按对象进行一次。方法引入“摄影载体”，即每个输入重建针对另一次访问照片的3DGS训练损失，并反向传播到渲染各像素的高斯；结合GS-Diff的几何与颜色项以及蒸馏的DINOv3特征。这些证据与两次访问共现对象的证据比较，为每个场景设定变化阈值。

Result: 在PASLCD上，GS-Pool达到mIoU/F1为0.751/0.846，优于最强先前方法GS-Diff的0.644/0.758，提升17%/12%；其mIoU还分别比O-SCD、PlenoCI和MV-3DCD高36%、40%和57%。在CL-Splats上达到0.855 mIoU，比MV-3DCD高33%。

Conclusion: GS-Pool能有效检测两次3DGS重建间发生变化的对象，并将每个变化对象以一组高斯及决策证据返回，便于检查者在3D中审阅。

Abstract: Factories, museums and surveyors photograph the same space months apart and need to know which objects changed. When each visit is reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), a direct comparison of the two reconstructions does not answer this. Training is stochastic, so two reconstructions of an unchanged space never coincide, and the second visit is often a quick re-scan with far fewer photographs. We propose GS-Pool, which takes two independently reconstructed Gaussian fields of the same space and returns the changed objects in each, together with their masks. SAM2 masks of each visit's photographs are lifted onto the Gaussians that render them and merged into an object pool, so every decision is taken once per object in 3D. We introduce a photographic carrier, the 3DGS training loss of each input reconstruction against the other visit's photographs, backpropagated to the Gaussians that rendered each pixel. We combine it with GS-Diff's geometry and colour terms and our distilled DINOv3 features. This evidence is compared with that of the objects present in both visits, which sets a change threshold for each scene. On PASLCD, GS-Pool reaches mIoU/F1 scores of 0.751/0.846 against 0.644/0.758 for GS-Diff, the strongest prior method, a gain of 17%/12%. Its mIoU is also 36%, 40% and 57% above that of O-SCD, PlenoCI and MV-3DCD, and it reaches 0.855 mIoU on CL-Splats, 33% above MV-3DCD. Each changed object is returned as a set of Gaussians with the evidence behind its decision, which an inspector can review in 3D.

</details>


### [224] [Extending Dynamic World Surface Water Mapping to Sentinel-1 with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06704)
*Rohit Mukherjee,Frederick Policelli,Beth Tellman,TC Chakraborty,Jonathan Giezendanner,Jonathan A. Sullivan,Ning Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用Dynamic World (DW) 水体类作为弱监督标签，结合Sentinel-1 SAR后向散射与Google AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) 年度嵌入，实现任意S1观测时刻的类DW水体制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DW虽能在全球10 m尺度制图土地利用/覆盖，但仅限无云观测，限制了水体在时间与空间上的覆盖；作者希望借助SAR不受云影响的特性，突破这一限制。

Method: 以DW水体类作为弱监督标签训练Sentinel-1 SAR模型；AEF年度嵌入提供空间上下文，S1后向散射提供获取时刻观测；在53个全球分布场景（配3 m PlanetScope独立标注，S1过境48小时内）及独立S1S2-Water基准上评估。

Result: S1-only模型汇总水体IoU为0.77，与业务化OPERA DSWx-S1的0.75相当；加入AEF后提升至0.85；融合模型在53个场景中44个优于S1-only、48个超过OPERA；在S1S2-Water基准上达0.94，优于OPERA的0.87。

Conclusion: 光学土地覆盖产品可为SAR地表水体制图提供可扩展的训练标签，融合光学标签与SAR观测及嵌入能显著提升水体提取精度。

Abstract: Dynamic World (DW) maps land use and land cover globally at 10 m from Sentinel-2 (S2) imagery, but only for cloud-free observations, which limits where and when surface water can be mapped. We use the DW water class as weak supervision for a Sentinel-1 (S1) synthetic aperture radar (SAR) model so that DW-like water maps can be produced for every S1 acquisition. Google's AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) annual embedding supplies spatial context, while S1 backscatter supplies the acquisition-time observation. On 53 globally distributed scenes with independent annotations of 3 m PlanetScope imagery acquired within 48 h of the S1 overpass, the S1-only model already reaches a pooled water intersection over union (IoU) of 0.77, comparable to 0.75 for the operational OPERA DSWx-S1 product, and adding AEF raises it to 0.85. The fused model improves on the S1-only model on 44 of 53 scenes and exceeds OPERA on 48, and on the independent S1S2-Water benchmark it reaches 0.94, compared with 0.87 for OPERA. Optical land-cover products can thus provide scalable training labels for SAR surface water mapping.

</details>


### [225] [MC-Sparse: Deconstructing and Closing the Dense-Sparse Attention Gap in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06801)
*Jiarui Chen,Zeqiang Lai,Jiangshan Wang,Ziheng Ouyang,Ye Huang,Xiangyu Yue,Cewu Lu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MC-Sparse是一种免训练的稀疏注意力框架，通过缓存查询分组、KV索引与残差元数据并跨去噪步复用，在视频与3D生成中实现高保真、高加速的扩散Transformer推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在长序列生成（视频、高分辨率3D资产）中延迟高，稀疏注意力是主要加速手段，但现有方法在高稀疏度下会损害生成质量与保真度。作者希望通过受控的oracle对比分析，定位这种退化的根本来源，从而设计更有针对性的稀疏方案。

Method: 通过oracle对比分析，将退化归因于三个来源：token分组带来的约束、交互选择不准确、以及被丢弃token所丢失的注意力贡献。据此提出MC-Sparse：一种免训练框架，在选取单个KV token的同时将相似查询组织为tile对齐分组以便GPU高效执行；并缓存元数据（查询分组、基于精确注意力概率选出的KV索引、稠密与稀疏注意力输出间的残差），在后续去噪步中复用。

Result: 在视频与3D生成模型上，MC-Sparse比现有稀疏注意力基线更接近稠密注意力输出，且取得更大的去噪加速，无明显质量退化。相对稠密注意力，在Minimax-H3-Base上实现1.80×去噪加速，在3D资产生成上实现2.32×加速，质量损失均可忽略。

Conclusion: 基于对稀疏注意力退化来源的分析所设计的MC-Sparse，通过细粒度KV选择、tile对齐查询分组与跨步元数据缓存复用，在不训练、不损质量的前提下显著加速长序列扩散Transformer生成。

Abstract: Sparse attention is a primary approach to reducing the latency of diffusion transformers in long-sequence generation tasks, such as video and high-resolution 3D asset generation. However, existing methods can degrade generation quality and fidelity at high sparsity levels. Through controlled oracle comparisons, we trace this degradation to three sources: constraints imposed by token grouping, inaccurate interaction selection, and the attention contributions lost when tokens are discarded. Guided by this analysis, we propose Meta-Cached Sparse Attention (MC-Sparse), a training-free framework that selects individual key-value (KV) tokens while organizing similar queries into tile-aligned groups for efficient GPU execution. MC-Sparse caches metadata comprising query groups, KV indices selected using exact attention probabilities, and residuals between dense and sparse attention outputs, and reuses them across subsequent denoising steps. Across video and 3D generation models, MC-Sparse achieves higher fidelity to dense-attention outputs and larger denoising speedups than existing sparse-attention baselines, without visible quality degradation. Relative to dense attention, it delivers a $1.80\times$ denoising speedup on Minimax-H3-Base and a $2.32\times$ speedup on 3D asset generation, both with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [226] [Less Context, Better Geometry: Masked Geometric Encoder for Robust 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06813)
*Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Zhaoliang Zhang,Yutao Tang,Abhay Yadav,Rama Chellappa,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出掩码几何编码器（MGE），通过训练时从全局注意力中策略性丢弃帧token并蒸馏自完整上下文的教师模型，学习鲁棒的几何表示，并结合锚点引导的自适应token合并技术实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型依赖全对全全局注意力，导致二次复杂度和长序列推理受限；无约束的跨视图交互会传播来自遮挡视图或视觉相似但几何上遥远视图的不可靠信息。

Method: 提出Masked Geometric Encoder（MGE）：训练时策略性丢弃帧token以进行全局注意力，并从未经掩码的完整上下文教师模型蒸馏，获得更丰富的逐帧表示。推理阶段开发Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging，保留代表性锚点帧并合并其余视图的冗余token。

Result: 在遮挡和"分身"（doppelganger）视图下性能显著增强，同时在标准基准上保持高性能；相比其他高效推理方法，在保持更高重建质量的同时实现推理加速，尤其在有限视图设置下表现更优。

Conclusion: MGE通过掩码训练与蒸馏学习到更丰富的帧表示，既提升鲁棒性又支持有效token削减，配合锚点引导的自适应token合并实现了效率与质量的兼顾。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D foundation models has enabled rapid 3D reconstruction and camera calibration by leveraging learned 3D priors from vast amount of spatial data. However, the all-to-all global attention design leads to quadratic complexity and limits long-sequence inference; unconstrained cross-view interactions also can propagate unreliable evidence from occluded or visually similar but geometrically distant views. In this paper, We introduce a Masked Geometric Encoder (MGE), which promotes the learning of robust geometric representations under incomplete cross-view context. During training, MGE strategically drops frame tokens from global attention and distills from a pretrained full-context teacher model. This allows the model to learn an intrinsically richer per-frame representation while providing sufficient intermediate supervision to avoid performance degradation. Through extensive experiments, we show that MGE leads to much stronger performance under occlusion and doppelganger views while retaining high performance on standard benchmarks. Such a richer frame representation also leads to more effective token reduction during inference. To this end, we develop a novel Anchor-Guided Adaptive token merging technique that preserves representative anchor frames while jointly merging redundant tokens from the remaining views. Compared to other efficient inference approaches, we can achieve inference speedup while consistently maintaining higher reconstruction quality, particularly in limited-view settings.

</details>


### [227] [UniSlider: Perceptually Uniform Sliders for Continuous Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06831)
*David Serrano-Lozano,Duygu Ceylan,Yannick Hold-Geoffroy,Iliyan Georgiev,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Anna Frühstück*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSlider提出将滑条与生成强度参数解耦，通过训练LoRA使感知距离随滑条值线性增长，并用自适应采样的推理时重映射实现平滑、单调的连续图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式图像编辑中的滑条只是对强度参数（如适配器系数、提示词权重、插值因子）的重新缩放，这种强度与感知变化关系不佳：图像可能在滑动时部分回退，长区间无可见变化，短区间突变。重映射强度可解决步速不均，但前提是轨迹单调，而现有方法并未保证单调性。

Method: 作者区分滑条与强度，要求图像与输入的感知距离随滑条值线性增长。提出UniSlider：在少步编辑骨干上训练轻量LoRA，使其强度逼近理想滑条。少步采样使该目标可在像素空间直接施加，无需中间真值，且全强度下保留骨干输出。由于低秩适配器无法使强度完全均匀，滑条被设计为推理时通过自适应采样对强度进行重映射；因训练已优化轨迹单调性，该重映射无需额外训练或参数即可弥合剩余差距。

Result: 在包含300个连续编辑的新基准上，从均匀性、单调性、编辑保真度和身份保持四方面评估，UniSlider优于所有先前方法，并在用户研究中更受偏好。

Conclusion: UniSlider通过解耦滑条与强度并结合少步采样训练与自适应采样重映射，实现了感知线性、单调平滑的连续图像编辑，性能与用户偏好均超越既有方法。

Abstract: Sliders provide an intuitive interface for continuous image editing. In current generative approaches, however, the slider is simply a rescaling of the method's strength parameter, such as an adapter coefficient, a prompt weight, or an interpolation factor. This strength relates poorly to perceptual change. The image can partially revert as the slider moves, long stretches of the range produce no visible difference, and short intervals transform the image abruptly. Remapping the strength could fix this uneven pace, but only if the trajectory is monotone, which current methods do not enforce. We therefore distinguish the slider from the strength, and require perceptual distance from the input to grow linearly with the slider value. We introduce UniSlider, a lightweight LoRA trained on a few-step editing backbone so that its strength approximates this ideal slider. Few-step sampling lets us impose this objective in pixel space without intermediate ground truth, and the backbone's output is preserved at full strength. However, a low-rank adapter cannot make the strength fully uniform. Our slider is thus an inference-time remapping of the strength, obtained by adaptive sampling. Since training optmizes to make the trajectory monotone, this remapping closes the remaining gap without extra training or parameters. On a new benchmark of 300 continuous edits evaluating uniformity, monotonicity, edit fidelity, and identity preservation, UniSlider outperforms all prior methods and is preferred in a user study.

</details>


### [228] [Anatomy-aware Fine-grained Multimodal Fusion for Laryngopharyngeal Cancer T-Staging Prediction Using CT and Radiology Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06837)
*Xingyue Zhao,Yanzhou Su,Fang Zhang,Zhanghexuan Ji,Yirui Wang,Dazhou Guo,Sibo Ju,Yuehua Cheng,Yuzhen Chen,Ming Feng,Le Lu,Tsung-Ying Ho,Jian Wang,Dakai Jin,Na Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖感知多模态框架，整合器官级CT上下文与放射报告用于喉咽癌T分期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的T分期对喉咽癌个性化治疗至关重要，但现有临床依赖侵入性活检，CT分期因肿瘤浸润模式复杂而困难。现有计算机辅助方法存在两大挑战：1）结构关系建模不足，要么处理整个CT体素缺乏肿瘤特异解剖约束，要么依赖费力的肿瘤分割；2）细粒度跨模态对齐不足，全局特征融合难以将单个解剖结构与对应文本描述准确对齐。

Method: 框架首先构建解剖结构化器官图（AOG）以捕获原发部位与周围器官间的浸润模式；然后进行器官锚定跨模态对齐（OCA），使每个器官节点聚合放射报告中对应文本证据；最后通过报告增强图精炼（REG）注入从报告中提取的器官特异性浸润线索，得到融合空间与文本证据的多模态器官图。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在喉咽癌T分期中取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 所提解剖感知多模态框架通过器官图建模与跨模态对齐，有效提升喉咽癌T分期效果。

Abstract: Accurate T-staging is crucial for guiding personalized treatment strategies for laryngopharyngeal cancer. However, current clinical practice relies on invasive biopsy procedures, whereas CT-based staging remains challenging due to the complex patterns of tumor invasion. Recent computer-aided approaches face two key challenges: 1) Structural relationship modeling: existing methods underrepresent anatomically structured patterns of tumor invasion, as they either process whole CT volumes without tumor-specific anatomical constraints or rely on labor-intensive tumor segmentation. 2) Fine-grained cross-modal alignment: while radiology reports contain organ-specific invasion details, current methods that apply global feature fusion struggle to accurately align individual anatomical structures with their corresponding textual descriptions. To address these issues, we propose an anatomy-aware multimodal framework that integrates organ-level CT context and radiology reports into a unified representation for laryngopharyngeal T-staging. The framework first constructs an Anatomy-Structured Organ Graph (AOG) that captures invasion patterns between primary sites and surrounding organs, then performs Organ-Anchored Cross-Modal Alignment (OCA) so that each organ node aggregates textual evidence from the radiology report, and finally refines this graph representation by injecting organ-specific invasion cues extracted from the report via Report-Enhanced Graph-Refinement (REG), yielding a multimodal organ graph that combines spatial and textual evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves superior performance in T-staging of laryngopharyngeal cancer.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Read the Contextual Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06844)
*Omer Dahary,Etai Sella,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过自然语言"询问"MM-DiT中间上下文token的框架，揭示其编码全局场景语义，并据此提出Contextual Alignment提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-DiT在生成过程中反复用多模态注意力更新文本token，形成动态的"上下文token"，但其功能尚不清晰，需要一种可解释的方法来理解这一内部表示空间。

Method: 训练一个轻量瓶颈网络，将中间上下文token映射到冻结LLM的输入空间，使LLM能直接从这些隐藏表示中回答关于正在生成图像的问题；并基于此提出Contextual Alignment训练技术，显式强化上下文token中的视觉语义信息。

Result: 上下文token编码了丰富的全局场景表示：生成相关语义（包括提示未指定的属性）在去噪早期即可被读取，细节随时间逐渐可读；即使输入空提示，信息仍可解码，说明其从演化中的视觉表示本身积累了图像特定信息；可读性更高的生成往往获得更高人类偏好评分。

Conclusion: 上下文token既是理解MM-DiT内部动态的可解释视角，也是改进生成模型的有效目标，Contextual Alignment可提升生成质量与分布覆盖。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) jointly process visual and textual representations throughout generation. These models repeatedly update the text tokens through multimodal attention, forming dynamic contextual tokens whose function is not well understood. In this work, we introduce a framework for reading this contextual space through natural-language interrogation. We train a lightweight bottleneck network that maps intermediate contextual tokens into the input space of a frozen Large Language Model (LLM), allowing the LLM to answer questions about the emerging image directly from these hidden representations. Our reader reveals that contextual tokens encode a rich, global representation of the emerging scene: generation-specific semantics, including attributes left underspecified by the prompt, are accessible surprisingly early in denoising, while increasingly fine-grained details become readable over time. Remarkably, this information remains decodable even when the MM-DiT receives an empty prompt, showing that contextual tokens accumulate substantial image-specific information from the evolving visual representation itself. We further find that generations with more readable contextual representations tend to receive higher human-preference scores. Building on these observations, we introduce Contextual Alignment, a training technique that explicitly reinforces the visual-semantic information encoded in the contextual tokens, improving generation quality and distributional coverage. Together, our results establish contextual tokens as both an interpretable view into the internal dynamics of MM-DiTs and an effective target for improving generative models.

</details>


### [230] [S2PD: Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion for Physically and Logically Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06847)
*Jeffrey Hu,Daniel Olmeda Reino,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 S2PD 方法，在高噪声阶段用自回归扩散、低噪声阶段用并行扩散，兼顾事件协调与采样效率，在游戏、物理模拟和真实视频上更可靠地遵循规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向视频扩散模型并行去噪整个视频，但即使在海量分布内数据上训练，仍会违反物理规律和简单符号规则，因为缺乏协调相互依赖事件所需的串行计算。

Method: S2PD（Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion）：在高噪声阶段进行自回归扩散以提供串行计算、协调相互依赖事件并产生有效状态转移；在低噪声阶段切换为并行扩散以联合精修整段视频并降低采样时间。以两种架构实现：从头训练的像素空间扩散 Transformer，以及通过 LoRA 微调并结合因果注意力的预训练视频模型。

Result: 在游戏、物理模拟和真实视频上，S2PD 比匹配的双向基线更可靠地遵循规则，并且相比其他串行方法生成具有更好时间稳定性和采样效率的视频。

Conclusion: 通过在高噪声与低噪声阶段分别采用自回归与并行的混合扩散策略，可在保持规则遵守与时间稳定性的同时提升采样效率，为视频生成提供一种兼顾串行协调与并行精修的方案。

Abstract: Bidirectional video diffusion models denoise entire videos in parallel, yet when trained on effectively unlimited in-distribution data from procedural generators, continue to violate physical laws and simple symbolic rules. We introduce Serial-to-Parallel Diffusion (S2PD), which performs autoregressive diffusion at high noise before switching to parallel diffusion at low noise. The autoregressive phase provides the serial computation needed to coordinate interdependent events and produce valid state transitions while the parallel phase jointly refines the entire video and reduces sampling time relative to fully serial generation. We implement S2PD with two architectures: a pixel-space diffusion transformer trained from scratch and a pretrained video model adapted through LoRA fine-tuning with causal attention. Across games, physical simulations, and real video, S2PD follows rules more reliably than matched bidirectional baselines and generates videos with greater temporal stability and sampling efficiency than other serial methods.

</details>


### [231] [One Figure, Every Canvas: Editable Flowchart Relayout via Agentic Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06852)
*Shih-Chen Tseng,Chih-Hsuan Chen,Ryan Yang,Hsi-An Chen,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"宽高比自适应流程图重排版"新任务：给定栅格流程图与目标比例，生成结构忠实、无幻觉、可编辑的布局。方法采用Parse、Style、Layout三阶段智能体流水线，每阶段配备critic结合确定性约束检查与VLM视觉反馈，输出draw.io可编辑的mxGraph XML。在100个流程图×5种比例基准上，内容保真度达68.6%，远超前人工作（11.2–41.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML论文中的流程图需在多种画布上复用（论文栏、16:9幻灯片、竖版海报、1:1社交图、9:16手机预览），每种格式对同一计算图施加不同宽高比，而任何被静默破坏的连接都会错误表达方法。现有方法各有缺陷：图像到图像模型拉伸方块且无法处理极端比例，文本到图像智能体系统产生幻觉，解析-渲染系统错连边。因此需要一个能适配宽高比且结构忠实的重排版方案。

Method: 提出智能体流水线，分解为Parse、Style、Layout三个阶段。每个阶段将一个主智能体与一个critic配对，critic结合确定性约束检查与VLM视觉反馈，从而显式检查连通性、防止连接被静默破坏。最终输出为draw.io可编辑的mxGraph XML。

Result: 在包含100个流程图、5种宽高比的精选基准上，由Gemini 3.1 Pro评测并经人工判断验证，本方法内容保真度达68.6%，而先前工作仅为11.2–41.4%。

Conclusion: 宽高比自适应流程图重排版可被形式化为独立任务，三阶段智能体流水线配合critic约束与视觉反馈能够显著提升结构忠实度并保持可编辑性，优于现有图像到图像、文本到图像及解析-渲染等方法。

Abstract: Pipeline figures in ML papers must be repurposed across many canvases, including paper columns, 16:9 slides, portrait posters, 1:1 social teasers, 9:16 phone previews. Each format imposes a different aspect ratio on the same computational graph, where any silently broken connection misrepresents the method. We formulate aspect-ratio-adaptive flowchart relayout as a distinct task: given a raster flowchart and a target ratio, produce a structurally faithful, hallucination-free, editable layout. Existing methods fail characteristically: image-to-image models stretch blocks and reject extreme ratios, text-to-image agentic systems hallucinate content, and parse-then-render systems mis-route edges. We propose an agentic pipeline factored into Parse, Style, and Layout stages, each pairing a main agent with a critic that combines deterministic constraint checks with VLM visual feedback so connectivity is explicitly checked and prevented from being silently broken. Outputs are draw.io-editable mxGraph XML. On a curated benchmark of 100 flowcharts at five aspect ratios, evaluated by Gemini 3.1 Pro and validated against human judgments, our method reaches 68.6% Content Fidelity versus 11.2-41.4% for prior work. Project page: https://onefigureeverycanvas.vercel.app/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [TACET: Context-Appropriate Acoustic-Social Navigation for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03828)
*Sungsan Park,Young-Sik Shin,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TACET，一种情境适配的声学-社交导航方法，让四足机器人依据社交情境同时决定"走向哪里"与"多安静地走"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院、养老院、安静办公室中的四足机器人，其脚-地冲击主导的运动噪声本身是一种社交变量。既有社交导航只尊重人类空间却把机器人视为声学中立，而静音运动方法又只按操作者设定降噪、忽视情境。

Method: TACET从机器人自我中心视角推断社交情境，用一个紧凑行为token <gait, speed, social_cost> 将慢速微调的视觉-语言推理器与快速反应式控制器耦合；该token同时条件化社交代价地图（去哪）与静音运动策略（多安静），并用结构化视野外记忆在人物离开画面后保留其上下文。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上，情境条件化运动在同等速度下最多降低9.3 dBA噪声；完整方法在各场景中将个人空间合规率保持100%，声学侵扰低（≤2.9 dBA），在评测场景中同时改善空间与声学表现。项目页：https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/tacet/。

Conclusion: 将运动噪声作为社交变量纳入导航决策，可统一优化空间合规与声学侵扰，实现情境适配的社交导航。

Abstract: Quadruped robots entering hospitals, care homes, and quiet offices must be context-appropriate not only in where they move but in how loudly they move: a legged robot's locomotion noise, dominated by foot-ground impacts, is itself a social variable. Prior social navigation respects human space but treats the robot as acoustically uniform, while quiet-locomotion methods reduce noise to an operator-specified, context-blind level. We present TACET, a context-appropriate acoustic-social navigation method that infers social context from the robot's egocentric view and decides both where it walks and how loudly, coupling a slow fine-tuned vision-language reasoner to a fast reactive controller through a single compact behavior token, <gait, speed, social_cost>. The same token conditions both a social costmap (where to go) and a quiet locomotion policy (how loudly to move), while a structured out-of-view memory keeps recently seen people in the reasoner's context after they leave the camera view. On a real quadruped, context-conditioned locomotion lowers locomotion noise by up to 9.3 dBA at matched speed, and across our scenarios the full method keeps personal-space compliance at 100% with low acoustic intrusion (<=2.9 dBA), jointly improving spatial and acoustic performance in the evaluated scenarios. The project page is available at https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/tacet/.

</details>


### [233] [GOTT: Object-centric Dexterous Manipulation with a Reusable Cross-Embodiment Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03861)
*Yulin Liu,Lai Wei,Yen-Jen Wang,Akash Sharma,Pieter Abbeel,Henrik I. Christensen,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOTT是一个以跨本体接触获取原语为核心的三阶段框架，将操纵意图转化为多指机器人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型和大规模人类数据提供了丰富的操纵意图来源，但将意图转化为多指机器人的具体行为仍很困难：灵巧手缺乏一种可复用、能跨任务和跨本体可靠建立接触的底层原语。

Method: 提出GOTT（reach-acquire-move，接近-获取-移动）框架，围绕单一跨本体接触获取原语构建：1）给定与机器人无关的物体轨迹和接近规格（reach specification），首先让手靠近任务相关的接触区域；2）共享的闭环原语在此基础上建立稳定接触；3）位姿条件控制器跟踪期望的物体运动。接近规格可来自未来感知规划、外部模型或人类演示，而原语与跟踪后端保持不变。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，GOTT能够在多样物体、不同的臂-手平台以及见过和未见过的手部形态上建立稳健接触；相比开环抓取执行，它一致地提升了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 接触获取可作为灵巧操作中可复用、跨本体的低层原语，使上层操纵意图能以与硬件无关的方式驱动多指机器人行为，并优于开环抓取策略。

Abstract: Foundation models and large-scale human data provide rich sources of manipulation intent, but translating this intent into multi-fingered robot behavior remains difficult. Dexterous hands still lack a reusable low-level primitive that reliably establishes contact across tasks and embodiments. We propose GOTT, a reach-acquire-move framework built around a single cross-embodiment contact-acquisition primitive. Given a robot-agnostic object trajectory and a reach specification, GOTT first brings the hand near a task-relevant contact region. The shared closed-loop primitive then establishes stable contact from this approximate initialization, and a pose-conditioned controller tracks the desired object motion. Reach specifications may come from future-aware planning, external models, or human demonstrations, while the primitive and tracking backend remain unchanged. Simulation and real-world experiments show that GOTT is able to establish robust contact across diverse objects, arm-hand platforms, and seen and unseen hand morphologies. It also consistently improves end-to-end task success over open-loop grasp execution.

</details>


### [234] [MOSAIC-SV: Real-Time Adaptive Identification of Vessel Dynamics for the Control and Deployment of Aquatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03898)
*Wensen Liu,Jerry Peng,Shravani Vedagiri,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SV：无需专门辨识试验、从工程先验出发的实时自适应水动力辨识与控制系统，在闭环每一控制步重估动力学模型，并在仿真与实船试验中优于冻结先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的水面机器人控制依赖水动力模型，而该模型辨识成本高，且强依赖于特定船体、载荷与测量工况。作者希望摆脱专门的辨识试验，仅凭规格书式工程先验即可获得"控制足够"的动力学模型，并在任务过程中持续自适应。

Method: 提出 MOSAIC-SV：以物理可行的无迹卡尔曼滤波在每个闭环控制步重估水动力、扰动与执行器参数；采用命令相关的 consider 投影，对当前命令无法区分"执行器效能"与"外力"的修正加以抑制；模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器基于当前估计进行规划。

Result: 仿真（CyberShip II）中，MOSAIC-SV 在静态失配与瞬态变化下恢复标定模型的航行性能；当惯性或阻尼先验偏差一个数量级时，航行时间仍控制在不缩放先验的 20% 以内。BlueBoat 双推进器双体船实船试验中，相比冻结工程先验，航行至少快 25%、运动预测误差至少低 56%，且在未建模载荷下依然有效。同一系统概念也成功部署于 6.3 吨双舷外机单体船。

Conclusion: MOSAIC-SV 证明了可在无专门辨识试验条件下，实现可部署的实时自适应水动力学辨识与闭环控制，并在仿真与多类实船上验证了其有效性与跨平台可行性。

Abstract: Model-based control of an aquatic robotic platform depends on a hydrodynamic model that is costly to identify and specific to the hull, payload, and conditions it was measured in. Here, we present MOSAIC-SV, a deployable real-time adaptive dynamics identification and control system that identifies a control-sufficient dynamics model from a spec-sheet engineering prior, without dedicated identification trials, and re-estimates it at every control step of a closed-loop mission while the controller plans on it. A physically admissible unscented Kalman filter re-estimates hydrodynamic, disturbance, and actuator parameters at every control step of the closed-loop mission; while a command-dependent consider projection withholds corrections the current command cannot attribute between actuator effectiveness and external force; and a model predictive path integral controller plans on the current estimate. In simulation on a CyberShip II plant, MOSAIC-SV recovers the transit performance of the calibrated model under static mismatch and transient changes, and stays within 20% of its own transit time at the unscaled prior when its inertia or damping prior is wrong by an order of magnitude. In on-water field trials on the Blue Robotics BlueBoat, a twin-thruster catamaran, MOSAIC-SV transits at least 25% faster and predicts its own motion with at least 56% less error than its frozen engineering prior, including under an unmodeled payload. The same MOSAIC-SV system concept was also feasibly deployed on a 6.3-tonne dual outboard monohull.

</details>


### [235] [Sparse Calibration-Based Personalization of Kernel-Based Gait Phase and Speed Estimation Using Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03931)
*Myeongju Cha,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏标定的个性化核方法，用于可穿戴IMU的步态相位与步速联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用群体平均核存在个体差异问题，需要个性化方法提升步态相位与步速估计精度。

Method: 基于20人数据集构建28速度参考库；用3个标定速度估计用户基线，结合基线中心化运动学偏差的主成分模型，生成个性化核。

Result: 离线验证中个性化核降低了相位误差；单被试在线试验中相位与速度误差数值上更低且嵌入式实时运行，但速度效应不显著，多重比较校正后相位差异也不显著。

Conclusion: 验证了可穿戴实现的可行性，但存在参考与在线数据集不匹配问题，需更大规模验证。

Abstract: This paper presents sparse calibration-based personalization for kernel-based gait phase and walking speed co-estimation from wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs). A 28-speed reference library was constructed from bilateral thigh and shank trajectories in a 20-subject locomotion dataset. Rather than directly applying population-average kernels, the method combines a user-specific baseline estimated from three calibration speeds with a principal-component (PC) model of baseline-centered kinematic deviations. Offline validation showed that personalized kernels reduced phase error relative to the population kernel. In a single-participant online pilot evaluation, the personalized kernels produced numerically lower mean phase and speed errors and ran in real time on embedded hardware. However, the speed effect was not significant, and pairwise phase differences did not remain significant after multiple-comparison correction. The pilot evaluation establishes wearable implementation feasibility while indicating reference-to-online dataset mismatch and the need for larger-cohort validation.

</details>


### [236] [REDIRECT: A 1% Fix for Bad Robot Habits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03997)
*Yu Zhang,Jiazhuo Li,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REDIRECT 通过仅使用 1% 的完整训练样本-反向传播预算，利用情节级"保留/有问题"标签定位不良行为分支，并为其分配连贯的保留延续动作，在不需帧级标注或额外交互的情况下修复机器人从少量缺陷示教中学到的坏习惯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在遥操作中可能从少数缺陷时刻习得坏习惯。在混合质量数据中，正常与有问题的示教共享大部分任务行为，仅在局部动作延续上存在差异。完整重训练成本高昂，而在有限更新预算下仅用干净数据微调恢复效果有限。因此作者提出：能否将更新预算集中用于修复局部差异，而非重新学习整个共享行为。

Method: REDIRECT 仅依赖情节级的"保留/有问题"标签，定位问题分支，将连贯的保留延续动作分配给该分支周围的原始观测，并对共享行为进行锚定。该方法不需要帧级标注，也不需要额外交互。作者在三个随机化的 ManiSkill 任务和三个随机种子上进行评估，并在真实 PiPER 机械臂上测试了倒杯和折毛巾任务。

Result: 在三个 ManiSkill 任务、三个随机种子上，REDIRECT 将平均成功率从 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复了 88.8% 的干净重训练差距，而同等计算量的微调仅恢复 22.1%。在真实 PiPER 机械臂上，REDIRECT 在倒杯和折毛巾任务中恢复了 86.7%–92.9% 的仅用干净数据的差距。

Conclusion: 局部机器人坏习惯可以通过将更新预算用于行为差异而非重新学习共享行为来修复，证明了 REDIRECT 在低预算下修复局部缺陷的高效性。

Abstract: Robots can acquire bad habits from a few defective moments in otherwise useful teleoperation. In mixed-quality robot data, normal and problematic demonstrations share most task behavior and differ only at a local action continuation. Full retraining is costly, while fine-tuning on clean data alone offers limited recovery under a small update budget. We ask whether the local difference can instead be fixed with only 1% of the full-training sample-backward budget. Using only episode-level retained/problematic labels, REDIRECT localizes the branch, assigns a coherent retained continuation to the original observations around it, and anchors shared behavior, without frame-level annotations or additional interaction. Across three randomized ManiSkill tasks and three seeds, REDIRECT raises the mean success rate from 68.2% to 90.3%, recovering 88.8% of the clean-retraining gap versus 22.1% for matched-compute fine-tuning. On a PiPER arm, it recovers 86.7-92.9% of the clean-only gap across cup insertion and towel folding. Local robot habits can therefore be repaired by spending the update budget on the behavioral difference rather than relearning shared behavior.

</details>


### [237] [SUAVE: Unified Video-Action Models via Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04009)
*Rhythm Syed,Jean Mercat,Sedrick Keh,Kushal Arora,Paarth Shah,Aykut Onol,Mengchao Zhang,Tony Dear*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUAVE 是一个单词汇表统一动作-视频模型，用掩码扩散 transformer 将语言、视频、动作全部离散化为共享序列中的 token，通过选择掩码位置即可切换为世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型擅长语义落地但只预测动作、不能在行动前想象未来；基于视频扩散的世界动作模型能想象但把语言当作冻结条件、且处于连续潜空间；现有统一模型要么逐 token 自回归解码，要么保持视频连续并附加动作头，难以兼顾生成效率与统一建模。

Method: 提出 SUAVE：单一词汇表的统一动作-视频模型，将语言、视频、动作三种模态都表示为离散 token 放入共享序列，由掩码扩散 transformer 在语言条件下同时生成视频与动作。推理时通过选择掩码哪些 token，同一网络即可充当世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型；无动作共训练时，把未标注视频的动作位置填为掩码 token 并从损失中排除。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验显示两点：其一，单个 SUAVE 模型可预测长时程视频并作为策略，在静态与动态操作任务上与专用世界模型和专门动作策略相当；在真实机器人上，模型生成子目标图像和覆盖 1 秒运动的动作块耗时 1,030 ms（RTX 5090 GPU），维持约每秒 2.5 次动作的闭环控制。其二，在机器人视频上预训练并在人类视频上共训练，显著提升策略性能以及对分布偏移的零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散是统一视频-动作建模的一种实用且通用的基础范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit strong semantic grounding from pretrained vision-language backbones but are typically optimized for predicting actions rather than future observations. They can see and act, but they do not imagine the future before acting. World action models (WAMs) built on video diffusion backbones can imagine but treat language as frozen conditioning on a continuous latent space. Unified models bring these modalities into one architecture, but they either decode autoregressively, one token at a time, or keep video continuous with an auxiliary action head. In this work, we present SUAVE, a Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl in which a masked diffusion transformer generates video and actions conditioned on language, with all three modalities represented as discrete tokens in a shared sequence. Choosing which tokens to mask at inference turns the same network into a world model, a robot policy, or a video-action model. For action-free co-training, the action positions of unlabeled video are filled with mask tokens and excluded from the loss. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate two findings. First, a single SUAVE model predicts long-horizon video and acts as a policy, competitive with dedicated world models and specialized action policies on static and dynamic manipulation tasks. On a real robot, our model generates subgoal images and an action chunk spanning one second of motion in 1,030 ms on an RTX 5090 GPU, sustaining closed-loop control at 2.5 actions per second. Second, pretraining on robot video and co-training on human video substantially improves policy performance and zero-shot robustness to distribution shift. Together, these results show that masked diffusion is a practical and versatile foundation for unified video-action modeling.

</details>


### [238] [Return-to-Home Feasible Micro-Aerial Vehicle Exploration for 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04013)
*Prajit Krisshnakumar,Fan Yang,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种拓扑优先的主动重建框架，解耦高保真渲染与导航，利用3DGS作为重建目标、TSDF/占据栅格构建Voronoi骨架路网，并用飞行时间预算感知目标选取视点，在ReplicaCAD、Gibson、HM3D上实现更优或相当的重建与RTH表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型飞行器（MAV）用于室内3D重建时，受严格飞行时间预算与安全约束限制，必须显式保证带保守余量的返航（RTH）可行性；同时需在有限时间内高效探索并避免不稳定规划。

Method: 拓扑优先的主动重建框架：将高保真渲染与导航解耦。以稠密3DGS地图作为重建目标，轻量TSDF/占据栅格作为碰撞检查与在线稀疏3D Voronoi骨架路网构建的支撑；针对TSDF几何直接提取路网在不完备在线融合下不稳定的问题，利用雕刻自由空间一致性与稠密可见性校验节点和边，抑制墙后幻影结构；在路网上以飞行时间预算感知目标并结合轻量滚动时域前瞻选取视点。

Result: 在ReplicaCAD、Gibson、HM3D真实感室内仿真基准上评估重建覆盖率/误差、RTH成功率与计算成本；在相同飞行时间预算下，性能相较近期基于GS的基线方法更优或具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该拓扑优先框架可在严格飞行时间预算下兼顾重建质量与安全返航，验证了自由空间一致性校验与滚动时域视点选择的有效性。

Abstract: Micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) enable rapid indoor 3D reconstruction for inspection and time-critical situational awareness, but must operate under strict flight-time budgets and safety constraints that require explicit return-to-home (RTH) feasibility with a conservative margin. We present a topology-first active reconstruction framework that decouples high-fidelity rendering from navigation. A dense 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map is optimized as the reconstruction target, while a lightweight Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF)/occupancy scaffold supports conservative collision checking and online construction of a sparse 3D Voronoi skeleton roadmap. Since directly extracting roadmaps from TSDF geometry can be unstable under noisy and incomplete online fusion, we validate nodes and edges using carved free-space consistency and dense visibility checks, which suppress behind-wall phantom structure and stabilize planning. Viewpoints are selected on the roadmap using a flight-time-budget-aware objective and a lightweight receding-horizon lookahead to improve non-myopic exploration behavior. We evaluate on photorealistic indoor simulation benchmarks (ReplicaCAD, Gibson, and HM3D), reporting reconstruction coverage/error, RTH success, and computational cost. Results demonstrate improved or competitive performance relative to recent GS-based baselines under identical flight-time budgets.

</details>


### [239] [A Biomimetic Gaze Control to Restore Head-Neck Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04022)
*Colin Rubow,Haohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于眼动追踪的颈部外骨骼控制器，利用前庭眼反射实现自然的眼-头协调，并加入可调参数的个性化工具，在人体验证中相比旧模型速度曲线更自然，准确运动意图检测平均提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力颈部外骨骼旨在为颈部肌肉无力者恢复头颈运动，但现有手持输入设备控制方式不直观甚至不可行；眼睛与头部在完成日常任务时天然协调，故眼动可作为无需手部操作的控制信号，而以往控制模型缺乏生物行为学依据和个性化手段，导致头颈运动不自然。

Method: 设计了一种新颖的眼动追踪控制器，利用前庭眼反射来模拟自然的眼-头协调；同时设计了个性化工具，包含滑块让用户调节控制器参数；并在人类用户中进行了技术设计与验证。

Result: 与之前的眼动追踪模型相比，新控制器通过更自然的速度曲线提供个性化的自然头部运动，准确运动意图检测平均提升24%。

Conclusion: 该系统为研究眼动控制器中的个性化与偏好提供了有力平台，并最终有望恢复患者群体丧失的头颈活动能力。

Abstract: Powered neck exoskeletons are developed to restore head-neck motions for those with neck muscle weakness. Current methods using hand-held input devices to control these robotic systems are unintuitive or non-viable for these populations. Alternatively, the eyes coordinate with the head to accomplish many daily tasks. Therefore, movements of the eyes can be a useful signal to control the neck exoskeletons without needing to use the hands. However, previous control models lack biobehavioral basis as well as means to personalize the control behavior, causing unnatural head-neck movements. This paper presents a novel eye-tracking controller design leveraging the vestibulo-ocular reflex to emulate natural eyehead coordination. Personalization tools were also designed, consisting of sliders to allow users to adjust the parameters of this controller. Here, we present the technical design and validation of this system in human users. Compared to the previous eye-tracking model, the new controller provides natural head motions personalized to the user through more natural velocity profiles and a 24 percent average increase of accurate motion intent detection. This system provides a powerful platform for studying eye-tracking controllers in the context of personalization and preference, and ultimately restoring the lost head-neck mobility in patient populations.

</details>


### [240] [Reward-DAgger: Robot-Gated Interactive Imitation Learning with General-Purpose Progress-Based Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04054)
*Ryan Li,Yigit Korkmaz,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Reward-DAgger，一种基于通用奖励模型密集进度信号的机器人门控交互式模仿学习框架，用于跨任务检测失败并教学恢复行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人控制策略在未见环境中性能下降，需要检测失败并教学恢复行为。现有运行时监测方法往往需要针对特定任务和策略进行训练或超参数调优，限制了跨任务部署，并在策略迭代更新时引入额外开销。

Method: Reward-DAgger利用通用奖励模型提供的密集进度信号，由机器人自主门控决定何时需要人类干预。该方法对底层策略架构不可知，无需访问策略内部信息，且门控机制无需在跨任务时重新调参。

Result: 在失败检测的准确率-延迟权衡上优于现有运行时监测基线；在八个仿真与真实任务中，交互学习过程中持续提升下游策略的自主成功率，并取得较高的人类投入回报，在多数设置下优于基线。同一门控配置跨任务通用，无需任务特定超参数调优，实现了跨任务、环境和策略架构的迁移。

Conclusion: Reward-DAgger通过通用奖励模型驱动的机器人门控机制，实现了无需任务特定调参的交互式模仿学习，能在多任务和多环境中有效检测失败并教学恢复行为，具备良好的迁移性与人类投入回报。代码与视频见 https://liralab.usc.edu/reward-dagger。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled generalist control policies capable of completing a wide range of tasks. However, their performance degrades when deployed in unseen environments, making it critical to detect failures and teach recovery behaviors. Existing runtime monitoring methods often require task- and policy-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, limiting cross-task deployment and introducing additional overhead during iterative policy updates. We present Reward-DAgger, a robot-gated interactive imitation learning framework that uses dense progress signals from a general-purpose reward model to determine when human intervention is needed. Our approach is agnostic to the underlying policy architecture, requires no access to policy internals, and can be applied across tasks without retuning the gating mechanism. Our results show that Reward-DAgger achieves a better failure-detection accuracy-latency tradeoff than existing runtime monitoring baselines. Across eight simulated and real-world tasks, Reward-DAgger consistently improves the downstream policy's autonomous success rate throughout interactive learning and achieves strong return on human effort, outperforming the baselines in most settings. Importantly, the same gating configuration is used across tasks without task-specific hyperparameter tuning, demonstrating transfer across tasks, environments, and policy architectures. Code and videos are available at https://liralab.usc.edu/reward-dagger.

</details>


### [241] [Terrain-Dependent Intra-Cycle Leg Timing for Effective Locomotion on Granular Slopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04144)
*Long Zhao,Xingjue Liao,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上的实验，揭示了斜坡运动性能下降主要由锚定延迟和后滑增加导致，而非过度下陷，并建立了预测模型与失效相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在沙丘等颗粒斜坡上运动面临根本性挑战，原因是颗粒介质剪切强度降低以及重力引起的各向异性屈服。目前对斜坡上机器人—地形相互作用的力学机理缺乏定量理解与预测能力。

Method: 使用六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上系统测量运动速度以及随坡度变化的法向与切向颗粒阻力。基于测量结果，构建一个简单的机器人—地形相互作用模型，用于预测锚定时机、步长和机器人速度随地形强度与坡角的变化；并将模型推广至一般地形条件，构建失效相图。

Result: 法向贯入阻力随倾角几乎不变，而切向（剪切）阻力随坡度增加显著下降。模型表明斜坡导致的性能损失主要源于锚定延迟和后滑增加，而非过度下陷。扩展模型给出失效相图，可区分下陷型和滑移型失效区域，实现定量风险评估。

Conclusion: 该物理信息框架为颗粒斜坡上运动的失效机制提供了预测性认识，并为在可变形斜坡上更安全、更稳健的机器人作业提供了指导。

Abstract: Locomotion on granular slopes such as sand dunes remains a fundamental challenge for legged robots due to reduced shear strength and gravity-induced anisotropic yielding of granular media. Using a hexapedal robot on a tiltable granular bed, we systematically measure locomotion speed together with slope-dependent normal and shear granular resistive forces. While normal penetration resistance remains nearly unchanged with inclination, shear resistance decreases substantially as slope angle increases. Guided by these measurements, we develop a simple robot-terrain interaction model that predicts anchoring timing, step length, and resulting robot speed, as functions of terrain strength and slope angle. The model reveals that slope-induced performance loss is primarily governed by delayed anchoring and increased backward slip rather than excessive sinkage. By extending the model to generalized terrain conditions, we construct failure phase diagrams that identify sinkage- and slippage-induced failure regimes, enabling quantitative risk estimation for locomotion on granular slopes. This physics-informed framework provides predictive insight into terrain-dependent failure mechanisms and offers guidance for safer and more robust robot operation on deformable inclines.

</details>


### [242] [Autoware in Construction: Gap Analysis and LiDAR Perception Toward Off-Road Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04187)
*Yu Otsuki,Teja Emmey,Sena Matsushita,Akiro Harada,Takeshi Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文总结将 Autoware 开源自动驾驶平台扩展至建筑工地大型自卸卡车应用的经验教训,梳理公路与越野场景在感知、建图、定位、规划与控制等模块上的差异,并给出解决方案与路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autoware 原主要面向公共道路(乘用车、出租车、公交),现逐渐被拓展到建筑、农业等越野环境。但建筑工地的非结构化地形、扬尘、持续变化的场地条件以及施工特有物体,从根本上挑战了传统自动驾驶系统的诸多假设,因此有必要系统识别并弥合这些差距。

Method: 以 Autoware 栈为基础,针对大型自卸卡车在建筑工地的作业场景开展持续研发,逐模块(传感、建图、定位、感知、规划、控制)对比公共道路与越野施工应用的差异;重点发展基于 LiDAR 的感知流水线,并结合实地测试进行验证。

Result: 识别出公共道路与越野施工应用在各模块间的具体差距,提出相应的潜在解决思路;给出了基于 LiDAR 的感知流水线开发现状,以及实地测试中所获得的发现。

Conclusion: 提出面向施工车辆端到端自动驾驶的发展路线图,为 Autoware 在越野施工场景的进一步扩展提供方向指引。

Abstract: Autoware is an open-source autonomous driving software platform widely adopted by researchers and industry developers. Originally developed primarily for public-road applications, including passenger vehicles, taxis, and buses, Autoware is increasingly being extended to off-road environments such as construction and agricultural sites. Construction sites, however, differ fundamentally from public roads and challenge many assumptions underlying conventional autonomous driving systems. They are characterized by unstructured terrain, airborne dust, continuously evolving site conditions, and construction-specific objects. This paper presents lessons learned from ongoing efforts to extend an Autoware-based autonomous driving system to large dump trucks operating at construction sites. We identify gaps between public-road and off-road construction applications across the sensing, mapping, localization, perception, planning, and control modules of the Autoware stack. We then discuss potential solutions for addressing these gaps, with particular emphasis on ongoing LiDAR-based perception pipeline development and findings from field testing. Finally, we propose a roadmap toward end-to-end autonomous driving for construction vehicles.

</details>


### [243] [Bi-manual Stabilization of the Cervical Spine for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction in Rolling Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04220)
*Elizabeth Peiros,Moira Bohley,Lucas Yager,Derek Chen,Rebecca Fellbaum,Chris D'Ambrosia,Ananya Rao,Xiao Liang,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双臂机器人稳定颈椎的算法，在翻滚操作中实现接近人类水平的颈部旋转对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈椎脆弱且易受灾难性损伤，在战场、灾害或运动事故中搬运患者时需格外小心保护颈椎，因此需要机器人辅助稳定颈椎。

Method: 提出用于双臂机器人在翻滚操作中稳定颈椎的算法与适配方案，研究在安全界限内最大化抓握力的技术，并提出实现正确颈部旋转的控制方法。

Result: 采用该方法，双臂机器人能够达到人类水平的旋转对齐保持性能；带预测的机器人轴向对齐与人类新手相差在0.21度以内。

Conclusion: 双臂机器人可在颈椎稳定任务中达到接近人类新手的表现，验证了所提算法的有效性。

Abstract: The neck, specifically the cervical spine, is a highly fragile and mobile part of the body with a high risk of catastrophic injury. Whether someone is injured on a battlefield, in a natural disaster, or in an athletic event, great care is always taken with the cervical spine when transporting, rolling, or moving the patient. In this work, we present an algorithm and adaptations for bi-manual robots to stabilize the cervical spine during rolling maneuvers.
