The repair stage grounds imagination in observed robot behavior through a kinematic alignment score that measures agreement between predicted and demonstrated motion, enabling the predicted video to faithfully reflect its input actions. Using the repaired video model, the rejection stage compares imagined outcomes of sampled and demonstrated actions, selectively applying negative fine-tuning to samples whose predicted task progress falls below the demonstrated reference by a prescribed margin. Together, the two stages extend video prediction from representation learning to consequence-based policy refinement without additional environment interaction or changes to the inference procedure. $R^2$-WAM achieves 93.8% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 across clean and randomized settings. On the long-horizon real-world Fold Shirt task, it achieves 87.5% average success, compared with 0% for Fast-WAM.

</details>


### [280] [PreAct-Nav: Agentic Reasoning Before Action for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04916)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Junwei Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PreAct-Nav 智能体导航框架，通过中程子目标锚定、动作后果预测与记忆更新，提升城市长距离导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市导航需智能体依据第一视角观测做局部决策以完成长程目标。现有方法依赖对瞬时观测或有限历史的语言推理，难以将遥远目标转化为连贯的局部决策，缺乏对动作后果与未来状况的预见能力，在长且复杂的城市路线中表现受限。

Method: 核心是以持久的中等时程子目标锚定局部决策，在执行前评估预测动作的后果，并用真实结果持续更新推理上下文。具体包括：导航记忆模块维护当前子目标与相关经验，将远期目标转化为可执行的中间目标；预测性世界沙箱利用动作条件世界模型（AC-WM）预测候选动作下的世界动态；视觉语言模型（VLM）推理器在子目标下解读预测，决定保留或修正动作；执行后用真实观测评估结果、纠正不一致假设并更新记忆。

Result: 大量实验表明，PreAct-Nav 通过记忆更新与视觉预测改进了动作选择，且在更长路线和转弯更多的路线上提升更为显著。

Conclusion: 为冻结策略赋予预见性推理能力，可有效缓解长程城市导航中局部决策与远期目标脱节的问题。

Abstract: Urban navigation requires embodied agents to pursue long-horizon goals through local decisions based on egocentric observations. However, existing agentic navigation methods often struggle to translate distant goals into coherent local decisions in large-scale physical environments. Their reliance on linguistic reasoning over transient observations or limited history constrains anticipation of the consequences of actions and future conditions, despite the importance of such foresight for navigating long and complex urban routes. To bridge this gap, we propose PreAct-Nav, an agentic navigation framework that equips frozen policies with anticipatory reasoning for robust urban navigation. Our central idea is to anchor local decisions in persistent medium-horizon subgoals, assess the consequences of predicted actions before execution, and continually update the reasoning context using actual outcomes. At its core, a navigation memory module maintains the active subgoal and relevant experience across decisions, translating distant goals into actionable intermediate objectives. We further introduce a predictive world sandbox that uses an action-conditioned world model (AC-WM) to forecast world dynamics conditioned on candidate movements. A vision-language model (VLM) reasoner interprets these predictions under the current subgoal to retain or revise actions. After execution, real observations are used to assess outcomes, correct inconsistent assumptions, and update memory to continue or reformulate the subgoal. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that the proposed PreAct-Nav improves action selection through memory updates and visual prediction, with more pronounced gains on longer routes and routes with more turns.

</details>


### [281] [Nudge Before You Push: Physics-Aware Navigation via Tactile Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04924)
*Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Ruitong Tian,Bowen Sun,Xiao Hu,Yang Ye,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TANav系统，通过轻推测量推动阻力，判断容器是否可安全推行，用于巡检导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外观相同的容器可能装载需不同处理决策的物品，需在站点设定的处理边界下判断能否推动。

Method: TANav用短促轻推测推动阻力；TacPhys将力序列（可含RGB-D与运动学）读为质量估计以授权推动；重复巡检规划器权衡探测与路径成本，必要时二次接触，并跨访问复用观测。

Result: 仿真中TacPhys接近仅用阻力的贝叶斯参考，漏推率从28.7%降至5.5%；重复巡检仿真中TANav恢复90%的oracle路径节省，人工干预减半以上，边界违规从4.3%降至2.9%。四足机械臂上离线零样本MAE为0.28-1.07 kg（容器达2.82 kg），3 kg力升校准离线准确率93.5%，原始峰值规则在线20次中15次正确。

Conclusion: 轻推测阻结合力-质量估计与巡检规划，可有效降低误推与违规，并在真实四足平台上验证可行性。

Abstract: Visually identical containers can conceal loads that require different handling decisions. We present TANav, which uses a brief nudge to measure push resistance for navigation under a site-defined handling boundary. TacPhys reads the force sequence, with optional RGB-D and kinematics, into a mass estimate for push authorization. A repeated-patrol planner weighs probe and route costs, requests a second contact when useful, and reuses observations across visits. In simulation, TacPhys approaches a resistance-only Bayes reference and reduces missed pushes from 28.7% to 5.5% relative to peak-force thresholding at comparable low-risk operating points. In repeated-patrol simulation, TANav recovers 90% of the oracle's path saving, more than halves human interventions relative to always-detour, and reduces boundary violations from 4.3% to 2.9% relative to RGB-D-only probing. On a quadruped manipulator with a Hall-array fingertip, offline zero-shot MAE is 0.28-1.07 kg on containers up to 2.82 kg. Force-rise calibration at 3 kg gives 93.5% pooled offline accuracy (86.4% on non-cube episodes); a separate raw-peak rule gives 15 of 20 correct online decisions on unseen boxes.

</details>


### [282] [RobotUse: Allocating Computation, Context, and Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04929)
*Junhoo Lee,Injun Baek,Seungyeon Kim,Suhyun Jeon,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RobotUse 是一个机器人智能体框架，将计算、上下文与决策围绕"指定与修订物理动作"来组织，在 RoboLab 上达到 45% 任务成功率，优于 CaP-X 6.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人智能体接口常把动作选择封装在预定义工具内部，或要求智能体自行管理详细的执行代码及其不断增长的历史记录，导致智能体难以将意图动作与观测结果对齐，也难以在反复尝试中保留修订决策所需的上下文。

Method: 提出 RobotUse 框架：智能体通过视觉方式选择目标与位姿，后端负责几何计算、运动规划与控制；子智能体在每个子目标内保留详细交互并返回后续决策所需信息；通过持续框架化（continual harnessing），智能体利用更新持久化的 playbook 从执行中学习。

Result: 在 RoboLab 上，RobotUse 取得 45% 任务成功率，比 CaP-X 高 6.7 个百分点，同时保持紧凑的决策上下文并减少对预定义动作抽象的依赖；此外，该方法能在反馈不完美的情况下从真实世界执行中学习，并将所学迁移到后续任务。

Conclusion: RobotUse 表明，以"指定与修订物理动作"为中心来组织计算、上下文和决策，可提升机器人智能体的任务成功率、决策效率与经验迁移能力。

Abstract: Robot agents must connect their intended actions to observed outcomes while retaining the context needed to revise their choices over repeated attempts. Existing interfaces often leave these choices inside predefined tools or require agents to manage detailed execution code and its growing history. We introduce RobotUse, a robot agent harness that organizes computation, context, and decisions around specifying and revising physical actions. Agents visually select targets and poses, while the backend handles geometry, motion planning, and control. Subagents retain detailed interactions within each subgoal and return the information needed for subsequent decisions. Continual harnessing lets agents learn from execution by updating a persistent playbook. On RoboLab, RobotUse achieves 45% task success, outperforming CaP-X by 6.7 percentage points while maintaining compact decision contexts and reducing reliance on predefined action abstractions. Furthermore, we show that RobotUse learns from real-world execution despite imperfect feedback and transfers what it learns to subsequent tasks. Project page is available at https://robotuse-team.github.io/.

</details>


### [283] [DiVeR: Decision-Critical Verifier Learning for VLA Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04933)
*Seongheon Park,Heecheol Kim,Shulin Tian,Lilika Makabe,Namiko Saito,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Sharon Li,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiVeR方法，通过采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，重加权验证器学习，提升VLA策略的测试时动作选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略受限于机器人数据获取成本高。验证器引导的测试时扩展提供高效替代方案，但现有基于分类的验证器从轨迹级结果学习，对所有访问状态一视同仁，忽略了不同状态对候选动作区分的价值差异。许多状态下候选动作相似、区分信号有限，只有少数决策关键状态才有显著影响下游结果的动作差异。

Method: DiVeR从采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，利用该信号将验证器学习重加权至动作选择最关键的决策状态，无需步级标注或额外环境交互。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa以及Franka Research 3真实机器人实验中，DiVeR通过更有效的验证器引导动作选择持续提升任务成功率，且验证器推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: DiVeR通过关注决策关键状态重加权验证器学习，可有效改进验证器引导的动作选择，在仿真与真实机器人任务上稳定提升成功率，并保持极低推理开销。

Abstract: Scaling robot data and model capacity has improved Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, but further progress is constrained by the high cost of robotic data. Verifier-guided test-time scaling offers an efficient alternative by sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the one most likely to lead to task success at inference time. Existing classification-based verifiers learn from trajectory-level outcomes but treat all visited states equally, even though their value for candidate discrimination can vary across a trajectory. At many states, plausible actions are similar and provide limited discrimination signal, while only a sparse set of decision-critical states admits meaningfully different actions that can substantially affect downstream outcomes. To address this, we propose DiVeR, which estimates decision criticality from the dispersion of sampled action representations. DiVeR then uses this signal to reweight verifier learning toward states where action selection is most consequential, without requiring step-level annotations or additional environment interaction. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world experiments on a Franka Research 3 robot, DiVeR consistently improves task success through more effective verifier-guided action selection, while adding negligible verifier inference overhead.

</details>


### [284] [A Unified Dynamics Framework for Reinforcement Learning and Classical Control of a Six-DOF Pipeline-Tracking ROV in NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04949)
*Cheng Siong Chin,M. Venkateshkumar,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种六自由度ROV管道跟踪架构，用同一USD场景为NumPy实现（Fossen方程）与Isaac Sim部署提供一致的物理参数，统一比较5种RL算法与6种经典控制基线，并评估科里奥利项的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习控制器通常在某一种物理表示上训练、却在另一种上部署，导致报告性能无法反映训练外的真实行为，因此需要训练与部署物理一致的评测框架。

Method: 用单一USD场景向两条分支（向量化NumPy版Fossen海洋航行器方程，以及NVIDIA Isaac Sim中每步作为PhysX力施加的相同方程）提供BlueROV2-Heavy的质量、附加质量、阻尼、浮力与推进器参数；以科里奥利-向心项为主要动力学模型；通过检查点兼容层在统一环境、奖励与随机化评估下训练并打分PPO、SAC、TD3、DDPG、TRPO五种算法；并扩展加入PID、滑模、模糊逻辑、反馈线性化、模型预测控制、ANFIS六种经典基线，使用与学习策略相同的制导几何与推力分配。

Result: 在PPO与TRPO上的受控消融显示，加入科里奥利项不会破坏稳定性且略微提升跟踪性能（TRPO的站位RMS误差约降低19%）。在启用科里奥利动力学下，PPO、TRPO与反馈线性化取得100%成功率并兼具竞争力精度；PID、模糊控制与神经模糊基线同样达到100%成功率但跟踪更松；DDPG与TD3各自表现出特定且可解释的失效模式，而非离策略学习的普遍弱点；经典控制仍是对抗最优学习策略的强基线。

Conclusion: 通过共享物理表示的训练-部署管线，可实现RL与经典控制的公平比较；科里奥利项有价值但不影响稳定性；经典控制依然是强基线，而部分离策略算法的失败具有可解释的具体原因。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning controllers for underwater vehicles are usually trained against one physics representation and deployed against another, so reported performance does not always describe behavior outside training. This paper presents a pipeline-tracking architecture for a six-degree-of-freedom remotely operated vehicle (ROV) in which one Universal Scene Description (USD) scene supplies the real BlueROV2-Heavy mass, added-mass, damping, buoyancy, and thruster parameters to both halves of the system: a vectorized NumPy implementation of Fossen's marine-craft equations, and an interactive NVIDIA Isaac Sim deployment applying the identical equations as PhysX forces at every step. The Coriolis-centripetal term is the primary dynamics model in both branches; a controlled ablation on PPO and TRPO shows that including it does not destabilize either algorithm and modestly improves tracking, about 19 percent lower standoff RMS error for TRPO. Five reinforcement learning algorithms, PPO, soft actor-critic, TD3, DDPG, and TRPO, are trained against one environment, reward, and randomized evaluation harness through a checkpoint-compatibility layer scoring any policy with the same code. The pipeline is extended with six classical baselines, PID, sliding-mode, fuzzy logic, feedback linearization, model predictive control, and an adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference system, driven by the same guidance geometry and thruster allocation as the learned policies. Under Coriolis-enabled dynamics, PPO, TRPO, and feedback linearization reach the strongest combination of 100 percent success and competitive accuracy; PID, fuzzy control, and the neuro-fuzzy baseline also reach 100 percent success with looser tracking; DDPG and TD3 each show a specific, explainable failure mode rather than a general weakness of off-policy learning; and classical control remains a strong baseline against the best learned policies.

</details>


### [285] [FreeLoc: Online Floorplan Localization via Diffusion-Aided Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05011)
*Haocheng Peng,Boyang Zhou,Jiarui Hu,Xiyue Guo,Ziyang Zhang,Boming Zhao,Yifan Gao,Xiao Li,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeLoc 提出一种在线 RGB 平面图定位框架，将平面图作为可直接查询的几何地图，避免构建密集的场景专用离线数据库，实现实时在线推理并在单帧与序列定位上达到先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能平面图定位方法需将平面图转换为密集的场景专用离线数据库，使精度、存储与运行时间都受离散位姿空间采样分辨率制约，缺乏灵活性与可部署性。

Method: FreeLoc 引入高效的在线几何查询与扩散辅助精化方案：通过实时平面图射线查询检索可行位姿锚点，并精化为连续位姿估计；针对序列定位，提出在线似然构建策略，从粗采样候选与精化位姿假设构建似然，桥接单帧定位与概率时序融合，支持直方图滤波融合而无需离线数据库。

Result: 实验表明方法支持实时在线推理，在单帧与序列定位任务上均取得当前最优性能，真实场景结果验证了其在室内机器人定位中的实用可部署性。

Conclusion: FreeLoc 通过将平面图视为直接可查询的几何地图并结合在线几何查询、扩散精化与在线似然构建，摆脱了离线数据库依赖，兼顾精度、效率与可部署性，为室内机器人定位提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Floorplans provide compact and widely available geometric maps for indoor localization, but existing high-performing floorplan-based methods still convert them into dense scene-specific offline databases, tying accuracy, storage, and runtime to the sampling resolution of the discretized pose space. We present FreeLoc, an online RGB-based floorplan localization framework that treats the floorplan as a directly queryable geometric map. FreeLoc introduces an efficient online geometric querying and diffusion-aided refinement scheme, which retrieves plausible pose anchors through on-the-fly floorplan ray querying and refines them into accurate continuous pose estimates. For sequential localization, FreeLoc develops an online likelihood construction strategy that bridges single-frame localization and probabilistic temporal fusion by constructing likelihoods from coarse-sampled candidates and refined pose hypotheses, enabling histogram-filter-based temporal fusion without offline databases. Experiments demonstrate real-time online inference and state-of-the-art performance in both single-frame and sequential localization, while real-world results validate practical deployability in indoor robotic localization scenarios.

</details>


### [286] [Now You Feel It, Now You See Me: Digital-Twin-based Teleoperation Interface for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05081)
*Youngchan Shim,Kyutae Lee,JooYun Kim,Jaeseong Hwang,Harim Ji,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数字孪生的增强现实（AR）遥操作界面，通过可视化力反馈与遮挡缓解视觉反馈，改善裸手跟踪遥操作在精细力控与遮挡场景下演示数据采集的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸手跟踪遥操作虽能无需手套或外骨骼地控制机器人灵巧手并演示协同手指运动，但存在两类关键反馈缺陷：一是缺乏力反馈，操作者只能凭视觉推断接触力，难以安全精确操作；二是被物体或机器人其他部位遮挡的视觉区域缺失，影响手部位置、接近距离及适合物体形状的预抓取姿态判断。

Method: 提出基于数字孪生的AR遥操作界面，集成裸手跟踪、双臂机器人手及机器人与任务物体的虚拟表示；在机器人手网格上渲染触觉测量值作为视觉-力反馈；并提供用户可控的虚拟视角面板或遮挡感知透明渲染，以提供缓解遮挡的视觉反馈。通过包含Task 1与Task 2的用户研究进行评测。

Result: 通过涉及两项任务（Task 1与Task 2）的用户研究，考察了力可视化与视觉辅助如何在需要精细力调节和遮挡下操作的任务中支持演示数据采集。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生AR界面结合视觉-力反馈与遮挡缓解视觉反馈，可有效应对裸手遥操作中的力反馈缺失与遮挡问题，支持高质量灵巧操作演示的采集。

Abstract: Teleoperation is becoming increasingly important for collecting high-quality demonstrations to teach robots dexterous manipulation skills. For dexterous manipulation, bare-hand tracking provides a practical way to control robotic hands and demonstrate coordinated finger movements without gloves or exoskeletons. However, this type of teleoperation faces two key feedback limitations: a lack of force feedback and visual feedback of occluded region. The absence of force feedback hinders precise and safe manipulation, as operators must infer contact force visually rather than feel them directly. Occlusion by objects or other robot parts impairs the assessment of hand positions, approach distances, and pre-grasp configurations suited to the object's shape. To address these limitations, we propose a digital-twin-based augmented reality (AR) teleoperation interface that integrates bare-hand tracking, bimanual robotic hands, and virtual representations of the robot and task objects. The interface renders tactile measurements on robot hand meshes as visuo-force feedback. Furthermore, it offers a user-controlled virtual view panel or occlusion-aware transparency rendering to provide occlusion-mitigating visual feedback. We evaluated the interface through user studies involving Task 1 and Task 2, examining how force visualization and visual assistance support demonstration collection in tasks requiring careful force regulation and manipulation under occlusion.

</details>


### [287] [PB-STDG: A Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy Guidance Algorithm for a Drone Road System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05122)
*Zhouyu Qu,Andreas Willig,Xiaobing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测式短时去中心化贪心算法PB-STDG，用于结构化无人机道路系统，通过预测邻居决策缓解STDG过度保守问题，在保持安全性的同时提升交通效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市中无人机数量激增，多主体无人机需协同避碰并确定无冲突的短期飞行路径，而原STDG算法存在过度保守的问题，影响交通效率。

Method: 在STDG基础上引入预测机制，利用信标包中共享的额外信息，使无人机能够预判邻近无人机的决策。

Result: 通过多个仿真场景与STDG对比评估，结果表明PB-STDG在保持与STDG相当安全水平的同时提升了交通效率。

Conclusion: PB-STDG能够有效改善结构化城市空域中多无人机协同避碰的保守性问题，兼顾安全与效率。

Abstract: In recent years, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) or drones have been increasingly adopted in urban environments for applications such as parcel delivery, infrastructure inspection, emergency response, and drone light shows. A non-negligible issue is how to manage the increasing number of drones operated by different entities, to enable them to cooperatively avoid potential collisions and determine conflict-free short-term flight paths in urban airspace. This paper presents a Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy (PB-STDG) guidance algorithm for a structured Drone Road System (DRS). PB-STDG extends the original STDG algorithm by introducing a prediction mechanism that enables drones to anticipate the decisions of neighboring drones using additional information shared in beacon packets, aiming to address the over-conservative behavior observed in the STDG algorithm. Several simulation scenarios are conducted to evaluate and compare the proposed algorithm with STDG. The results show that PB-STDG improves traffic efficiency while maintaining a safety level comparable to that of STDG.

</details>


### [288] [Recursive Self-Improvement of Visuomotor Policies through Local Recovery Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05151)
*Yuzhi Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过局部恢复监督实现递归自我改进的视觉运动策略框架，并在LIBERO-Goal与robomimic Can任务上验证了成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略能执行熟悉任务，但在自身犯错后缺乏纠正行为，因此需要一种能利用失败状态进行自我改进的机制。

Method: 框架每轮审计当前策略，在支持的失败状态生成纠正演示并更新策略以驱动下一轮采集。离线审计器利用粗细时间证据定位未解决失败并指定可观测修复目标；固定的多模态智能体作为工具型教师，通过观察、计算、执行和反馈生成恢复动作；冻结的学生模型检验教师端点是否支持后续进展，若失败则恢复端点并延长演示。动作级质量评估定义连续训练窗口，包含对齐观测、质量权重和有效性掩码，仅部署学生模型。

Result: 在LIBERO-Goal初步研究中，恢复增强后训练在100个验证场景中成功88次，而同一π₀检查点原始数据继续训练为78次；在robomimic Can的早期BC-RNN研究中，使用26个局部恢复片段将成功从102/130提升至112/130，两者均匹配额外2000步优化。

Conclusion: 局部恢复监督能有效提升视觉运动策略的纠正能力与任务成功率，且仅需部署学生模型。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can execute familiar tasks yet lack the corrective behavior needed after their own mistakes. We present a framework for recursive self-improvement through local recovery supervision. Each round audits the current policy, generates corrective demonstrations at supported failure states, and uses them to update the policy that drives the next round of collection. An offline auditor locates unresolved failures using coarse and dense temporal evidence and specifies observable repair goals. A fixed multimodal agent acts as a tool-using teacher, generating recovery actions through observation, computation, execution, and feedback. The frozen student tests whether each teacher endpoint supports further progress. If continuation fails, the system restores that endpoint and extends the demonstration. Action-level quality assessment then defines continuous training windows with aligned observations, quality weights, and validity masks. Only the student is deployed. In a preliminary LIBERO-Goal study, recovery-augmented post-training achieves 88 successful episodes out of 100 validation scenes, compared with 78 for original-data continuation from the same $π_0$ checkpoint. An earlier BC-RNN study on robomimic Can improves success from 102/130 to 112/130 using 26 local recovery segments. Both comparisons match 2,000 additional optimization steps.

</details>


### [289] [VICON: Visual-Inertial-Contact based Hand-Object Tracking for Manipulation Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05180)
*Yubin Jeon,Uiseong Shin,Hwanchul La,Jaeseong Kang,Hyelim Choi,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VICON框架，联合捕捉手-物运动、接触点与法向力，在严重遮挡下仍保持鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习依赖人类演示数据，接触点与力提供关键监督信息，但现有方法难以联合捕捉手部、物体运动及接触力，且手物交互带来的严重遮挡使跟踪困难。

Method: 采用视觉-惯性手套与RGB-D相机进行手部跟踪，并基于抓握频率在手套布置力敏电阻同步记录接触状态与标定法向力；无需预存CAD模型，利用RGB-D图像与单目视频重建网格估计物体位姿，并提出因子图物体轨迹估计，融合按可见性加权的位姿估计、FSR测量与手部运动先验。

Result: 40组动捕会话、5类物体上，失败帧率2.5%（基线50.9-64.6%），遮挡下中位误差3.9 mm与3.0度；构建含同步手物运动、接触点和法向力的数据集，并公开扩展至10类物体的数据集。

Conclusion: VICON实现了遮挡下鲁棒的手-物-接触联合跟踪，并提供可公开获取的接触力数据集，支持灵巧操作学习。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation benefits from human demonstration datasets that capture diverse and natural hand-object interactions. In particular, contact points and forces provide supervision on where and how strongly to interact, which cannot be fully captured by motion trajectories alone. However, methods for jointly capturing hand and object motion, contact points, and forces remain limited. Moreover, severe occlusion from hand-object interaction challenges accurate tracking of both hands and objects. To address these limitations, we present a Visual-Inertial-CONtact based hand-object tracking (VICON) framework. It holistically captures both hand and object motion along with contact information during manipulation, even under severe occlusion. First, we adopt a visual-inertial glove and an RGB-D camera for accurate hand tracking, and redesign the glove to incorporate contact sensing. Specifically, force-sensitive resistors (FSRs) are placed on the glove based on human grasp frequency to synchronously record contact states and calibrated normal forces. Second, without requiring pre-existing CAD models, we estimate object poses using RGB-D images and a mesh reconstructed from a monocular video. We propose factor-graph-based object trajectory estimation that fuses object-pose estimates weighted by visibility under hand-object occlusion, FSR measurements, and a hand-motion prior. Across 40 motion-capture sessions with five objects, VICON achieves a 2.5% failed-frame rate compared with 50.9-64.6% for the baselines, with median errors of 3.9 mm and 3.0 degrees under occlusion. Using VICON, we construct a dataset containing synchronized hand-object motion, contact points, and normal forces, and will publicly release an expanded dataset covering 10 object categories at https://github.com/VICON-dataset/dataset.

</details>


### [290] [Vela: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with Adaptive Action Curve Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05230)
*Yifan Li,Jiaxu Wang,Dongming Wu,Yicheng Jiang,Ryan Ji,Xiangyu Yue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vela：以连续轨迹（样条+运动相关时间支撑）替代固定速率动作块，统一异构本体接口，用固定输出预算自适应时间分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型把未来运动表示为固定速率的动作块，导致时间分辨率与预测视野被固定输出预算绑定；逐点表示在高度相关的相邻动作上浪费容量，时序连续性与平滑性只能隐式学习，并在长时程覆盖与接触丰富操作所需的局部精度之间被迫取舍。

Method: 提出 Vela 视觉-语言-动作基础模型，将未来机器人行为表示为连续轨迹：由紧凑的样条动作表示、运动依赖的时间支撑（temporal support）以及面向异构本体的共享动作接口组成，使固定输出预算能在不同运动间自适应调整时间分辨率；在大规模多本体机器人数据上预训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO-X、EBench 以及两个真实世界长时程任务（蛋饼烹饪、土豆切丝）上评估，在仿真与物理操作中取得有希望的结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明连续动作表示有潜力成为未来具身基础模型的基础。项目主页：https://clementine24.github.io/Vela/

Abstract: Most vision-language-action models represent future motion as fixed-rate action chunks, tying temporal resolution and prediction horizon to a fixed output budget. This pointwise representation wastes capacity on highly correlated neighboring actions, leaves temporal continuity and smoothness to be learned implicitly, and forces a tradeoff between long-horizon coverage and the local precision required for contact-rich manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Vela, a vision-language-action foundation model that represents future robot behavior as continuous trajectories. Vela combines a compact spline-based action representation with motion-dependent temporal support and a shared action interface for heterogeneous embodiments, allowing a fixed output budget to adapt its temporal resolution across motions. We pretrain Vela on large-scale multi-embodiment robot data and evaluate it on LIBERO-X, EBench, and two real-world long-horizon tasks, egg-cake cooking and potato shredding, obtaining promising results across simulation and physical manipulation. These results highlight the potential of continuous action representations as a foundation for future embodied foundation models. Project page and more results: https://clementine24.github.io/Vela/ .

</details>


### [291] [Safe Ergodic Control for Multi-Robot Systems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05258)
*Yo Toyomoto,Mohamed Elobaid,Bryce L. Ferguson,Alberto Quattrini Li,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于二次规划的控制器，将遍历性与安全性作为联合目标，实现实时安全遍历控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全遍历控制器依赖离线轨迹优化或分层架构，限制实时性，并将遍历目标与安全约束解耦。

Method: 引入时间可微的高斯核遍历度量，替代经典指示函数度量；将其指数衰减作为时变控制障碍函数约束，用松弛变量放松，并与区域包含和机器人间避撞的硬CBF约束一并纳入QP求解。

Result: 证明该QP从任意安全初始构型均可行，且遍历度量（除松弛项外）指数衰减；仿真优于基线方法，三架飞行器实验验证了硬件可行性。

Conclusion: 通过联合处理遍历性与安全性的QP控制器，实现了实时可用的安全遍历控制，并在真实平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Ergodic control drives robots to spend time in each region in proportion to a spatial distribution of interest, making it well suited for dense spatiotemporal environmental monitoring. Existing safe ergodic controllers rely on offline trajectory optimization or hierarchical architectures, which limit real-time applicability and decouple the ergodicity objective from the safety constraint. This paper presents a quadratic programming (QP)-based controller that treats ergodicity and safety jointly. We first introduce a Gaussian-kernel ergodic metric that, unlike the classical indicator-based metric, is time differentiable. This allows the exponential decay of the metric to be imposed as a time-varying control barrier function (CBF) constraint, relaxed by a slack variable, alongside hard CBF constraints for region containment and inter-robot collision avoidance. We establish that the resulting QP remains feasible from any safe initial configuration and that, up to the slack term, the ergodic metric decays exponentially. Simulations show improved performance over baseline methods, and experiments with three aerial vehicles validate the work on real hardware.

</details>


### [292] [CoDance: Learning Reactive and Compliant Human-Humanoid Interaction from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05324)
*Zhuoqun Chen,Shucheng Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDance提出一个从视频学习人形机器人反应式柔顺人-人形交互的框架，以双人舞蹈为验证任务，实现机器人跟随人类伙伴的脚步协调与双手持续接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-人形协作需要将移动与持续身体接触耦合：人形机器人需在维持稳定自然运动的同时，协调人的运动并响应用户施加的交互力。现有方法难以从演示中同时获得 locomotion 风格与力柔顺性。

Method: 给定一段双人舞视频，将其动作重定向为机器人参考轨迹与移动伙伴；引入多连杆柔顺增强（multi-link compliance augmentation），在双手处施加结构化力，将运动学演示转化为力感知训练数据，进而训练策略跟随伙伴并保持演示步态风格、柔顺响应交互力。

Result: 仿真中策略能适应伙伴变化调整脚步，复现约80%增强演示所编码的手腕位移；在实体人形机器人上实现与人类伙伴持续双手共舞，并完成多次前进-后退转换。

Conclusion: 表明从单段视频学习反应式、柔顺的人-人形物理交互是可行的，为需要持续接触的协作locomotion任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Partnered human-humanoid interaction couples locomotion with continuous physical contact. A humanoid needs to coordinate with a person's motion while responding to interaction forces and maintaining stable and natural movement. We present CoDance, a framework for learning reactive and compliant human-humanoid interaction from video. We study partnered dancing as a challenging instantiation, where a humanoid coordinates its footsteps with a moving partner and maintains continuous two-hand contact. Given a single video of two human dancers, CoDance retargets their motions into a robot reference and a moving partner. We introduce a multi-link compliance augmentation that transforms the kinematic demonstration into force-aware training data by adapting the robot reference under structured forces at both hands. Policies trained on this data follow the observed partner while preserving the demonstrated locomotion style and responding compliantly to physical interaction. In simulation, the policies adapt their footsteps to changes in the partner and reproduce approximately 80% of the wrist displacement encoded by the augmented demonstrations. On a physical humanoid, CoDance enables sustained two-hand dancing with a human partner including repeated transitions between forward and backward motions.

</details>


### [293] [VAMPS: Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05331)
*Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Pietro Noah Crestaz,Quang-Nam Nguyen,Qilong Cheng,Ludovic Righetti,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAMPS 利用 MPPI 采样规划生成监督信号，在无人类示范下训练可复用的本体感知与视觉运动策略，并成功迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在物理系统上学习机器人策略面临数据采集成本高、策略探索不安全的问题。

Method: 基于 MPPI 控制生成轨迹用于训练策略，支持两种模式：一是在仿真中进行单步本体感知策略的迭代训练，策略热启动 MPPI，精炼轨迹提供新监督，配合学习到的终端值和 IQL 评论家；二是在真实机器人上用任务状态估计驱动 MPPI 执行并记录 RGB 与传感数据，离线训练基于 Transformer 的动作分块策略。

Result: 迭代精炼优于在固定 MPPI 数据上一次性训练；本体感知策略成功迁移到 Unitree Go2；实现了视觉运动抓放和力感知白板擦除任务。

Conclusion: VAMPS 既可在仿真中学习策略后迁移到硬件，也可直接从自主采集的真实机器人数据中学习策略。

Abstract: Learning robot policies directly on physical systems remains difficult because data collection is costly and policy exploration can be unsafe. We introduce Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning (VAMPS), a framework that uses Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control to train reusable policies without human demonstrations. VAMPS supports two training modes. For one-step proprioceptive policies, it operates iteratively in simulation: the policy warm-starts MPPI, and the refined trajectories provide new supervision as the policy changes. A learned terminal value improves short-horizon planning, while an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) critic guides the policy update. Iterative refinement outperforms training once on frozen MPPI data, and we transfer the learned locomotion policy to a Unitree Go2. For visuomotor policies, VAMPS operates directly from real-robot data. MPPI uses task-specific state estimates to plan and execute trajectories while recording RGB and sensor observations on a Flexiv Rizon 10S. Action Chunking with Transformers predicts action chunks, reducing the effective prediction horizon, and is trained offline on this fixed dataset. We demonstrate visuomotor pick-and-place and force-aware whiteboard erasing. In the latter task, the policy additionally observes the measured $6$-D wrench and desired normal force. These results show that VAMPS can learn policies either in simulation followed by hardware transfer or directly from autonomously collected real-robot data.

</details>


### [294] [Optimal Control with Learned Critics under Unmodeled State Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05359)
*Philipp Schoch,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习型 MPC 框架，将解析模型与残差动力学网络、动作价值评论家结合，并借助 GPU 加速的批量 iLQR 求解器，实现高效且具备长时程结构的基于模型的强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MPC 依赖对优化友好的解析动力学模型，难以处理接触等难建模的状态依赖；而无模型强化学习虽无需建模假设，却需要大量交互数据。因此需要一种兼具数据效率与结构化规划能力、又能补偿模型不完整性的方法。

Method: 在名义解析模型上叠加残差动力学网络，从数据中学习缺失的状态依赖效应；将生成得到的规划器与学习到的动作价值评论家结合，把长时程 MDP 结构注入局部 iLQR 优化；并开发 GPU 加速的批量 iLQR 求解器，在最优控制循环内评估学习到的动力学与评论家网络，并行求解数千个轨迹优化问题，整体集成于机器人仿真器中。

Result: 在存在偏差与建模不完整的控制任务上，该方法提升了闭环控制性能，同时保留了高效约束轨迹优化所需的基于模型的结构。

Conclusion: 学习型 MPC 框架通过结合解析模型、残差动力学学习与价值评论家，并利用 GPU 批量 iLQR，实现了可扩展的基于模型强化学习，在动力学不完整条件下兼顾了数据效率、长时程决策与约束优化结构。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a structured and constraint-aware mechanism for decision-making, but its reliance on optimization-friendly analytical dynamics models limits its use in tasks with contacts and other hard-to-model state dependencies. Model-free reinforcement learning avoids explicit modeling assumptions but typically requires large amounts of interaction data. We present a learning-based MPC framework that combines the data efficiency and structure of local model-based planning with learned components that compensate for incomplete dynamics and finite-horizon myopia. The method augments a nominal analytical model with a residual dynamics network that learns missing state-dependent effects from data and combines the resulting planner with a learned action-value critic that injects long-horizon MDP structure into the local iLQR optimization. To make this practical at reinforcement-learning scale, we develop a GPU-accelerated batched iLQR solver that evaluates learned dynamics and critic networks inside the optimal-control loop and solves thousands of trajectory-optimization problems in parallel. The complete system is integrated into a robotics simulator, enabling scalable model-based reinforcement learning under incomplete dynamics. Experiments on biased and incompletely modeled control tasks show that the approach improves closed-loop control performance while preserving the model-based structure needed for efficient constrained trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [295] [TUCO: Curating Simulation Demonstrations for Sim-to-Real Robot Policy Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05407)
*Ning Zhu,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Zhangyujie Sun,Peihao Li,Dongyue Ni,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究面向仿真到现实机器人策略联合训练的数据筛选问题，提出TUCO方法，利用影响函数将源演示对目标域行为的影响分解为轨迹效用与集合覆盖，实现主动筛选并取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真演示可弥补真实数据稀缺，但如何通过数据筛选主动选择这些演示用于仿真到现实联合训练仍缺乏研究。现有筛选方法也缺少从闭环目标行为出发统一衡量轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖的准则。

Method: 提出TUCO（轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖优化）。利用影响函数追踪每个源演示如何影响目标域评分rollout，并将其效应分解为对目标回报的整体贡献和跨rollout的方差，从而在闭环基础上统一度量轨迹效用与集合覆盖；进一步提出性能对齐的子集优化器，将两种度量结合为统一筛选目标，减少冗余并选出互补演示。

Result: 在RoboMimic和OmniReset上的大量实验验证了主动仿真数据筛选对仿真到现实策略联合训练的价值，TUCO在单仿真器、仿真到仿真以及仿真到现实设置下均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: TUCO通过统一的闭环效用与覆盖度量实现了有效的仿真数据筛选，证明主动数据筛选能显著提升仿真到现实机器人策略联合训练效果。

Abstract: Simulation demonstrations can supplement scarce real-world data for robot policy co-training. However, the value of using data curation to actively select these demonstrations for sim-to-real co-training remains underexplored. Existing curation methods also lack a unified criterion for measuring trajectory-level utility and set-level coverage from closed-loop target behavior. To address these gaps, we present the first systematic study of data curation for sim-to-real robot policy co-training and propose Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization (TUCO). TUCO uses influence functions to trace how each source demonstration affects target-domain scoring rollouts. Our key insight is that these effects can be decomposed into an overall contribution to target return and variation across rollouts, providing a common closed-loop basis for measuring trajectory utility and set coverage. We further propose a performance-aligned subset optimizer that combines these measures in a unified curation objective to reduce redundancy and select complementary demonstrations. Extensive experiments on RoboMimic and OmniReset establish the value of active simulation data curation for sim-to-real policy co-training and show that TUCO achieves state-of-the-art performance across single-simulator, sim-to-sim, and sim-to-real settings.

</details>


### [296] [RMMBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robotic Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05414)
*Huapeng Li,Fuxiang Feng,Jinqiu Fan,Shuo Yang,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Ran Song,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RMMBench 评测基准，将高层与低层具身任务统一到"导航-操作"任务套件中，用于细粒度评估 VLM 在机器人导航与操作中的具身能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）提升了机器人的环境理解与任务推理能力，但现有基准缺乏对多样化机器人任务的综合评测方法，评价指标也较为有限，难以全面、细粒度地衡量 VLM 的具身能力。

Method: 构建 RMMBench 基准，要求机器人理解语言指令并在连续空间中执行长时序任务；将高层和低层具身任务无缝整合至统一框架，形成包含 70 个典型任务场景的"导航-操作"任务套件，覆盖从局部操作到长时序复合导航的多种任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，主流 VLM 在执行移动操作任务时仍面临显著的**空间定位**挑战；同时凸显了在长时序交互中增强机器人**空间感知能力**的必要性。

Conclusion: RMMBench 提供了统一且细粒度的具身能力评测方案，可作为推动 VLM 融入机器人导航与操作的重要工具；基准已公开于 https://mxxq-stack.github.io/rmmbench-project/。

Abstract: Although the advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has endowed robots with enhanced environmental understanding and task reasoning, a comprehensive evaluation methodology is important to advance the integration of VLMs in robotic navigation and manipulation. However, current benchmarks lack a comprehensive method to evaluate diverse robotic tasks, and evaluation metrics remain relatively constrained, making it difficult to assess the embodied capabilities of VLMs in a thorough and fine-grained manner. To address this issue, we propose RMMBench, an evaluation benchmark that requires robots to understand language instructions and perform long-horizon tasks in continuous spaces. RMMBench seamlessly integrates high- and low-level embodied tasks into a unified framework, constructing a "navigation-manipulation" task suite comprising 70 canonical task scenarios that range from localized manipulation to long-horizon composite navigation. The results reveal that leading VLMs still face major challenges in spatial localization when performing mobile manipulation tasks, and also highlight the necessity of enhancing the spatial perception capability of robots during long-horizon interactions. RMMBench can be accessed at https://mxxq-stack.github.io/rmmbench-project/

</details>


### [297] [EvoMem-VLA: State-Evolution Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05418)
*Yuheng Na,Zhide Zhong,Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaan Wang,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Tianyu Huang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EvoMem-VLA，通过编码历史状态间的变化构建状态演化记忆，提升长时程记忆依赖任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数VLA模型依赖当前观测，一旦任务相关证据离开视野便丢失，限制长时程、记忆依赖任务表现。现有方法用压缩历史特征或稀疏关键帧，但孤立快照无法让策略了解过去交互中的变化及后续动作。

Method: 构建状态演化记忆，显式编码并保留历史状态间的观测变化。引入条件增量tokenization，将有序帧对编码为有方向的、源条件化的delta token，并关联相应状态证据。共享VLM主干支持任务自适应路由：长时程任务走直接动作路径，多阶段任务走子任务路径，先生成可执行子任务作为动作生成的额外输入。每个仿真基准训练单一联合策略。

Result: RMBench成功率80.7%，RoboMME为82.0%，四个真实世界任务（跨两种机器人本体）为83.8%，三个评测设置均显著超越此前最优。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码状态间变化构建演化记忆，可有效跟踪任务进展，显著提升长时程记忆依赖任务的性能。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on current observations and lose task-relevant evidence once it leaves view, limiting performance on long-horizon, memory-dependent tasks. Existing efforts incorporate compressed historical features or sparse visual keyframes. However, isolated snapshots can leave the policy uncertain about what changed during past interactions and which action should follow. To overcome this limitation, we propose EvoMem-VLA, which constructs state-evolution memory by explicitly encoding and retaining observed changes between historical states. These change representations preserve evidence of interaction outcomes, allowing the policy to track task progress beyond isolated snapshots. Specifically, we introduce conditional delta tokenization to encode ordered frame pairs into directional, source-conditioned delta tokens, each associated with its corresponding state evidence. A shared VLM backbone supports task-adaptive routing: normal long-horizon tasks follow a direct action route, whereas multi-stage tasks use a subtask route that generates an executable subtask as an additional input for action generation. With a single jointly trained policy for each simulation benchmark, EvoMem-VLA achieves success rates of 80.7\% on RMBench, 82.0\% on RoboMME and 83.8\% across four real-world tasks spanning two robot embodiments. These results represent substantial improvements over the previous state of the art in all three evaluation settings.

</details>


### [298] [Social Navigation for Tour-guide Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05455)
*Vikram Shree,Jose Nino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于力的社交导航模型，用于导游机器人，通过实验评估各社会力对用户社交感受的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让导游机器人在导航中更具社交性，考虑障碍物、用户位置与朝向等因素对用户跟随体验的影响。

Method: 建立力模型，将障碍物、用户位置和朝向等因素作为社会力；设计实验让用户跟随自主机器人在已知地图中到达目的地，中途完成简单干扰子任务；每名参与者进行多轮实验，分别采用不同力组合及A*最短路径基线模型；用每轮主观指标评估。

Result: 计划研究该力模型对可跟随性、感知安全性和感知智能等构念的影响（实验评估方案）。

Conclusion: 通过逐力对比实验，验证各社会力对提升机器人社交性的作用。

Abstract: We propose a force-based model for social navigation of a tour-guide robot. Social forces due to various factors like obstacles, user position and heading, have been accounted for in the model. We claim that each one of these forces makes the robot more sociable to the user and we design an experimental setup for evaluation. In the experiment, the user follows an autonomous robot to a destination in a known map, while undertaking a few simple sub-tasks in the middle, which serve as distractions. For each participant, we run several rounds of the experiment, each with different forces and a shortest path, A*-search baseline model. Using per-round subjective indicators, we propose to study the effect of our force model on constructs such as: follow-ability, perceived safety, and perceived intelligence.

</details>


### [299] [FLEX-WAM: Flexible Block-Causal World-Action Models for Long-Horizon Imagination and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05483)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joseph Amigo,Félix Lebel,Leon Seetoo,Jean Ponce,Zhenzhen Li,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLEX-WAM是一种灵活高效的分块因果世界-动作模型，支持变长上下文、非因果预测视野和无限自回归生成，实现稳定的长时域联合状态-动作推演与实时规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-动作模型常采用计算繁重、固定时域的主干网络，不适合流式推理和稳定的长时域开环推演。此外，联合训练可能产生对未来预测合理但对指令动作响应微弱的问题。

Method: 提出FLEX-WAM，采用分块因果、可缓存KV的架构，结合轴向注意力与分块扩散强迫，支持任意长度上下文和无限自回归生成。通过平衡状态与动作的流匹配梯度贡献，并利用前向动力学弹性（FD elasticity）调节世界模型采样，以增强动作响应性。

Result: 在仿真和真实数据集上，FLEX-WAM在多步预测质量和延迟方面表现优越，可稳定生成数千步的联合状态-动作推演。作为MCTS中的联合动作提议器和模拟器，完全在想象中解决长时域PushT和全部五个OGBench Puzzle-4x4任务。在双臂OpenArm机器人上，单一检查点同时作为play策略和预期结果预测器，实现模型-现实不匹配的实时识别与收集。

Conclusion: FLEX-WAM为统一的仿真与策略推理提供了高效、可扩展且动作响应良好的世界-动作模型框架，适用于长时域规划与真实机器人自改进。

Abstract: World--action models (WAMs) promise a unified model that predicts action-conditioned futures, generates feasible actions, and supports planning in imagination. However, existing joint video--action models often use computationally heavy, fixed-horizon backbones ill-suited to streaming inference and stable long-horizon open-loop rollouts. We introduce FLEX-WAM, a Flexible and Efficient Block-Causal World--Action Model for unified simulation and policy inference. FLEX-WAM supports variable-length contexts and non-causal prediction horizons, as well as infinite autoregressive generation frame by frame or block by block. Its block-causal, KV-cacheable architecture combines axial attention and blockwise diffusion forcing to enable efficient real-time rollout and deployment-time latency--throughput tradeoffs without retraining. Joint training can nevertheless produce plausible futures that weakly respond to commanded actions. We address this failure mode by balancing state and action flow-matching gradient contributions across the state--action diffusion-noise grid and regulating world-model sampling using Forward-Dynamics (FD) elasticity, an efficient training-time proxy for action responsiveness. Across simulated and real-world datasets, FLEX-WAM achieves superior multi-step prediction quality and latency while producing stable joint state--action rollouts for thousands of steps. As a joint action proposer and simulator within MCTS, it solves long-horizon PushT and all five OGBench Puzzle-4x4 tasks entirely in imagination. On a bimanual OpenArm-based robot, a single checkpoint jointly serves as a play policy and expected-outcome predictor, enabling real-time identification and collection of model--reality mismatches for future self-improvement.

</details>


### [300] [Robust Surgical Robotic Instrument Tracking via Sequential Multi-Cue Fusion and Sim-to-Real Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05491)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出追踪器引导的自训练框架，将合成数据预训练的模型适配到无标注真实手术视频，提升器械关键点检测精度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人器械追踪对微创手术很重要，但手术场景复杂、器械几何特殊带来挑战。基于关键点的方法高效，却依赖可靠的特征检测；而真实场景的精确标注代价高昂，难以大规模获取，导致检测器难以用真实监督提升。

Method: 采用追踪器引导的自训练框架：利用测得的机器人关节状态，用不确定性感知的EKF递归校正器械位姿与可观测关节角，通过比较投影的模型特征与检测到的关键点、轴边界及掩膜线索实现；再用RTS平滑器细化轨迹，并将其投影为伪标签，用于微调特征检测器，无需费力的人工位姿标注。

Result: 在真实视频上的实验表明，自训练在所有评估的关键点指标上均带来一致提升，所得模型在精度和运行时间上均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过追踪器生成伪标签实现无监督域适配，有效克服真实标注稀缺的瓶颈，为手术器械追踪提供了高效且准确的方案，代码与数据将于发表后公开。

Abstract: Efficient and robust tracking of surgical robotic instruments is important for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery, yet remains challenging due to the complexity of surgical scenes and the unconventional geometry of surgical instruments. Keypoint-based approaches are efficient, but their performance depends on reliable feature detection. Improving these detectors with real-world supervision is difficult because accurate real-world annotations are costly to obtain at scale. To address this limitation, we introduce a tracker-guided self-training framework that adapts a model pretrained on synthetic images to unlabeled real-world videos. Given measured robot joint states, an uncertainty-aware EKF recursively corrects the instrument pose and the observable joint angles by comparing projected model features with detected keypoints, shaft boundaries, and mask-derived cues. An RTS smoother subsequently refines the resulting trajectory, which is projected into pseudo-labels for fine-tuning the feature detector without laborious pose annotations. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate consistent improvements from self-training across all evaluated keypoint metrics, and the resulting model outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime. The code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [301] [Robust 2D Traversability Mapping for Construction AMRs via Failure-Mode-Aware Fusion of LiDAR Geometry and Monocular Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05505)
*Manoj Karnekar,Om Mandhane,Gautham Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向活跃施工工地的实时、故障模式感知多模态可通行性管线，LiDAR为主几何安全估计，单目语义作为选择性校正信号，并发布多模态施工工地数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMR在活跃施工工地导航面临严重挑战：基于几何的LiDAR可通行性建图会漏检视觉危险但几何平坦的表面（如湿泥、积水混凝土），而减速带和斜坡的突变几何则会产生幻影障碍。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上构建实时管线：LiDAR作为主要几何安全估计，单目语义作为类别与置信度门控的选择性校正信号；表示保留不同可通行类别（平坦道路、地形、岩石地形）并标记施工危险。将LiDAR投影到密集语义掩码，通过形态学修复解决稀疏性，结合Patchwork++进行故障模式感知融合，修正互补几何故障模式以生成局部AMR代价地图。

Result: 发布来自定制AMR的多模态施工工地数据集：两个活跃工地的四条ROS 2闭环序列（RGB、深度、LiDAR、IMU、GPS-RTK、里程计），以及28个语义类别的506个标注帧。

Conclusion: 通过利用语义对几何可通行性估计进行选择性校正，系统能够纠正互补的几何故障模式，为施工工地上的AMR生成更可靠的局部代价地图。

Abstract: Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) on active construction sites face severe navigational challenges: geometry-based traversability mapping (e.g., LiDAR) misses visually hazardous but geometrically flat surfaces like wet mud and ponding concrete, while abrupt geometry on drivable speed-breakers and inclines produces phantom obstacles. We propose a real-time, failure-mode-aware multimodal traversability pipeline on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, where LiDAR is the primary geometric safety estimate and monocular semantics act as a selective, class- and confidence-gated corrective signal. The representation retains distinct traversable classes, namely flat road, terrain, and rocky terrain, while flagging construction hazards. We also release a multimodal construction-site dataset from a custom AMR: four closed-loop ROS 2 sequences from two active sites (RGB, depth, LiDAR, IMU, GPS-RTK, odometry) plus 506 annotated frames across 28 semantic classes. By projecting LiDAR onto dense semantic masks, resolving sparsity via morphological in-painting, and applying failure-mode-aware fusion with Patchwork++, the system corrects complementary geometric failure modes for a local AMR costmap.

</details>


### [302] [Neural Barriers: An Online Certifiable Learning-enhanced Adaptive High Order Safety Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05542)
*Lishuo Pan,Mattia Catellani,An Cao,Lorenzo Sabattini,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可认证的在线学习增强鲁棒自适应控制障碍函数，用Neural ODE自适应扰动、用共形预测量化适应不确定性，在未知时变模型扰动下给出概率安全保证，兼顾保守性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制障碍函数能形式化认证系统安全，但把理论保证迁移到真实机器人需要高模型保真度。载荷变化或风扰等会引起显著模型扰动（如飞行器），导致安全性受损，因此需要在未知时变扰动下仍能保证安全的在线自适应方法。

Method: 提出可认证的在线学习增强鲁棒自适应控制障碍函数：利用Neural ODE对扰动进行自适应建模，并借助共形预测量化其适应不确定性；在不确定性高时采取保守策略，随着数据增加高效自适应以降低控制器保守性；在模型与轨迹满足适当Lipschitz光滑性假设下，给出具有概率界的可证明安全保证。

Result: 该方法在未知时变模型扰动下保证全时段安全，并能在适当Lipschitz光滑性假设下提供带概率界的可证明安全保证；控制器能在不确定性高时保守、数据增多后逐步降低保守性。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法有潜力作为在模型扰动下运行的机器人系统的实用安全控制器。

Abstract: Control barrier functions are an effective model-based tool to formally certify the safety of a system. However, transferring their theoretical guarantees to real-world robotics systems requires high model fidelity. For example, payloads or wind disturbances can cause significant model perturbations to an aerial vehicle, leading to safety compromises. In this work, we propose a certifiable online learning-enhanced robust adaptive control barrier function, which adapts to disturbances using a Neural ODE and quantifies its adaptation uncertainty with conformal prediction. Our approach guarantees safety at all time under unknown time-varying model disturbances. It adopts a conservative strategy when the adaptation uncertainty is high; and efficiently adapts to reduce controller conservativeness as it receives more data. Our approach provides a provable safety guarantee with a probability bound under suitable Lipschitz smoothness assumptions on the underlying model and trajectory. These results demonstrate the potential of our method as a practical safety controller for robotics system operating under model perturbations.

</details>


### [303] [GR-LIO: A Local Ground-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry System Using Body-to-Ground Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05546)
*Zhixin Zhang,Yang Song,Liang Zhao,Nathan Shankar,Barry Lennox,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于滤波的局部地面感知LIO框架，将局部地面平面几何显式引入状态估计，提升定位精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO系统对局部地面提供的几何约束利用不足，未能充分发挥地面信息在状态估计中的作用。

Method: 将局部地面平面用机器人姿态和机体-地面(B-G)高度参数化并在状态估计中持续传播；基于B-G几何模型实现高效可靠的地面分割；将分割地面点通过点到面约束引入滤波更新；提出平面运动更新抑制垂直漂移；针对未知B-G高度设计初始化策略与在线标定。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集和自采集真实场景数据集上评估，所提方法在定位精度和计算效率上均持续优于代表性LIO方法。

Conclusion: 显式利用局部地面几何约束可有效提升LIO的定位精度、鲁棒性与计算效率，为地面移动机器人状态估计提供有效方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for state estimation in ground-based autonomous mobile robots. However, the geometric constraints provided by the local ground surface remain largely underexploited in existing LIO systems. This paper proposes a filter-based local ground-aware LIO framework that explicitly incorporates local ground plane geometry into the state estimation process to improve both localization accuracy and computational efficiency. Specifically, a local ground plane is parameterized by the robot orientation and the body-to-ground (B-G) height and continuously propagated within the state estimation process. Based on the proposed B-G geometry model, a propagated local ground plane enables efficient and reliable ground segmentation. The segmented ground points are then incorporated into the filter update through point-to-plane geometric constraints, improving both state estimation accuracy and efficiency. Furthermore, a planar motion update is introduced to exploit the propagated local ground plane as an additional geometric constraint, effectively suppressing vertical drift and improving estimation robustness. To address the initially unknown B-G height, an efficient initialization strategy is developed, followed by an online calibration procedure for continuous refinement. The proposed system is evaluated on several public benchmark datasets and self-collected real-world datasets covering diverse operating scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative LIO methods in terms of both localization accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [304] [TacGELLO: Tactile Contact Feedback for a 3D-Printed Teleoperation Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05583)
*Younsoo Kim,Sangmin Song,Sarath Kodagoda,Marc Carmichael,Karthick Thiyagarajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacGELLO 利用 3D 打印主导臂的现有伺服电机，在夹爪扳机处提供触觉接触反馈、在关节处提供基于电流的负载提示，实现被动式主从遥操作的力/接触感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动式主导臂只能传递操作者动作，无法回传远程接触或负载信息，导致操作者缺乏力觉与接触感知。作者希望在不改造低成本 3D 打印主导臂硬件的前提下，为其补充触觉与负载反馈。

Method: 复用主导臂现有伺服电机：AnySkin 接触检测触发扳机位置保持；跟随臂关节电流变化用于调整主导臂伺服输出限幅，从而在起吊时产生阻力感。在 UR3 平台上进行 6 组单人实验，记录发送指令跟踪误差、轨迹滞后、触觉触发与闭合指令。

Result: 发送指令跟踪误差为 0.15–0.36° RMSE，估计轨迹滞后 82–98 ms。62 个分割后的 TacGELLO 抓取区间中有 60 个记录到触觉触发。触觉或宽度基准接触后，TacGELLO 的中位闭合指令为 0.7–2.1 mm，而原接口为 13.0–13.9 mm。

Conclusion: TacGELLO 可在不新增硬件的情况下为被动主导臂提供有效的触觉与负载反馈，接触后闭合精度显著优于原接口，且指令跟踪与延迟表现良好。

Abstract: Passive leader arms transmit an operator's motion without returning remote contact or load cues. TacGELLO uses the existing servos of a 3D-printed leader for tactile contact feedback at the gripper trigger and current-based load cues at the arm joints. AnySkin contact detection activates a trigger position hold, while changes in follower joint current adjust leader-servo output limits to command resistance during lifting.
  Across six UR3 sessions with one operator, sent-command tracking error was 0.15--0.36$^\circ$ RMSE, with estimated trajectory lag of 82--98\,ms. Tactile onset was logged in 60 of 62 segmented TacGELLO grasp intervals. Median commanded closure after tactile or width-based contact references was 0.7--2.1\,mm with TacGELLO and 13.0--13.9\,mm with the original interface.

</details>


### [305] [Building A Multi-Sensor Platform For Autonomous Driving Research: Challenges and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05604)
*Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Eslam Mounier,Qamar Bader,Emma Dawson,Aboelmagd Noureldin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分享了在开发与部署面向自动驾驶研究的柔性多传感器平台过程中遇到的挑战与经验教训，旨在为开发新型多传感器系统的研究者提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对可靠、多样化数据集需求的增长，新环境与新传感配置对应对真实场景挑战至关重要，许多研究团队因此自建多传感器数据采集平台。然而无论传感器类型如何，机械设计、传感器标定、电源管理和时间同步等根本问题都会出现，因此有必要总结这些共性挑战以指导未来平台设计。

Method: 作者基于自建柔性多传感器平台的开发与部署实践，反思其中的挑战并总结关键洞见；具体采用了基于GNSS授时与NTP协议的系统级时间同步、针对不同传感器配置的定制标定流程，以及提升可靠性与数据完整性的设计实践等缓解策略。

Result: 报告识别出机械设计、传感器标定、电源管理与时间同步等共性根本问题，并给出了相应的缓解策略，包括系统级时间同步、定制标定流程与外场部署中的可靠性设计实践。

Conclusion: 本文的经验与洞见可为未来多传感器平台设计提供指导，有助于提升自动驾驶测试中的可复现性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper reports on the challenges encountered and lessons learned during the development and deployment of a flexible multi-sensor platform for autonomous driving research. It aims to serve as a reference for researchers developing new multi-sensor systems. As the need for reliable, diverse datasets increases, novel environments and sensing configurations are essential to tackle real-world operational challenges. Consequently, many research groups create custom multi-sensory data collection platforms, where fundamental issues, such as mechanical design, sensor calibration, power management, and time synchronization, arise regardless of sensor types. We reflect on these challenges and share key insights to guide future platform designs, enhancing reproducibility and robustness in autonomous vehicle testing. We also summarize the mitigation strategies we adopted, for instance, system-wide time synchronization using GNSS timing and NTP protocols, custom calibration routines for different sensor configurations, and design practices to improve reliability and data integrity during field deployments.

</details>


### [306] [Emergency Obstacle Avoidance Maneuvers in Differential-Drive Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05607)
*Kautik Mandve,Nilay Kant*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过540次随机分组实验，考察差速驱动机器人在45-65 cm/s接近速度下五种紧急制动与转向动作的性能及里程计误差，发现反向旋转动作的编码器误差随速度显著增大并与避障成功率下降相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急避障要求移动机器人在可用距离内制动或改变航向，但机动性能与里程计误差对接近速度的依赖关系尚不明确，需要实证表征以支持速度特定的紧急机动设计。

Method: 采用足迹间隙分析与归一化轮运动指标对五种制动和转向机动进行运动学描述；在瓷砖地面上开展540次分组随机试验（保留539次），命令接近速度为45、55、65 cm/s；使用俯视相机提供独立位姿参考，比较编码器、陀螺仪与相机航向变化。

Result: 在完整运动记录上，两种反向旋转机动在最低与最高速度间平均编码器偏差增加4.7-5.1度，而弧线与制动辅助枢轴变化小于0.4度；较大编码器偏差与较低避障成功率相关（已对距离、速度、机动、日期与转向方向进行调整）；反向旋转的陀螺仪偏差约为3度；并给出了90%成功率所需反应距离及其不确定性。

Conclusion: 结果支持对紧急机动进行速度特定的经验表征，并将编码器误差与恢复运动和测量窗口不匹配区分开来。

Abstract: Emergency obstacle avoidance requires a mobile robot to brake or change its heading within the available distance. This paper investigates the dependence of maneuver performance and odometric error on approach speed for a differential-drive robot. A footprint-clearance analysis and a normalized wheel-motion index provide a kinematic description of five braking and turning maneuvers. The principal experiment comprises 540 block-randomized trials on tile, of which 539 are retained, at commanded approach speeds of 45, 55, and 65 cm/s. An overhead camera provides an independent pose reference. When encoder, gyro, and camera heading changes are evaluated over complete motion records, the mean encoder discrepancy increases by 4.7-5.1 degrees for the two reverse-spin maneuvers between the lowest and highest speeds; the arc and brake-assisted pivot change by less than 0.4 degrees. Larger encoder discrepancy is associated with lower avoidance success after adjustment for distance, speed, maneuver, day, and turn direction. Gyro discrepancies remain approximately 3 degrees for the reverse spins. Estimated reaction distances for 90% success and their uncertainty quantify the maneuver trade-offs. The results support speed-specific empirical characterization of emergency maneuvers and distinguish encoder error from recovery motion and measurement-window mismatch.

</details>


### [307] [FOCUS: Fine-Grained Open-Vocabulary Change Detection for Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05639)
*Can Xu,Mingfeng Yuan,Mahan Mohammadi,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCUS框架，用于半静态环境中机器人对象级变化检测与地图维护，通过融合几何与3D高斯渲染外观的似然进行概率推断，显著提升替换检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的自主机器人需要在环境变化时更新记忆。在半静态环境中，物体可能被几何与语义相似的实例原地替换，仅凭几何或粗粒度语义难以检测这种身份变化。

Method: 将半静态记忆维护形式化为概率推断，融合几何似然与从3D高斯地图渲染的外观似然；采用递归三状态估计器判断每个物体为PERSISTED、REPLACED或REMOVED；针对3DGS渲染不完美，将渲染外观证据建模为概率而非直接变化分数，参数由初始建图会话的无变化回放自动校准。

Result: 在含歧义原地替换的新Isaac Sim仓库基准及真实TorWIC数据集上评估，对象级替换F1从概率基线的0.20提升至0.84，并可无需手动调参迁移至真实数据。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过不确定性感知的概率融合方法，有效解决了相似实例原地替换的检测难题，具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating over long periods must keep their environmental memory up to date as the world changes between visits. In semi-static environments, an object may be replaced in place by a different but geometrically and semantically similar instance, making the identity change difficult to detect from geometry or coarse semantics alone. We present FOCUS, an uncertainty-aware framework for object-level change detection and map maintenance. We formulate semi-static memory maintenance as probabilistic inference that fuses geometric likelihood with appearance likelihood rendered from a 3D Gaussian map. A recursive three-state estimator maintains whether each mapped object is PERSISTED, REPLACED, or REMOVED. To account for imperfect 3DGS rendering, we model the rendered appearance evidence probabilistically rather than using it as a direct change score, with the model parameters automatically calibrated from a change-free replay of the initial mapping session. We evaluate our method on a new Isaac Sim warehouse benchmark with ambiguous in-place replacements and on the real-world TorWIC dataset. It improves object-level replacement F1 from 0.20 to 0.84 over a probabilistic baseline and transfers to real-world data without manual parameter retuning.

</details>


### [308] [Spacecraft Rendezvous Trajectory Generation with Modular Constraints via Diffusion Model Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05642)
*Mariko A. Storey-Matsutani,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的交会与接近操作（RPO）轨迹生成方法，可灵活组合多种任务约束而无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴任务（在轨服务、卫星巡检、主动碎片清除）需要能适应多种场景的轨迹设计方法，传统方法难以灵活配置任务约束。

Method: 首先基于优化轨迹分别训练针对不同约束（接近锥、传感器视线）的能量型扩散模型；推理时将学习到的能量模型相互组合，或与解析定义的能量场组合，以施加特定约束组合。

Result: 通过组合学习的接近锥模型与传感器视线模型，以及接近锥模型与合成避障模型进行验证，约束满足率与单约束模型相差不超过1个百分点或更高。

Conclusion: 该组合式扩散框架为RPO轨迹设计提供了模块化方法，可在无需重新训练的情况下针对新约束组合进行重构。

Abstract: Emerging mission classes such as on-orbit servicing, satellite inspection, and active debris removal require trajectory design methods that are adaptable to a variety of mission scenarios. We present a diffusion-based trajectory generation approach for rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) that enables flexible configuration of mission constraints. First, individual energy-based diffusion models are trained to satisfy distinct constraints such as approach cone and sensor line-of-sight from a set of optimized trajectories. Then, at inference time, the learned energy models can be composed with one another, or with an analytically defined energy field, to enforce specific constraint combinations. We validate this framework with the composition of a learned approach cone model and a learned sensor line-of-sight model, as well as a learned approach cone model and synthetic obstacle avoidance model, both of which yield constraint satisfaction rates that are within 1 percentage point of the single-constraint models or higher. These results indicate that our compositional diffusion framework can provide a modular approach to RPO trajectory design and enable reconfiguration for new constraint combinations without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [309] [Bayesian Data Augmentation for DNN Retraining with Binomial Outcomes in Vision-Based UAV Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05674)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合贝叶斯数据增强与高保真仿真器的迭代重训练框架，用于优化无人机着陆停机坪检测网络，提升视觉着陆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止或复杂城市环境中，视觉着陆对无人机任务可靠性至关重要，但真实着陆点非结构化且多变，要求感知系统具有强泛化能力。合成数据增强虽可扩展学习，但DNN重训练成本高、试飞验证困难，限制了充分的模型微调，因此需要优化的重训练流程。

Method: 将贝叶斯数据增强框架与具备高保真飞行动力学的逼真仿真器相结合，以着陆性能为目标函数，迭代重训练停机坪检测DNN。

Result: 在不同环境条件和载具状态的仿真实验中，着陆性能得到提升，且预测着陆结果的置信区间更窄。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效优化视觉着陆检测模型，在复杂环境下提升着陆性能并提高结果预测的稳定性。

Abstract: In GPS-denied or cluttered urban environments, vision-based landing is essential for reliable UAV missions. Real-world landing sites are often unstructured and highly variable, requiring strong generalization by the perception system. Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) trained with synthetic data augmentation offer a scalable solution for learning landing-site features across diverse vehicle and environmental states. However, computationally expensive DNN retraining, along with challenging performance validation via test flights, limits exhaustive model fine-tuning and necessitates an optimized retraining pipeline. In this work, we deploy a Bayesian data augmentation framework integrated with a photorealistic simulator featuring high-fidelity vehicle dynamics to iteratively retrain the helipad detector DNN, maximizing landing performance as the objective function.
  We validate our framework with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, demonstrating improved landing performance and tighter confidence intervals on predicted landing outcomes.

</details>


### [310] [Learning Coordinated Visuomotor Box-Pushing from Solo Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05677)
*Ryusei Matsumoto,Mei Kinjo,Yoko Sasaki,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无通信、机载式去中心化多机器人模仿学习，以协调推箱为测试平台，探索单机器人遥操作数据复用于大规模多机器人任务，并系统研究数据集构建策略与轻量策略架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人模仿学习面临集体示教数据难采集的问题：单个操作者无法同时控制多台机器人。同时，许多协调任务主要靠最小化机器人间干扰实现系统效率，这与耦合协作操作不同，因而促使作者探究单机器人遥操作数据能否用于大规模协调推箱。

Method: 以协调推箱任务为测试平台，系统研究数据集创建策略与轻量级策略架构，开展最多40台机器人的实验，并考察通过被动观察其他运行机器人获取协调能力的可行性。

Result: 实验表明，仅通过被动观察其他运行机器人难以获得有效协调能力，这一现象在最多40台机器人的实验中尤为突出，揭示了多机器人研究中的具体瓶颈。

Conclusion: 单机器人遥操作数据在大规模协调任务中的复用仍存在明显局限，被动观察不足以支撑有效协调，多机器人模仿学习的数据采集与策略设计仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Multi-robot imitation learning, particularly in settings where visuomotor policies are deployed in a communication-free, onboard decentralised style, represents an attractive paradigm. However, its realisation remains insufficiently understood, largely due to the difficulty of collecting collective demonstrations, since a single operator cannot control many robots simultaneously. Meanwhile, unlike coupled collaborative manipulation, many coordinated tasks achieve system-wide efficiency primarily through minimising inter-robot interference. This structure motivates us to study whether data collected by a teleoperated single-robot can be leveraged for large-scale coordinated box-pushing as a testbed. We systematically investigate dataset creation strategies and lightweight policy architectures. In particular, experiments with up to 40 robots highlight the difficulty of acquiring effective coordination solely through passive observation of other operating robots, revealing a concrete bottleneck for multi-robot research.

</details>


### [311] [Dataset-Free Compliant Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Dynamic Online Posture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05678)
*Seungho Yeom,Zhenyu Wu,Jaeyoung Huh,Diego Williams,Yuheng Zhi,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCLO是一个无需人类动作数据、仅用双末端执行器目标指令的人形移动作业系统，通过在线姿态生成与全身柔顺性训练实现稳定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形移动作业控制器大多依赖人类动作数据来学习全身协调与姿态，导致策略必须依赖外部数据源。本文希望摆脱这一依赖，仅用两个末端执行器目标作为指令。

Method: OCLO（Online-posture Compliant LOco-manipulation）仅以两个末端执行器目标为输入。由于这些目标无法唯一确定全身姿态，它利用解析可达性先验在线生成骨盆高度与躯干朝向，并通过策略在环采样与任务无关代价进行精化；同时通过弹簧-阻尼模型根据实测力偏移末端参考，学习全身柔顺性，使腿、腰、骨盆对外部载荷让步。

Result: 仿真中，使用可达性先验使目标获取成功率达77.8%，远高于无先验的37.8%；精化降低了所有评估任务的末端朝向误差。同一姿态模块在五项任务中的四项上提升了预训练的SONIC控制器。无柔顺训练的策略在扰动下更易失衡。在Unitree G1上，OCLO在使消融版本失败的末端扰动下仍保持平衡，并完成七项移动作业任务，包括蹲姿行走和从低矮表面拾取箱子。

Conclusion: 无需人类动作数据，仅凭末端目标指令结合解析可达性先验与在线精化，并配合全身柔顺性学习，即可让人形机器人实现稳健的移动作业与抗扰平衡。

Abstract: Most humanoid loco-manipulation controllers require human motion data to learn whole-body coordination and posture, leaving policies reliant on external sources to provide this data. We present OCLO (Online-posture Compliant LOco-manipulation), a humanoid loco-manipulation system trained without human motion data and commanded only through two end-effector targets. Because these targets do not uniquely determine whole-body posture, OCLO generates pelvis height and torso orientation online using an analytic reachability prior, further refined through policy-in-the-loop sampling with a task-agnostic cost. OCLO also learns whole-body compliance by displacing end-effector references according to measured forces through a spring-damper model, encouraging the legs, waist, and pelvis to yield to external loads. In simulation, using the reachability prior leads to a 77.8% success rate in acquiring the commanded reference, a vast improvement over the 37.8% success rate accomplished without the prior. Further, refinement reduces end-effector orientation error across all evaluated tasks. The same posture module improves a pretrained SONIC controller on four of five tasks. Without compliance training, policies tend to lose balance under disturbances rather than sacrifice tracking. On a Unitree G1, OCLO maintains balance under end-effector disturbances that cause its ablations to fail and performs seven loco-manipulation tasks, including crouched walking and picking up a box from a low surface. Project website: https://oclo-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [312] [Benchmarking Generative Trajectory Models for Active-Inference Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05692)
*Yulin Li,Mohsen A. Jafari,Andrea Matta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式主动推断控制（GenAIF）：用单一生成轨迹模型同时完成动作提议、受控预测与观测证据映射，并在MuJoCo任务上比较扩散、自回归Transformer、CVAE与流匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统动力学难以显式建模，轨迹示范为主动推断控制提供了一条替代路径；需要明确该控制设计对生成轨迹模型的建模要求。

Method: GenAIF：一个生成轨迹模型从示范与施加动作的干预数据中学习，提供目标条件策略分布及状态到观测的似然映射。由此推导三项模型要求：有效的动作提议、施加动作下的准确预测、用于信念更新与期望信息增益的概率观测证据。在MuJoCo多物理条件操作任务上基准比较扩散模型、自回归Transformer、CVAE与流匹配。

Result: 扩散模型在测试动力学中控制效果最佳；CVAE以远快得多的推断给出相当的短时程预测。正确条件化起决定性作用，轨迹复用可进一步节省计算。冻结同一模型进行隐藏动力学实验，在未告知的倾斜变化后表现出即时的信念适应，但随后出现不稳定。

Conclusion: 支持使用共享生成轨迹模型，在GenAIF框架内统一动作提议、受控预测与观测证据；持续推断仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: Learning from trajectory demonstrations offers a route to active-inference control of complex systems whose dynamics are difficult to model explicitly. We introduce generative active-inference control (GenAIF), in which one generative trajectory model learns from demonstrations and measured action interventions to supply a goal-conditioned policy distribution and a state-to-observation likelihood mapping. From this control design, we derive three model requirements: (i) useful action proposals, (ii) accurate prediction under imposed actions, and (iii) probabilistic observation evidence for belief updating and expected information gain. We benchmark diffusion, autoregressive Transformers, conditional variational autoencoders (CVAEs), and flow matching in a MuJoCo manipulation task with multiple physical conditions. Diffusion delivers the strongest control across the tested dynamics, while CVAE combines comparable short-horizon prediction with much faster inference. Correct conditioning is decisive, and trajectory reuse offers further computational savings. With the same frozen models, a hidden-dynamics experiment demonstrates prompt belief adaptation after an unannounced tilt change; subsequent instability identifies sustained inference as a remaining challenge. These findings support the use of shared generative trajectory models to connect action proposal, controlled prediction, and observation evidence within GenAIF.

</details>


### [313] [Virtual model control for compliant reaching under uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05695)
*Yi Zhang,Daniel Larby,Fumiya Iida,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究虚拟模型控制（VMC）框架在力控机械臂到达任务中的应用，提出六种虚拟模型设计方法，并在8自由度人形机器人上开展真实世界实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VMC过去主要用于腿足机器人运动控制，但其在复杂不确定环境中的通用潜力尚未被充分挖掘，本文希望将其推广至力控机械臂的到达任务。

Method: 提出六种不同的虚拟模型设计方法，用于在存在障碍和不确定性的环境中实现到达任务；采用力控8自由度人形机器人进行真实世界验证，并设计三类实验考察控制器的可预测性、对外部力的敏感性以及对已知和未知障碍的适应性。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法虽需在精度和轨迹最优性上做出牺牲，但能够在不确定性下以直观且可扩展的方式设计复杂的到达运动。

Conclusion: VMC框架可有效推广至力控机械臂的到达任务，在复杂不确定环境中提供直观、可扩展的控制器设计途径，尽管其代价是精度与轨迹最优性的降低。

Abstract: Virtual Model Control (VMC) is an approach to design a controller for force-controlled robots in complex uncertain environments. While this method was primarily investigated for legged robot locomotion in the past, it can be more generally applicable to other types of robotic systems. This paper investigates the VMC framework for reaching tasks in a force-controlled robotic arm. We propose six different approaches to designing virtual models in order to achieve reaching tasks in environments with obstacles and uncertainties. A force-controlled 8 degree-of-freedom humanoid robot was used to validate the proposed approach in the real world. We conducted three experiments to test the performance of VMC controllers in terms of predictability, sensitivity to external force, and adaptability against known and unknown obstacles. Experimental analyses show that, even though the proposed approach needs to sacrifice accuracy and trajectory optimality, it enables us to design complex reaching motions under uncertainties, in an intuitive and extendable manner.

</details>


### [314] [When to Switch: Reliable Action-Chunk Extension for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05719)
*Seonghoon Yu,Dongwon Kim,HyungRok Jung,Yoonjae Baek,Byung-kwan Lee,Suha Kwak,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RACE通过预测并条件化子技能切换时机，实现长动作块的可靠执行，减少机器人推理停顿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型推理开销大，机器人需在策略调用间歇停顿，导致停走式执行，破坏运动平滑性并延长任务时间。延长动作块可减少调用次数，但长块预测不可靠，需探究其误差来源。

Method: 分析长动作块内的动作误差，发现误差集中在操纵子技能切换处且随块长急剧增长，凸显切换时机的重要性。据此提出RACE框架，通过辅助的一步去噪过程预测切换时机，并将动作生成条件化于该时机。

Result: 仿真基准中，同块长下RACE优于微调；2倍长块时成功率超过最新SOTA及高效VLA，4倍长块时仍具竞争力。真机上使用4倍长块，停走空闲时间减少约5倍，成功率高于同块长微调。

Conclusion: 通过学习并条件化子技能切换时机，可降低切换处误差，实现更长时间动作块的可靠执行，兼顾效率与成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models serve as unified policies for robotic manipulation, yet their expensive inference forces robots to pause between policy calls, resulting in stop-and-go execution that interrupts smooth motion and prolongs task completion. Extending the action chunk reduces policy calls and hence these pauses, but predicting farther into the future makes long-chunk execution unreliable. To understand where this unreliability arises, we analyze action errors within long chunks and find that they concentrate around transitions between manipulation subskills, growing sharply with chunk length. This suggests the importance of transition timing, i.e., when to switch subskills within a chunk. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RACE (Reliable Action-Chunk Extension), a framework that predicts the transition timing from an auxiliary one-step denoising pass and conditions action generation on it. By learning and conditioning on transition timing, RACE reduces errors at subskill transitions and enables reliable execution of longer action chunks. Across simulation benchmarks, RACE outperforms fine-tuning at the same chunk length; with 2x longer chunks, it surpasses recent state-of-the-art and efficient VLAs in success rate, and with 4x longer chunks, it remains competitive. On a real robot, RACE uses 4x longer chunks, which reduces the idle time caused by stop-and-go execution by about 5x, while achieving a higher success rate than fine-tuning with the same chunk length. Code and a real-robot demo are available at https://github.com/Seonghoon-Yu/RACE-VLA

</details>


### [315] [End-to-End Safe Social Navigation via Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning and Probabilistic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05733)
*Tommaso Van Der Meer Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Alberto Vaglio,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JESSI：基于JAX的端到端RL社交导航框架，直接从原始LiDAR扫描映射到运动学可行的控制指令，通过Dirichlet参数化连续动作空间与确定性边界增强安全性，并用注意力感知模块提取概率性人体状态，实现可解释的社交感知决策，在仿真与真机部署中兼顾高成功率与优异社交行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主社交导航需同时平衡效率、物理安全与社交规范。现有强化学习方法虽有效，但往往依赖不切实际的假设，例如已知行人的位置与速度。因此需要一种不依赖此类先验信息、可直接从原始传感输入进行决策的方案。

Method: 提出JESSI——一种基于JAX的轻量级端到端RL框架：以原始LiDAR扫描为输入，直接输出运动学可行的控制指令；采用Dirichlet参数化的连续动作空间与确定性边界来提升安全性；集成基于注意力的感知模块，提取概率性人体状态以支撑可解释的社交感知决策；并采用多任务范式，将RL策略与监督式感知信号联合优化。

Result: 通过大量仿真实验以及在差速驱动机器人上的真实环境部署验证：以多任务方式联合优化RL策略与监督感知信号能够增强社交行为；与当前最先进基线相比，JESSI在高导航成功率与更优社交行为之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: JESSI表明，无需依赖人类位置与速度等先验假设，仅凭原始LiDAR输入即可实现安全且社交合规的端到端导航，且多任务联合优化是提升社交导航表现的有效途径。

Abstract: Autonomous social navigation requires balancing efficiency, physical safety, and social compliance. Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods provide a viable and effective solution but often rely on unrealistic assumptions, such as the knowledge of humans' position and velocity. In this paper, we introduce JESSI (JAX-based E2E Safe Social Interpretable navigation), a lightweight end-to-end RL framework that maps raw LiDAR scans directly to kinematically feasible control commands. JESSI enhances safety via Dirichlet-parameterized continuous action spaces and deterministic bounding, while an integrated attention-based perception module extracts probabilistic human states for interpretable, socially aware decision-making. Through extensive simulations and real-world deployment on a differential-drive robot, we demonstrate that jointly optimizing the RL policy with a supervised perception signal in a multi-task paradigm enhances social behavior. Ultimately, JESSI is able to balance high navigation success rates and superior social behaviors compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [316] [FreeSpeed: Training-Free Speed Control for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05734)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Qiheng Wang,Jinghan Yang,Junqiao Fan,Hao Wan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeSpeed：一种免训练模块，通过动作块方向不一致性信号实现机器人策略执行速度的在线灵活调节，在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下支持0.22x–2.53x变速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署需在线调节执行速度（提速应对时限、降速便于交互与安全），但模仿学习策略继承演示速度，测试时变速会引入不可恢复的分布外观测，降低任务成功率。

Method: 观察到动作块的方向不一致性可反映任务阶段的关键程度，据此提出FreeSpeed：对预训练策略输出的动作块按请求速率重采样，并以相邻动作的方向不一致性作为主要信号进行重缩放，自适应决定执行速度接近请求速度的程度。

Result: 在3类策略族、50个仿真任务上支持在线变速，保持各任务成功率的前提下实现0.22x–2.53x执行速率；4个真实操作任务平均成功率94.0%，与冻结策略的93.8%相当，执行速率达0.38x–1.97x。

Conclusion: 通过方向不一致性度量任务阶段关键性，可在保持成功率的同时实现免训练的灵活在线变速，为机器人策略实际部署提供有效支持。

Abstract: Online control of execution speed is essential for deploying robot policies in real-world scenarios, as robots may need to speed up under time constraints or slow down to facilitate human interaction and improve safety. However, imitation-learned policies inherit the execution speed of their demonstrations, and test-time speed modification can introduce unrecoverable out-of-distribution observations, reducing task success. We observe that the directional inconsistency of action chunks reflects task-phase criticality, indicating how aggressively action step lengths can be modified while preserving task success. Based on this observation, we introduce FreeSpeed, a training-free module that post-processes action chunks from pretrained policies. FreeSpeed resamples each predicted chunk at the requested rate, then uses directional inconsistency between adjacent actions as the primary signal for rescaling. This signal adaptively determines how closely the execution speed can approach the requested speed, allowing flexible speed adjustment within the evaluated limits without compromising task success. Across three policy families and 50 simulated tasks, FreeSpeed supports online speed changes, with realized execution rates spanning 0.22x to 2.53x among settings that preserve per-task success. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, FreeSpeed achieves an average success rate of 94.0%, matching the frozen policy's 93.8%, while realizing execution rates from 0.38x to 1.97x.

</details>


### [317] [The Unexpired Plan: A Free Monitor for Accelerated Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05747)
*Yi Zhao,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将免训练扩散策略加速的"监控器"重新定义为统计量与其触发响应的组合，并以闭环成功率而非特征距离为其定价；提出"永不遗忘"的吸收式响应配合低触发率、测量相对被替代策略偏差的统计量，在114种加速器配置和五类策略族上验证，可将单次调用算力降低1.55–3.09倍且守住成功率裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练加速器依赖内部相似度信号判断"捷径未改变结果"才接受复用，但监控设计普遍被当作单一整体，且以特征距离衡量其价值，忽视了监控的响应方式对闭环成功率的影响。作者认为监控应由"读取的统计量"与"触发后的动作"两个设计组成，并主张应以闭环成功而非特征相似度来为其定价。

Method: 在114种加速器配置与四类策略族上系统评测监控设计：对比"每次拒绝后重新武装"的已发表门控与"吸收式"响应；揭示再武装响应即使给oracle全部前向与精确局部动作误差也会损失20点成功率，而吸收同一统计量仅损失1点；提出以低触发率统计量（测量相对被替代策略的偏差，复用分块策略已付代价的上一份未过期计划）配合永不遗忘的响应。


Result: 在114种配置和四个策略族上，两个加速器各自通过自身门槛并将每次复用记为已认证，但在某一任务上一个完成全部episode、另一个一个都没完成；吸收式响应相对再武装响应在成功率上表现显著更好（1点 vs 20点损失）。按此方式守护每次调用可将单次算力降低1.55–3.09倍，而任何需要参考的检查在同等覆盖下将完全无法加速。四个族中有三个仅靠调度即已满足预设±2点裕度；监控的价值体现在第四族、四个未被选中的配置，以及在设计冻结后运行的接触丰富第五族上。

Conclusion: 监控器是"统计量+响应"的双重设计，其价值必须以闭环成功率计价。吸收式（永不遗忘）响应配合低触发率、测量相对被替代策略偏差的统计量，是既快又稳的免训练扩散加速方案；该方案在多数族上仅靠调度即达标，并在预期会失败的接触丰富任务上证明监控不可替代。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration of a diffusion policy is accepted when an internal similarity signal reports that the shortcut changed nothing.
