We price every monitor a control loop can afford in closed-loop success rather than feature distance, over $114$ accelerator configurations and four policy families: two accelerators each clear their own gate's bar and log every reuse as certified, while on one task one finishes every episode and the other none. A monitor is two designs, not one --- the statistic it reads, and what it does when that statistic fires. Published gates re-arm after every rejection, and under that response even an oracle handed every forward pass and the exact local action error loses twenty points; absorbing the same statistic costs one point, and most of its speed. What makes a response that never forgets affordable is a statistic that rarely fires, and what it must measure is deviation from the policy the accelerator replaced --- which a chunked policy has already paid for, its last plan not yet expired and free to read. Guarding every call this way cuts per-call compute by $1.55$--$3.09\times$, where any reference-requiring check at the same coverage would have to stop accelerating altogether. On three of our four families the schedule alone already holds the pre-stated $\pm2$-point margin. What the monitor is measurably worth shows in three places: on the fourth family, where it rescues the candidate selection landed on; on four configurations it did not select; and on a contact-rich fifth family, chosen where the schedule was expected to fail and run after every design choice was frozen, where no unmonitored arm at its speed holds the margin and the monitored one does.

</details>


### [318] [Demonstration-Calibrated Port-Hamiltonian Retuning for Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05755)
*Yulong Yang,Fan Wu,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Amit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHRetune 是一种离线方法，通过从示教中学习端口-哈密顿模型，为冻结策略推导下游阻抗控制器的增益，无需评估 rollout 或增益搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略与 VLA 操作策略通常通过下游阻抗控制器部署，其刚度与阻尼增益影响任务成功率，但常继承自数据采集而非针对所部署策略选择。经验性增益扫描虽可提升性能，却需要反复评估 rollout。

Method: 从示教中学习端口-哈密顿模型，估计策略预测动作对应的 effort 与 energy 预算；将策略应用于记录的示教观测，并把其预测与示教导出的 effort/energy 预算比较，据此以闭式解推导单一增益缩放系数，调整下游控制器同时保留策略及其动作表示。

Result: 在 LIBERO 各套件上，PHRetune 将 Diffusion Policy 成功率最高提升 9.4 个百分点，所推导增益取得经验增益扫描中的最高成功率。在四个真实操作任务上，PHRetuned Diffusion Policy 优于原始策略、其他增益调优方法及策略重训练基线。相同流程在 SmolVLA 与 OpenVLA-OFT 上均提升每个任务的成功率，并降低两种 VLA 主干下的加速度与 jerk。

Conclusion: PHRetune 可在评估前固定增益，无需先验机械臂模型、任务奖励、策略重训练或额外运行时计算，即可有效提升操作策略性能。

Abstract: Diffusion and VLA policies for manipulation are often deployed through downstream impedance controllers. The stiffness and damping gains of these controllers affect task success, yet are commonly inherited from data collection rather than selected for the deployed policy. Although empirical gain sweeps can improve performance, they require repeated evaluation rollouts. We introduce PHRetune, an offline method that derives controller gains for a frozen policy without evaluation rollouts or gain search. Our approach learns a port-Hamiltonian model from demonstrations to estimate the effort and energy associated with the policy's predicted actions. The policy is applied to recorded demonstration observations, and its predictions are assessed against demonstration-derived effort and energy budgets. From this comparison, we derive a single gain scale in closed form, adjusting the downstream controller while preserving the policy and its action representation. The gains are fixed before evaluation, without requiring a prior manipulator model, task rewards, policy retraining, or additional runtime computation. Across LIBERO suites, PHRetune improves Diffusion Policy success by up to 9.4 percentage points, with the derived gains achieving the highest observed success rates in empirical gain sweeps. On all four real-world manipulation tasks, PHRetuned Diffusion Policy outperforms the nominal policy, alternative gain-tuning methods, and a policy-retraining baseline. The same procedure improves success with SmolVLA and OpenVLA-OFT on every task, while reducing acceleration and jerk for both VLA backbones.

</details>


### [319] [Human-in-the-Loop Neuro-Symbolic Drift Anticipation for Reliable Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05757)
*Junhyun Nam,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid DeepSEE（HDS），一种人在回路（HITL）的神经符号框架，用于视觉SLAM中的主动漂移预测。通过以LLM作为推理桥梁，将定性人类上下文转化为可解释的符号约束，与神经漂移风险估计相结合，以提升V-SLAM的可靠性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动模型虽具预测能力，但其"黑箱"特性在分布外（OOD）环境中常产生物理上不一致的输出，因此需要提升漂移预测的可靠性与物理一致性。

Method: 提出HDS框架，集成神经漂移风险估计与符号约束推理，并利用大语言模型（LLM）作为推理桥梁，将定性的人类上下文转化为可解释的符号约束，形成人在回路的神经符号漂移预测架构。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据或量化结果，仅说明所提漂移预测框架在视觉SLAM中实现了更高的可靠性与一致性。

Conclusion: 神经符号与人在回路相结合的方法能够缓解数据驱动模型的黑箱问题，提升视觉SLAM中漂移预测的可靠性与一致性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Hybrid DeepSEE (HDS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) neuro-symbolic framework for proactive drift anticipation in Visual SLAM (V-SLAM). While data-driven models offer predictive power, their "black-box" nature often yields physically inconsistent outputs in out-of-distribution (OOD) environments. To address this, HDS integrates neural drift risk estimation with symbolic constraint reasoning. By utilizing a Large Language Model (LLM) as a reasoning bridge, the framework translates qualitative human context into interpretable symbolic constraints. Building upon this architecture, we propose a superior drift anticipation framework that ensures enhanced reliability and consistency in Visual SLAM

</details>


### [320] [Bilinear Flow Policy: Distributional Extrapolation for Goal-Conditioned Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05765)
*Wonsuhk Jung,Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha,Chen Xu,Abhishek Gupta,Masha Itkina,Shreyas Kousik,Haruki Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出双线性流策略（BFP），将转导检索与双线性条件流结合，实现对未见目标的分布外推，在仿真和真实任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件模仿学习（GCIL）结合流匹配虽能表示多模态行为并适应多样目标，但当目标超出演示数据支撑范围时往往失败。核心挑战是在外推到未见目标时，不破坏动作分布的多模态性，即"分布外推"问题。

Method: BFP给定未见"观测-目标"对后，检索一个"锚点"训练样本，并将其转导式重构为"熟悉锚点+残差项"。使用预训练视觉特征和一种新颖的学习式锚点选择算法，让残差紧凑编码当前观测-目标对与锚点的差异。随后用双线性流模型刻画锚点与残差如何共同决定多模态动作分布。理论上证明，在适当假设下，未见目标处的动作分布误差由分布内流匹配误差界定，最多相差问题相关因子。

Result: 在五个仿真操作任务上，BFP的分布外成功率是GCIL策略的2.63倍，是最强外推目标基线的1.36倍；在两个真实任务上，BFP比GCIL提升32%。理论还给出了可在部署前预测哪些策略外推效果好、以及能外推到哪些未见目标的实用诊断方法。

Conclusion: BFP通过转导检索与双线性流的结合，有效解决了GCIL在未见目标上的分布外推问题，兼顾多模态保持与外推能力，并提供理论保证及部署前的实用诊断工具。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned imitation learning (GCIL) with flow matching is a promising framework that can represent multimodal behaviors while adapting to diverse, user-specified goals, yet often fails when goals lie outside the demonstration support. To extrapolate to such unseen goals without collapsing multimodality - a problem we call distributional extrapolation - we introduce Bilinear Flow Policy (BFP), a generative visuomotor policy that combines transductive retrieval with a bilinear conditional flow. Given an unseen observation-goal pair, BFP retrieves an "anchor" training example and transductively reformulates the unseen pair as this familiar anchor plus a residual term. For this decomposition to guide action prediction, the residual must compactly encode how the current observation-goal pair differs from the anchor, and the anchor must be chosen so that this difference is predictive of the corresponding action distribution. BFP achieves this with pretrained visual features and a novel learned anchor-selection algorithm. The novel bilinear flow then models how the anchor and the residual jointly determine the multimodal action distribution. We prove that, for bilinear flow under suitable assumptions, action distribution error at unseen goals is bounded by the in-distribution flow-matching error up to problem-dependent factors. Across five manipulation tasks in simulation, BFP achieves 2.63x the out-of distribution success rate of a GCIL policy and 1.36x that of the strongest extrapolation-targeted baseline. On two real-world tasks, BFP improves over GCIL by 32%. Finally, our theory yields practical, pre deployment diagnostics for predicting which trained policies will extrapolate well and to which unseen goal.

</details>


### [321] [DASH: A da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid Robots as an Accessible Platform for Surgical Robotics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05792)
*Sara Wickenhiser,Junrong Zhou,Zekai Liang,Lizzie Peiros,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DASH适配器，将达芬奇手术器械适配到通用机器人上，无需改装器械即可实现微创手术遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术临床优势明显，但专用平台成本高、基础设施要求高，限制了农村和资源匮乏地区的使用。现有将通用机器人用于腹腔镜任务的研究依赖手持器械通过被动连杆连接，而非机器人原生驱动，限制了能力。

Method: 设计DASH（达芬奇串联臂与人形平台适配器），包含两种适配器，无需改装器械本身；每个适配器兼容多种机器人负载能力，可啮合器械原生锁扣，并无线识别插入的工具，从而加载器械专用运动学与耦合矩阵。

Result: 遥操作实验验证了DASH的有效性，并展示了其为手术机器人平台提供研究访问途径的益处。

Conclusion: DASH能够以低成本方式将达芬奇手术器械集成到通用机器人平台，促进手术机器人研究的可及性。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery offers well-documented clinical benefits, but the cost and infrastructure requirements of purpose-built platforms limit access in rural and lower-resourced facilities. Recent work has teleoperated general-purpose robots for laparoscopic tasks and in vivo procedures, but relied on handheld instruments coupled through passive linkages rather than native robotic actuation. Instead, we adapt da Vinci Classic and Xi instruments onto general-purpose robots that can integrate in clinical workflows. We present DASH, a da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid platforms, consisting of two types of adapters that require no modification to the instruments themselves. Each adapter is compatible with many robotic platform load capacities, engages the instrument's native latch, and wirelessly identifies inserted tools to load instrument-specific kinematics and coupling matrices. Teleoperated experiments demonstrate the efficacy of DASH and its benefits for enabling research access to surgical robotic platforms.

</details>


### [322] [Dynamics-Aware Adaptive Corridors with Feasibility-Perturbed Trust-Region SQP for Certified Nonholonomic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05796)
*Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向带倒挡的类车车辆的优化泊车规划器，其优化阶段每次迭代都精确满足离散动力学，并保证车辆矩形在采样点之间不与障碍物碰撞，通过凸走廊与信赖域SQP实现可行迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的泊车规划器仅在离散时间采样点施加碰撞约束，可能导致车辆角点在采样点之间穿过障碍物；此外在求解器收敛前无法得到可执行轨迹。

Method: 为每个时间区间构建一条凸走廊，包含两端所有车辆角点，并按扫掠边界收缩以约束角点路径偏离弦的范围；当初始猜测发生碰撞时用分离半平面给出离开障碍物的方向，随后从当前迭代的速度、曲率和步长生成航向对齐的盒子并在接受步后重建；采用可行性扰动的信赖域序列二次规划，通过反馈将每步投影到动力学上并精确验证。

Result: 在820个基准案例中成功818个且无穿透（797个从初始猜测直接成功），7103次优化迭代均可用；当99%初始猜测与障碍物相交时成功率为96.5%；机动时间中位数仅比同等认证的精确碰撞基线长0.7%。

Conclusion: 该方法保证规划模型下迭代可行且无碰撞，成本单调下降，走廊稳定后极限点为KKT点或违反约束资格；但保证仅针对规划模型，不适用于物理车辆。

Abstract: Optimisation-based parking planners usually impose collision constraints only at the time samples, so a vehicle corner can cut an obstacle between samples, and no executable trajectory exists until the solver converges. We present a planner for car-like vehicles with reverse gear in which every iterate of the optimisation phase satisfies the discretised dynamics exactly and keeps the whole vehicle rectangle clear of obstacles between the samples. Each time interval receives one convex corridor that holds all vehicle corners at both ends and is shrunk by a sweep margin bounding how far the corner paths leave their chords. Separating half-planes give the corridors a direction out of obstacles when the initial guess is in collision; later, heading-aligned boxes are grown from the speed, curvature and step of the current iterate and rebuilt after accepted steps. A feasibility-perturbed trust-region sequential quadratic programming method projects each step onto the dynamics by feedback and verifies it exactly; the cost decreases monotonically, and once the corridors stop changing, limit points are Karush-Kuhn-Tucker points of the corridor-constrained problem or violate a constraint qualification. On 820 benchmark cases the planner succeeds in 818 without penetration (797 from the first initial guess), none of the 7103 evaluated optimisation-phase iterates is unusable, and it succeeds in 96.5% of the cases when 99% of the initial guesses intersect an obstacle. Its maneuvers take 0.7% longer in the median than those of a similarly certified exact-collision baseline. The guarantees hold for the planning model, not for a physical vehicle.

</details>


### [323] [What the Guard Misses, the Robot Executes: Implied Harm in VLA Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05818)
*Sripad Karne,Arjun Balaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）无法拒绝指令，需依赖监控器筛查有害请求。实验发现：文本守卫能拦截直白的有害请求，却漏掉大量隐含意图的请求（高达95%的隐含有害任务在无警报下完成），监控模型激活也无法弥补该缺口，线性探针在机器人训练后区分能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型会被动执行给定指令而无法拒绝，因此有害请求的筛查责任落在外部监控器上。作者想检验这些监控器能否识别"任务本身普通、但出于有害动机"的请求，并探究意图表述的明确程度对监控效果的影响。

Method: 保持任务固定，仅改变有害意图表述的明确程度，测试π0.5模型在不同明确程度下的完成率；评估文本守卫的拦截率并进行机器人指令重校准；同时监测模型激活，并使用线性探针在基础语言模型、视觉-语言模型以及机器人训练后的模型中区分有害与无害指令。

Result: π0.5在所有明确程度上都完成了任务，完成率与无害对照相当。文本守卫几乎能标记所有直白请求，但很少标记隐含请求：高达95%的隐含有害运行在无警报下完成任务，重校准后仍高达90%。监控模型激活未能缩小这一差距。线性探针在基础语言和视觉-语言模型中几乎能完美区分有害与无害指令，但机器人训练后区分能力减弱，隐含有害情形尤为明显。

Conclusion: 现有的文本守卫与激活监控对隐含有害意图的VLA请求存在严重筛查缺口，机器人训练还会削弱模型内部的有害/无害表征可分离性。这表明需要针对隐含意图设计更鲁棒的监控机制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) act on instructions without being able to refuse, so screening harmful requests falls to monitors. We test whether these monitors catch ordinary robot tasks requested for harmful reasons, holding the task fixed while varying only how explicitly the intent is stated. $π_{0.5}$ completes the task at every level of explicitness, as often as for harmless controls. Text guards flag nearly every blunt request but few implied ones: up to 95% of implied-harm runs end with the task done and no flag raised, and up to 90% even after recalibrating on robot instructions. Monitoring the model's activations does not close this gap. Linear probes separate harmful from harmless instructions almost perfectly in the base language and vision-language models, but this separation weakens after robot training in two model families, most for implied harm.

</details>


### [324] [Transporting Unsecured Stacked Payloads with a Quadrupedal Robot via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05819)
*Nobuo Namura,Masayuki Hiromoto,Kento Uemura,Hironobu Sasaki,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Payload-Adaptive Multi-Objective Reinforcement learning for Transportation (PAMORT)，实现四足机器人运输未固定堆叠箱体，通过在线自适应权衡运动性能与载荷稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人在崎岖地形运输未固定载荷时，需要在运动性能与载荷稳定性之间取得平衡；激进运动可能导致载荷失稳。且无专用载荷传感器或主动承载机构，需要仅凭本体感知实现自适应。

Method: PAMORT：先训练一个以偏好向量为条件的多目标基础策略（偏好向量加权运动与载荷稳定性奖励组），再在冻结策略上训练权重调节器，从本体感知在线调整偏好。

Result: 仿真中，在不同载荷配置（含未见过的三箱堆叠，尽管仅用两箱训练）下，整体运输成功率与单目标基线相当或更优。Unitree Go2真机实验实现零样本迁移到训练难度及以上的斜坡和台阶，八个任务平均成功率：PAMORT 0.850，基线 0.675。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可仅凭本体感知信息在线自适应运动—载荷权衡，实现稳健的未固定载荷运输。

Abstract: Transporting unsecured payloads with legged robots over uneven terrain requires balancing locomotion performance and payload stability, since aggressive motion can destabilize the payload even when the robot remains stable. We study quadrupedal transportation of unsecured stacked boxes on an edgeless torso-mounted board without dedicated payload sensors or active carrier mechanisms. To address this trade-off, we propose Payload-Adaptive Multi-Objective Reinforcement learning for Transportation (PAMORT). PAMORT trains a multi-objective base policy conditioned on a preference vector that weights locomotion and payload-stability reward groups, then trains a weight adjuster on the frozen policy to adapt this preference online from proprioception. In simulation, PAMORT achieves comparable or better overall transportation success than a corresponding single-objective baseline across different payload configurations, including an unseen three-box stack, despite training only with two boxes. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 demonstrate zero-shot transfer to slopes and steps at or beyond the training difficulty, with mean success rates of 0.850 for PAMORT and 0.675 for the baseline across eight tasks. These results demonstrate robust unsecured-payload transportation with online adaptation of the locomotion--payload trade-off from proprioceptive information.

</details>


### [325] [Dual-Rate Force-Image Control with Model-Based Orientation Limits for Robotic Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05839)
*Tyler Foster,Qiang Zhang,A B M Tahidul Haque,Anh Thu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对机器人超声中快速力控环与慢速延迟图像反馈的双速率耦合问题，推导出闭式旋转速率上限以约束旋转引起的力偏差，并在明胶体模上验证了该速率设计规则的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声中高频率接触力环与慢速、带延迟的图像反馈耦合，导致图像引导的探头旋转会在图像响应被观察到之前就扰动接触力。因此需要建立旋转速率与力偏差之间的约束关系，以保证图像引导下的接触力安全。

Method: 推导了一个闭式定向速率极限，用于在有限时间窗口内约束建模中由旋转引起的估计力偏移，同时考虑快速力环的扰动抑制。该极限依赖局部接触刚度、力环增益、保守的旋转-力增益界、偏移预算及预测时域。将模型实现于具有基于时间戳的延迟重建和基于关节力矩的力估计的双速率控制器，并在弯曲明胶体模上通过配对控制器比较与组件消融进行评估。

Result: 相对于无约束图像引导，所提出的速率受限控制器将第一秒的RMS估计力误差降低0.40 N，但线索收敛时间增加0.94 s。接近解析预测上限的固定速率上限在力误差上无显著差异，且收敛快0.32 s。延迟重建在测试延迟下无显著影响。单受试者腘动脉扫描证明可行性，尽管在异质组织上图像线索受噪声限制。

Conclusion: 该模型的主要实用价值在于速率设计规则而非在线预测；采用解析预测上限附近的固定速率上限即可在几乎不损失力精度的情况下获得更快的收敛，验证了该速率约束框架的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound couples a high-rate contact-force loop with slower, delayed image feedback, so image-guided ultrasound probe rotation can perturb contact force before the resulting image response is observed. We derive a closed-form orientation-rate limit that bounds the modeled rotation-induced estimated-force excursion over a finite horizon while accounting for disturbance rejection by the fast force loop. The limit depends on local contact stiffness, force-loop gains, a conservative rotation-to-force gain bound, the excursion budget, and the prediction horizon. We implement this model in a dual-rate controller with timestamp-based delay reconstruction and joint-torque-based force estimation, and evaluate it on a curved gelatin phantom using paired controller comparisons and component ablations. Relative to unconstrained image guidance, the proposed rate-limited controller reduced first-second root-mean-square (RMS) estimated-force error by 0.40 N while increasing cue-convergence time by 0.94 s. A fixed rate cap near the analytically predicted ceiling produced no resolvable difference in force error and converged 0.32 s faster, indicating that the principal practical value of the model is the rate-design rule rather than online prediction. Delay reconstruction had no resolvable effect at the tested latency. A single-subject popliteal scan demonstrated feasibility, although the image cue was noise-limited on heterogeneous tissue.

</details>


### [326] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Collision-Free Locomotion of an Underactuated Biped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05855)
*Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Saurabh Kumar,Surya Prakash S. K.,Samiran Datta,Abhay Dwivedi,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习框架，让欠驱动双足机器人在不破坏平衡的前提下实现避障导航，成功率显著优于经典规划器混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人避障时会扰乱自身平衡，这种耦合在欠驱动平台上尤为严重（每条腿仅四个驱动关节，无髋或踝侧摆），传统方法难以兼顾导航与平衡。

Method: 采用分层强化学习（HRL）：高层策略输入机器人姿态、36 条射线邻近测量、移动障碍物状态与滚动时域局部目标，每 10 个控制步输出机体速度指令 (v_x, v_y, ω_yaw)；低层速度条件策略通过 PD 控制关节目标跟踪该指令。两层用 SAC 联合训练；收敛后的步态与任务无关，故冻结后由经典规划器（A*、RRT*、APF）在同一指令接口驱动，形成三个对照基线。

Result: 在随机化 PyBullet 环境中每种方法各 100 次评估：所提方法静态场景成功率 98.0%、动态场景 88.0%，而规划器混合基线最高仅 78.0% 与 68.0%；路径长度与 A* 参考相差 4% 以内；消融实验表明每个观测通道与奖励项均有实质贡献。

Conclusion: 分层强化学习可有效解耦欠驱动双足机器人的平衡与避障，冻结的通用步态策略配合统一速度接口兼具性能与可对比性。

Abstract: A bipedal robot cannot deviate from its path to avoid an obstacle without disturbing its balance, and this coupling is most severe on underactuated platforms such as the biped considered here, which has four actuated joints per leg and no hip or ankle roll. This paper presents a Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) framework in which a High-Level (HL) policy observes the robot pose, 36 raycast proximity measurements, moving-obstacle states, and a receding-horizon local goal, and outputs a body-velocity command $(v_x, v_y, ω_{yaw})$ every ten control steps, while a velocity-conditioned Low-Level (LL) policy tracks each command through PD-controlled joint targets. Both policies are trained jointly with Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Because the converged gait is task-agnostic, it is frozen and driven by classical planners over the same command interface, yielding three controlled baselines: SAC+A*, SAC+RRT*, and SAC+APF. Across 100 evaluation trials per method in randomized PyBullet environments, the proposed method reaches the goal in 98.0% of static and 88.0% of dynamic trials, against at most 78.0% and 68.0% for the planner hybrids, with path lengths within 4% of the A* reference, and ablations confirm that each observation channel and reward term contributes materially to this performance.

</details>


### [327] [Mulligan: Performance-Guided Data Collection for Efficient On-Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05882)
*Lars Ankile,Perry Dong,Rohan Bhowmik,Aneesh Muppidi,David D. Yuan,Shuran Song,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mulligan通过将初始状态分布作为决策变量，在固定监督部署预算下用失败和未尝试状态启动每轮采集，并结合价值函数提升高精度操作任务的数据效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类示范学习是教机器人新任务的可靠方式，但随着策略改进，每次额外示范的收益递减。持续改进可来自监督部署：操作员放置物体并在策略失败时介入。核心问题是在对象放置范围广、精度要求高的任务中，如何用固定的监督回合预算最大化改进。观察到失败常集中于少量初始状态，均匀采集会浪费操作员时间在策略已掌握的状上。

Method: 将初始状态分布设为决策变量，每轮回合从观察到的失败状态和未尝试状态开始；为进一步提升数据效率，在交互式模仿学习中引入一个价值函数，利用包括模仿学习所丢弃的失败数据在内的全部数据进行训练。将该思路与基于价值的动作选择结合，形成HiL-IDQL+Mulligan。

Result: 在三个真实任务（2,550个留出盲测回合）和两个模拟任务上，Mulligan在相同采集预算下优于均匀初始状态采样；结合基于价值的动作选择后，HiL-IDQL+Mulligan将真实任务最终成功率提升10至34个百分点。在有操作员介入的情况下，人机团队完成98%的采集回合，在策略学习过程中保持高效生产。

Conclusion: 将初始状态分布作为主动决策、并利用包括失败在内的全部数据训练价值函数，能显著提高监督部署中固定预算下的学习效率与最终任务成功率，相关视频、代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Learning from human demonstrations is a reliable way to teach robots new tasks, but the gains from each additional demonstration shrink as the policy improves. Continued improvement can instead come from supervised deployment, where an operator places the objects and intervenes when the policy fails. We ask how to maximize improvement from a fixed budget of supervised episodes on high-precision manipulation tasks with wide ranges of object placements. We observe that failures can concentrate in a small subset of initial states, so uniform collection spends much of the operator's time on states the policy already handles. Mulligan makes the initial-state distribution a decision, starting each round's episodes at observed failures and untried states. To further improve data efficiency, we augment interactive imitation learning with a value function trained on all data, including failures that imitation discards. Across three real-world tasks evaluated on 2,550 held-out, blinded episodes and two simulated tasks, Mulligan outperforms uniform initial-state sampling at matched collection budgets, and combined with value-based action selection, HiL-IDQL+Mulligan, improves final real-task success by 10-34 percentage points. With operator interventions, the human-robot team completes 98% of collection episodes, remaining productive while the policy learns. Videos, code, and data are available at https://mulligan.page/.

</details>


### [328] [From Social Reasoning to Embodied Interaction: An Agentic Framework for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05964)
*Ziyu Cheng,Yuewen Guo,Zhirui Liu,Dong Zhang,Haotao Lu,Jingyi Yu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARISE 是一个在 Sophia 人形机器人上统一智能体推理与社交具身表达的框架，实现多模态理解、长期记忆与主动/被动交互，并将社交意图转化为与语音、面部表情协调的机器人原生手势，评测显示交互质量高、表达协调且延迟显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人机面对面交互要求机器人理解动态社交情境、判断何时参与，并通过协调的物理行为表达意图。然而现有方法难以闭环：基于基础模型的智能体虽具备多模态推理与记忆能力，却大多缺乏实体；表现力强的虚拟智能体不受真实机器人物理约束；实体社交机器人则往往只部分解决社交推理或具身表达问题。

Method: 提出 ARISE 统一框架，在 Sophia 人形机器人上连接智能体推理（Agentic Reasoning）与交互式社交具身（Interactive Social Embodiment）。框架整合多模态上下文理解、长期记忆以及被动与主动交互机制，以决定何时交流、交流什么，并通过流式执行将社交意图转化为与语音和机械面部表情协调的机器人原生手势。

Result: 在 Sophia 上的大量评测表明：交互质量感知良好，具身行为富有表现力且协调性好，流式执行带来了显著的延迟降低。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了联合推理"交流什么、何时参与、如何物理表达社交意图"对于实现与人形机器人自然交互的重要性。

Abstract: Natural face-to-face human--robot interaction requires a robot to understand an evolving social situation, decide when to engage, and express its intent through coordinated physical behavior. Yet existing approaches rarely close this loop: foundation-model agents provide increasingly capable multimodal reasoning and memory but remain largely disembodied, while expressive virtual agents do not face the physical constraints of real robots, and physical social robots typically address social reasoning and embodied expression only partially. We present ARISE, a unified framework that bridges Agentic Reasoning and Interactive Social Embodiment on the Sophia humanoid robot. ARISE integrates multimodal context understanding, long-term memory, and reactive and proactive interaction to determine when and what to communicate, and translates social intent into robot-native gestures coordinated with speech and mechanical facial expressions through streaming execution. Extensive evaluations on Sophia demonstrate strong perceived interaction quality, expressive and well-coordinated embodied behavior, and substantial latency reductions through streaming execution. These results highlight the importance of jointly reasoning about what to communicate, when to engage, and how to physically express social intent for natural interaction with humanoid robots. Project Page: https://robosocial.github.io/

</details>


### [329] [A State Based Dispatch Controller for Hospital Delivery Robots with Shared Human and Infrastructure Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05971)
*Krzysztof Siwek,Aleksandra Świetlicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出位置感知调度模型，联动机器人准入、人工支持与共享电梯、充电、清洁，基于33079条医院请求复现，发现电梯容量等设施假设影响大于调度策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人配送研究常因忽略取货行程与人工支持而高估运输能力，需在真实约束下评估调度流程。

Method: 构建耦合机器人准入与运输支持、共享电梯、充电和清洁的位置感知调度模型，重放33079条医院请求；用两组完整网格，对人工调度、资源感知控制器和工作量-邻近基准进行每场景30次配对重复比较，并探索更简化的截止时间准入规则。

Result: 取货行程加重共享电梯需求；高人手情形下，将假定电梯容量由2提高到4使纯人工延迟从55.11%降至3.18%，超过调度策略间差异；直接政策比较与机器人覆盖度可区分准入选择性与系统服务。

Conclusion: 该分析解释了机器人可通过截止时间准入测试却因人工交接与共享设施延误服务；贡献在于可复现地评估耦合调度流程，区分准入、完成与恢复可用性，结果为条件性比较而非医院收益或释放临床能力的估计。

Abstract: Robot delivery studies can overstate transport capacity when travel to pickups and human support fall outside the modeled schedule. We formulate a location aware dispatch model that couples robot admission to transporter support and shared elevators, charging, and cleaning. The model replays 33,079 observed hospital requests; missing contents, deadlines, staffing, and completion times remain explicit scenario assumptions. Two full grids compare human dispatch, a resource aware controller, and a proximity and workload benchmark in 30 paired replications per scenario. An exploratory extension tests a simpler deadline admission rule under the same operating model. Pickup travel increases demand on a pooled elevator bank. In one high staffing case, raising assumed elevator capacity from two to four reduces human only lateness from 55.11\% to 3.18\%, exceeding the dispatch differences. Direct policy comparisons and robot coverage distinguish admission selectivity from system service. The analysis explains why a robot can pass a deadline admission test yet delay service through staff handoffs and shared facilities. Its contribution is a reproducible evaluation of coupled dispatch workflows, with a clear separation between admission, completion, and return to availability. The results are conditional comparisons, not estimates of hospital benefit or released clinical capacity.

</details>


### [330] [How (and How Not) to Use Data Augmentation in VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05994)
*Bram Grooten,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图像增强对VLA模型强化学习后训练的影响，发现仅在critic模块上施加增强、保持actor输入干净，能显著提升视觉分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实机器人任务中表现良好，但在大幅视觉分布外偏移下泛化能力不足。用RL后训练可提升鲁棒性，但仍有较大改进空间，作者因此探索图像增强的作用。

Method: 系统研究图像增强在VLA的RL后训练中的效果，针对π0.5与GR00T N1.5模型，在LIBERO-Plus任务上进行实验，对比仅增强critic模块与同时增强actor输入、不同增强类型与强度的影响。

Result: 仅在critic上增强时，π0.5与GR00T N1.5在LIBERO-Plus上的分布外成功率分别提升7.8和10.0个百分点；而增强actor会导致训练完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 图像增强应仅作用于critic模块，actor在rollout和更新时需保持干净输入，并给出了改善VLA后训练泛化性的实用建议。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models currently demonstrate strong performance in a wide range of real-world robotics tasks. However, they often still lack the generalization ability to handle large visual out-of-distribution shifts. Post-training of VLAs with reinforcement learning (RL) has been shown to benefit robustness, but significant room for improvement remains. In this work, we systematically study the effect of image augmentation on VLA post-training. We find that it is crucial to augment only the critic module during RL updates, while leaving the actor's input clean during both rollouts and updates. For $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 this raises out-of-distribution success on LIBERO-Plus by $7.8$ and $10.0$ points respectively, while augmenting the actor collapses training entirely. We investigate a range of augmentation types and strengths, and provide practical recommendations for improving generalization in VLA post-training.

</details>


### [331] [Infant simulator with an embodied caregiver: Generating infant-perspective touch and vision during social interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05997)
*Noemi Vaculinova,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出MIMo的看护者扩展模型，可在MuJoCo中重放二元互动并生成婴儿第一人称多模态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展机器人仿真通常将婴儿孤立建模，忽视了看护者-婴儿二元互动中身体接触与面对面交流对感觉运动发展的塑造作用。

Method: 在Multi-Modal Infant Model (MIMo)基础上扩展：构建与MIMo形态兼容的关节化看护者模型（依人体测量学参数化，可选缩放至真实看护者）；提供重放自然主义看护-婴儿抱持与安抚互动的工作流；记录并可视化婴儿第一人称多模态体验；引入来源感知接触日志，区分自我、看护者与环境接触。

Result: 以触觉为例展示工具效果：来源感知接触日志可聚合为与人工编码可比的接触率统计；同时可视化触觉与自我中心视觉。

Conclusion: 该平台可作为多模态二元数据集（触觉与自我中心视觉）生成器，用于建模社会互动的发展。

Abstract: Early development unfolds in caregiver-infant dyads, where infants' sensorimotor streams are shaped by physical contact and face-to-face interaction. Yet developmental robotics simulators commonly model infants in isolation, limiting the study of caregiver-mediated experience. We present a caregiver-enabled extension of the Multi-Modal Infant Model (MIMo) in MuJoCo that turns MIMo into a controllable platform for replaying dyadic interaction and generating dense infant-perspective observations. The system provides (i) an articulated caregiver model compatible with MIMo morphologies, parameterized from anthropometrics and optionally resized to a recorded caregiver; (ii) a workflow to replay naturalistic caregiver-infant holding and soothing interactions; and (iii) logging and visualizing the infant's first-person multimodal experience (we show touch and vision). We showcase the tool on touch by introducing origin-aware contact logging that disambiguates self-, caregiver-, and environment-generated contact and supports aggregation into touch-rate statistics comparable to manual coding. While we showcase tactile analysis, the platform is intended more broadly as a generator of multimodal dyadic datasets (touch and egocentric vision) for modeling the development of social interaction.

</details>


### [332] [P3: Persistent Particle Planning for Constrained Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06002)
*Hikmet Simsir,Mahyar Fardinfar,Ozgur S. Oguz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P3将扩散模型去噪与重规划建模为一个Feynman-Kac粒子系统，通过跨重规划步维护加权候选方案种群，在不重训练的前提下提升测试时约束满足与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能提供轨迹先验，但在测试时约束下需要连续控制决策间保持可行性与一致性，逐次从头重采样代价高且易导致路线切换。

Method: 提出Persistent Particle Planning (P3)，一种序贯蒙特卡洛框架：每个控制步对候选轨迹进行平移与部分重加噪，在最新观测下精化，并用约束感知的加权与重采样选择延续方案；将去噪与重规划视为统一的Feynman-Kac粒子系统并做理想化修复分析。

Result: 理论上证明重加噪深度控制保留方案沿原路线的可靠性，且保留稀有且分离良好的路线所需方案数远少于从头采样发现；实验中种群复用减少路线切换、在无约束违反下提升成功率，每步重规划所需去噪迭代更少；迷宫导航任务上比重新生成种群及单样本约束优化方法更快。

Conclusion: 通过复用并精化先前方案而非重绘，P3在测试时约束扩散控制中兼顾可行性、一致性与规划速度，且无需微调扩散模型。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive priors over trajectories, but adapting these priors to test-time constraints requires maintaining feasibility and consistency across successive control decisions. We introduce Persistent Particle Planning (P3), a sequential Monte Carlo framework for diffusion control that maintains a weighted population of candidate plans across replanning steps. At each control step, P3 shifts and partially re-noises the candidate trajectories, refines them under the latest observation, and uses constraint-aware weighting and resampling to select among alternative continuations without retraining the diffusion model. We consider denoising and replanning as one Feynman--Kac particle system and analyze it under an idealized repair. We prove that the re-noising depth controls how reliably a kept plan stays on its route, and that keeping a rare, well-separated route takes far fewer plans than rediscovering it by sampling from scratch. Experiments under multiple test-time constraint configurations show that population reuse reduces route switching and improves success without constraint violations. Because P3 refines earlier plans instead of redrawing them, it also needs fewer denoising iterations per replan. On maze-navigation tasks, it plans faster than both regenerated populations and methods that correct a single sampled plan by constrained optimization. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/p3-username/p3-anon.

</details>


### [333] [Recon2Servo: Robotic Ultrasound Visual Servoing via Learned Image-to-Motion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06010)
*Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Dianye Huang,Yizhao Qian,Zhongyu Chen,Xiangyu Chu,K. W. Samuel Au,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Recon2Servo框架，直接从B模式超声图像学习图像到运动的推断，实现6自由度探头视觉伺服控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人超声中，从2D B模式图像进行6自由度探头控制很困难，因为平面外运动线索有限且模糊。现有方法依赖解剖先验或手工视觉特征，跨成像目标泛化能力差。受无跟踪器3D超声重建启发，希望直接学习图像到运动的映射。

Method: 采用带低秩适配的DINOv3编码器和双向关系模块，估计当前图像与目标图像之间的相对探头姿态，引导迭代闭环目标视图对齐。框架结合监督相对姿态学习、重建引导的闭环自适应以及有界残差姿态校正。

Result: 在公开数据集和12名健康志愿者（使用不同超声系统）的内部数据集上评估，证明其在重建体积伺服中的有效性。补充视频展示了人前臂目标视图对齐和动态跟踪的真实机器人演示。

Conclusion: Recon2Servo能有效从B模式图像学习6自由度运动推断，实现跨目标泛化的超声视觉伺服。

Abstract: Ultrasound visual servoing is essential for autonomous robotic ultrasound, yet 6-DoF probe control from 2D B-mode images remains challenging due to limited and ambiguous out-of-plane motion cues. Existing methods typically rely on anatomical priors or handcrafted visual features, limiting their generalizability across imaging targets. Inspired by trackerless 3D ultrasound reconstruction, we propose Recon2Servo, a visual servoing framework that learns image-to-motion inference directly from B-mode images for 6-DoF probe control. A DINOv3 encoder with low-rank adaptation and a bidirectional relation module estimate the relative probe pose between current and target images to guide iterative closed-loop target-view alignment. The framework combines supervised relative-pose learning, reconstruction-guided closed-loop adaptation, and bounded residual pose correction to improve motion inference during servoing. Evaluations on a public dataset and an in-house dataset collected from 12 healthy volunteers using different ultrasound systems demonstrate its effectiveness in reconstructed-volume servoing. Additional real-robot demonstrations of target-view alignment and dynamic tracking on a human forearm are provided in the supplementary video: https://youtu.be/qwsOdI-GMYk.

</details>


### [334] [MagServo: Uncertainty-Resilient Hierarchical Magnetic Servoing via Learned Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06046)
*Yuhan Tan,Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Yao Zhong,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MagServo，一个基于分层学习的6自由度磁伺服控制框架，直接利用学习到的潜在磁特征进行鲁棒控制，无需解析磁模型或显式雅可比监督。实验表明其达到亚毫米与亚度级精度，平均终端误差0.386 mm和0.479度，并在轨迹跟踪和未见磁源配置下表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁导航能为机器人系统提供非接触且不依赖视线的反馈，但现有方法通常依赖显式位姿估计或直接使用原始磁测量，导致控制精度易受建模误差、测量噪声和扰动影响。

Method: MagServo通过掩码重建学习具有抗不确定性能力的磁表征，并捕捉机器人运动与潜在磁变化之间的状态相关交互动力学，不依赖解析磁模型或显式雅可比监督。基于学习到的动力学，采用分层控制器：非线性模型预测控制用于粗略接近，局部雅可比反演用于精细调节。

Result: 大量物理实验证明其达到亚毫米和亚度级精度，6自由度位姿到达的平均终端误差为0.386 mm和0.479度。在复杂轨迹跟踪中优于基于定位的控制基线，并在未见磁源配置下无需重新训练仍保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: MagServo验证了基于潜在磁特征的分层学习框架在6自由度磁伺服中的有效性，实现了高精度与强鲁棒性，并可泛化到未见磁源配置。

Abstract: Magnetic navigation provides contact-free and line-of-sight-independent feedback for robotic systems, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit pose estimation or direct use of raw magnetic measurements, making accurate control susceptible to modeling errors, measurement noise, and disturbances. This work presents MagServo, a hierarchical learning-based framework for robust 6-DoF magnetic servoing directly using the learned latent magnetic feature. MagServo learns uncertainty-resilient magnetic representations through masked reconstruction and captures state-dependent interaction dynamics between robot motion and latent magnetic transitions without analytical magnetic models or explicit Jacobian supervision. Based on the learned dynamics, a hierarchical controller combines nonlinear model predictive control for coarse approach with local Jacobian inversion for precise fine regulation. Extensive physical experiments demonstrate submillimeter and subdegree accuracy, achieving mean terminal errors of 0.386 mm and 0.479 degree for 6-DoF pose reaching. MagServo further outperforms a localization-based control baseline in complex trajectory tracking and maintains robust performance under unseen magnetic-source configurations without retraining. A supplementary video of the real-robot experiments is available at https://youtu.be/rZt1NUP1Mr0.

</details>


### [335] [Do VLAs Understand and Adapt to the Objects They Handle, or Simply Replay Learned Behaviors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06078)
*Xinnuo Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探究VLA模型的泛化究竟源于对物体物理属性的理解，还是仅仅在复现已学会的动作。研究发现VLA对物理属性的编码较弱，且不能可靠地据此调整动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在区分VLA的"真实泛化"（基于对物体物理属性的全局理解来适应新场景）与"偶然鲁棒性"（复现已学会的、恰好在新场景中成功的动作）。核心问题是：VLA泛化是否真的建立在对物理属性理解的基础上。

Method: 第一，对七个VLA模型应用线性探测和表征相似性分析（RSA），考察其激活中对物理属性的编码情况；第二，在受控的LIBERO案例中，通过语言或视觉信号增加域内物体的质量，观察VLA是否据此调整动作。

Result: 在几乎所有模态中，物理属性（质量、易碎性、可变形性、摩擦、尺寸）的可解码性都低于非物理属性（语义类别、材质、声音、价格）。与基座VLM相比，机器人预训练削弱了语言流中物理属性的线性编码；预训练和微调都未增强物理属性差异与激活距离的对齐。在LIBERO案例中，多数VLA对更重和原始质量物体采用相似的抬升行为，导致任务成功率下降；少数例外是响应词汇或视觉线索而非质量本身。

Conclusion: VLA对物理属性的编码较弱，且不能可靠地利用这些信息来调整动作。其泛化更多体现为偶然鲁棒性，而非基于物理属性理解的真实泛化。

Abstract: This paper asks whether VLA generalization is grounded in a global understanding of objects' physical properties that enables policies to adapt their motion to unseen setups, or if policies simply replay the motions they've learnt that happen to succeed in new setups. The former reflects genuine generalization; the latter reflects incidental robustness. We first examine awareness of physical properties in seven VLAs by applying linear probing and representational similarity analysis (RSA) to their activations. We find that physical properties, including mass, fragility, deformability, friction and size are less decodable than non-physical properties such as semantic category, material, sound and price in nearly every modality stream. Compared with their base VLMs, robot pre-training weakens the linear encoding of physical properties in the language stream. Neither pre-training nor downstream fine-tuning strengthens the alignment between physical-property differences and activation distances. We then ask whether the weak physical information present in these activations shapes the actions a VLA generates. In a controlled LIBERO case study, we increase the mass of an in-domain object and signal the change through language or vision. Most VLAs use similar lifting behaviour for the heavier and original-mass objects, leading to task success declines. The few exceptions change their behaviour in response to lexical or visual cues rather than to mass itself. These results suggest that VLAs encode physical properties weakly and do not reliably use them to adapt their motion.

</details>


### [336] [Adaptive Mean Flow for Responsive Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06089)
*Aksel Vaaler,Marco Job,Christian Holden,Olav Egeland*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应平均流(AMF)，一种基于流的模仿学习方法，实现平滑、响应式的全闭环机器人控制，通过加速采样降低延迟并利用含噪历史轨迹保证预测一致性与平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流式机器人策略性能高且能建模多模态连续分布，但其迭代去噪过程带来显著预测延迟，阻碍高频闭环控制并导致抖动。常见做法是预测动作块顺序执行而不反馈，但这降低响应性且可能不使用最新状态信息。

Method: AMF采用Mean Flow(Flow Matching的加速形式)以最小化预测延迟；在预测新动作时使用上一步轨迹的加噪版本，且信噪比随轨迹时间参数增加，从而抑制相邻步预测的大幅变化，同时保留对未来步的适应自由度。

Result: 在多种仿真与真实机器人任务上评估AMF，相比基线方法取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: AMF能实现平滑且响应式的全闭环机器人控制，在保持性能的同时兼顾响应性与预测一致性。

Abstract: Diffusion- and flow-based robot policies have recently become widespread in robotic Imitation Learning (IL) due to their high performance and ability to model continuous and multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising procedure used by these models introduces significant prediction latency, hindering high-frequency closed-loop robot control and leading to jittery, unstable motion when frequent updates to the robot's action predictions are used. Therefore, it is common practice to train models to predict chunks of actions that can be executed sequentially without feedback, even when this reduces responsiveness and may mean the most recent state information is not used. In this article, we present Adaptive Mean Flow (AMF), a flow-based IL method that enables smooth and responsive, fully closed-loop robot control. AMF uses Mean Flow, which is an accelerated form of Flow Matching (FM), to minimize prediction latency. To ensure smoothness and consistency across predictions, AMF uses a corrupted version of the trajectory from the previous step when predicting new robot actions, with the signal-to-noise ratio increasing over the time parameter of the trajectory. This discourages large changes in the prediction from one step to the next, while allowing freedom to adapt the predictions for future steps. We evaluate AMF across a wide range of simulated and real robot tasks and demonstrate significantly improved performance compared with baselines. Code: https://github.com/akselva/Adaptive-mean-flow-RoboticIL.

</details>


### [337] [Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise for Consistent Asynchronous Replanning in Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06090)
*Di Wu,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出执行对齐渐进噪声（EAPN），通过在块间和块内引入结构化随机性，解决连续异步重规划中因独立随机初始化导致的模式切换与动作块不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时生成式机器人策略需要连续异步重规划，但独立随机初始化会导致动作块之间的模式切换和不一致延续，影响执行的连贯性和可靠性。

Method: EAPN在块间传播共享噪声轨迹并与实际执行位移对齐，建立执行对齐的块间相关性；在块内建模沿动作时间的时间相关性；并将对齐后的随机历史与已提交的动作上下文结合，条件化后续生成，使新块从执行一致的生成状态延续。

Result: 在D3IL上提升了多模态行为一致性；在Kinetix上取得88.59%平均成功率；在LIBERO上即使长推理延迟下仍保持稳健和强任务性能；真实机器人实验在Object Storage上达到90.0%成功率，在双臂Cloth Folding上达到96.7%。

Conclusion: EAPN通过执行对齐的结构化噪声机制，实现了异步重规划下的可靠连续执行，验证了其在仿真和真实机器人任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Continuous asynchronous replanning is essential for real-time generative robot policies, but independent stochastic initialization can cause mode switching and inconsistent continuation across action chunks. We propose Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise (EAPN), which introduces structured stochasticity at both inter-chunk and intra-chunk levels. Across replanning steps, EAPN propagates a shared noise trajectory and aligns it with the actual execution displacement, establishing execution-aligned inter-chunk correlation. Within each action chunk, it models temporal correlation along action time. The aligned stochastic history is further combined with committed action context to condition subsequent generation, allowing new chunks to continue from execution-consistent generative states rather than restart from independent noise. We evaluate EAPN on D3IL, Kinetix, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks. EAPN improves multimodal behavior consistency on D3IL and achieves an average success rate of 88.59% on Kinetix. On LIBERO, it remains robust and maintains strong task performance even under long inference delays. Real-robot experiments further achieve 90.0% success on Object Storage and 96.7% on bimanual Cloth Folding, demonstrating reliable continuous execution under asynchronous replanning.

</details>


### [338] [Propagating Elevation-Map Uncertainty Through the Contact Maximum in Closed Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06103)
*Aleš Kučera,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Clark成对递推的闭式方法计算接触最大值的矩，实现风险感知路径规划的精确不确定性传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知规划器用路径代价的均值与标准差在不确定高程图上评分。但建模刚性接触需要对多个不确定栅格取最大值：一阶传播只在单个栅格处求导导致精度损失，蒙特卡洛采样则每次抽样都需完整路径评估，效率低下。

Method: 用Clark成对递推闭式计算接触最大值的矩；通过追踪每个接触相对共享地图栅格的协方差，用精确高斯二次型恒等式传播平滑余项；再用一次在两条设计穿越上拟合的contest-depth校准，弥补残余的聚合标准差缺口。

Result: 在317条留出漫游车路径段上对照同一信念的蒙特卡洛参考，所有预注册标准均达成。校正Clark折叠将均值中位误差从线性化的2.3%降至0.19%，在90%水平CVaR上取得所有测试方法中最低误差；GPU上基准规划仅需5.5微秒。精度提升集中在争议接触处，98%决策中选择参考最优路径（线性化为93–96%）。第二个数据集上均值可迁移，但风险数值不可迁移。

Conclusion: 该闭式矩计算方法在接触最大值处显著提升精度与风险估计质量，且计算高效，但风险数值的跨数据集迁移性有限。

Abstract: Risk-aware planners score paths on uncertain elevation maps using the path cost's mean and standard deviation. Modeling rigid contact, however, requires computing a maximum over several uncertain cells. First-order propagation loses accuracy here by differentiating at only a single cell, while Monte Carlo sampling requires a full path evaluation per draw. We compute the moments of that contact maximum in closed form using Clark's pairwise recursion. By tracking each contact's covariance against the shared map cells, we propagate the smooth remainder using exact Gaussian quadratic-form identities. A contest-depth calibration, fitted once on two design traverses, closes the aggregate standard-deviation shortfall that remains. On 317 held-out rover path segments, scored against a Monte Carlo reference from the same belief, every pre-registered criterion was met. The corrected Clark fold cuts the median error of the mean from linearization's 2.3% to 0.19% and attains the lowest error in the conditional value at risk (CVaR) at the 90% level of every method tested. A benchmark plan costs just 5.5 microseconds on a GPU. These accuracy gains concentrate at contested contacts. While they seldom change which path is chosen on this terrain, the fold still selects the reference-best path in 98% of decisions against linearization's 93 to 96%. On a second dataset the mean transfers, though the risk number does not.

</details>


### [339] [Conditional Trajectory Peaks: Single-Pass Multimodal Policies over Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06104)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单次推理策略框架CTP，联合预测动作块候选、概率质量与轨迹尺度，兼顾多模态行为、闭环一致性与高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习需要在同一观测下生成多样且可执行的未来轨迹，同时保证重规划周期之间的行为一致性，而现有方法难以同时满足这两点。

Method: CTP联合预测完整动作块候选、概率质量与轨迹尺度；采用分布感知峰值特化（DAPS），基于轨迹级后验责任与质量、尺度调制的重叠约束来特化轨迹峰值；并采用证据门控轨迹信念传输（ETBT），通过可交换候选集间的几何对应维持跨块一致性，同时允许当前策略证据覆盖历史约束。

Result: Push-T覆盖率91.40%；D3IL的Avoiding、Aligning、Sorting-2成功率分别为100.0%、79.72%、84.44%；LIBERO平均成功率97.25%；真实双臂双盘任务50次全部成功并保留两种放置模式；在扶正瓶子和放笔入架任务中成功率与π₀.₅相当，推理延迟从218.24 ms降至75.80 ms。

Conclusion: 单次推理的轨迹建模能够同时实现多模态行为、闭环一致性与高效推理。

Abstract: Multimodal imitation learning requires diverse executable futures under the same observation and consistent behavior across replanning cycles. We present Conditional Trajectory Peaks (CTP), a single-pass policy framework that jointly predicts complete action-chunk candidates, probability masses, and trajectory scales. Distribution-Aware Peak Specialization (DAPS) specializes trajectory peaks using trajectory-level posterior responsibilities and mass- and scale-modulated overlap constraints. Evidence-Gated Trajectory Belief Transport (ETBT) maintains cross-chunk consistency through geometric correspondence between exchangeable candidate sets, while allowing current policy evidence to override historical constraints. CTP achieves a coverage score of 91.40% on Push-T; success rates of 100.0%, 79.72%, and 84.44% on D3IL Avoiding, Aligning, and Sorting-2, respectively. On LIBERO, CTP achieves an average success rate of 97.25%. In real-world dual-arm experiments, CTP preserves both placement modes in a two-plate task, succeeding in all 50 trials. On bottle uprighting and pen placement into a holder, it maintains success rates comparable to $π_{0.5}$ while reducing policy inference latency from 218.24 ms to 75.80 ms. These results demonstrate that single-pass trajectory modeling can combine multimodal behavior, closed-loop consistency, and efficient inference.

</details>


### [340] [Radar2Plan: Benchmarking 4D Radar for End-to-End Open-Loop Ego-Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06121)
*Ling Yao,Yichun Xiao,Jin Jin,Yihan Zhang,Fangqiang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Radar2Plan，首个面向真实4D毫米波雷达的开环自车轨迹规划基准，验证4D雷达单独即可支持有竞争力的规划并保持恶劣环境鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气与低光照是移动自主轨迹规划的主要难点。4D雷达在恶劣条件下感知可靠且提供径向速度，但感知鲁棒性未必转化为下游规划鲁棒性，且现有4D雷达基准多聚焦感知而非轨迹规划。

Method: 构建模块化基准Radar2Plan，通过统一接口连接传感器编码器、场景表示与规划头，支持受控对比不同传感器与规划器配置；基于DSERT-RoLL与MAN TruckScenes数据集，在统一协议下评估摄像头、4D雷达、LiDAR的七种组合、四种规划基线与12种天气/光照条件。

Result: 实验表明4D雷达单独即可支持有竞争力的自车轨迹规划，并在恶劣条件下保持鲁棒性能；传感器配置对比显示其与其他模态具有互补价值，同时揭示对传感器组合与规划架构的依赖性。

Conclusion: Radar2Plan模块化设计可扩展至更多数据集、传感模态与规划器，为基于雷达的自动驾驶规划研究提供灵活基础。

Abstract: Adverse weather and poor illumination remain major challenges for robust ego-trajectory planning in mobile autonomy. 4D radar offers reliable sensing under adverse conditions and direct radial-velocity measurements. However, sensing robustness does not necessarily translate into robust downstream planning, while existing 4D radar benchmarks focus primarily on perception rather than trajectory planning. We present Radar2Plan, a modular benchmark for open-loop ego-trajectory planning using real-world 4D radar data. Radar2Plan connects sensor encoders, scene representations, and planning heads through common interfaces, enabling controlled comparisons between different sensor and planner configurations. Using DSERT-RoLL and MAN TruckScenes, we evaluated seven combinations of camera, 4D radar, and LiDAR in four representative planning baselines and 12 weather and illumination conditions under a unified protocol. To our knowledge, Radar2Plan is the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating real-world 4D radars for autonomous driving planning. Experiments show that 4D radar alone supports competitive ego-trajectory planning and robust performance under adverse conditions. Sensor-configuration comparisons further demonstrate its complementary value to other modalities, while revealing dependencies on sensor combination and planning architecture. The modular design also supports additional datasets, sensing modalities, and planners, providing a flexible foundation for future research on radar-based autonomous driving planning.

</details>


### [341] [I-BFM: Reward-Conditioned Robust Humanoid Interaction via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06129)
*Ziqi Han,Yitang Li,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Yaojie Shen,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Yongqi Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Xue Wang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 I-BFM，首个面向人形机器人-物体交互的行为基础模型，通过前向-后向表示与无监督强化学习学习人形、物体及接触耦合动力学的共享表示，单一策略即可闭环执行多种交互与操控任务，并在真实机器人上验证了其鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFM）已证明单一的人形策略可支持多样化的全身控制，但将这种通用性扩展到物理交互仍然困难。现有方法多依赖任务专用策略或参考轨迹跟踪，缺乏对交互耦合动力学的通用表示，且难以在偏离标称执行后保持有效。

Method: I-BFM 不依赖任务专用策略或参考跟踪，而是利用前向-后向表示（forward-backward representations）和无监督强化学习，学习人形机器人、物体及其接触之间耦合动力学的共享表示。给定下游任务奖励，同一策略可直接以潜在命令为条件执行闭环交互，无需任务特定的策略优化。为改善不同时间尺度上的交互控制，策略同时用短时域交互目标和长时域目标进行训练。

Result: 单一 I-BFM 策略可完成搬运、推动和踢击，同时支持目标到达、运动跟踪、风格控制与长时域任务链。在 Carry 任务上，I-BFM 达到 94.3% 的标称成功率，机器人在跌倒后仍保留 89.3% 成功率，而基于规划的基线仅为 1.3%。在 Unitree G1 上的真实实验进一步展示了多样的移动-操控行为、交互失败与外部扰动后的快速恢复，以及无需任务专用重训练的任务链。

Conclusion: I-BFM 作为首个面向人形机器人-物体交互的行为基础模型，统一了多种交互与操控任务，并在大偏差及真实环境中保持鲁棒有效，展示了通用人形交互策略的可行性。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) have recently shown that a single humanoid policy can support diverse whole-body control, but extending such generality to physical interaction remains challenging. We introduce I-BFM, to our knowledge the first BFM for humanoid-object interaction. Rather than relying on task-specific policies or reference tracking, I-BFM learns a shared representation of the coupled dynamics among the humanoid, objects, and their contacts using forward-backward representations and unsupervised reinforcement learning. Given a downstream task reward, the same policy can be directly conditioned on a latent command to execute closed-loop interaction without task-specific policy optimization. To improve interaction control over different time scales, we further train the policy with both short-horizon interaction targets and longer-horizon goal targets. A single I-BFM policy performs carrying, pushing, and kicking, while also supporting goal reaching, motion tracking, stylistic control, and long-horizon task chaining. More importantly, it remains effective after large deviations from nominal execution: on Carry, I-BFM achieves 94.3% nominal success and retains 89.3% success after robot falls, compared with 1.3% for a planning-based baseline. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 further demonstrate diverse loco-manipulation behaviors, rapid recovery from interaction failures and external disturbances, and task chaining without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [342] [Robotizing Human Videos with Physically Consistent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06137)
*Ching-Lam Cheng,Shengfeng He,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对人手到机器人夹爪的具身差距问题，提出交互几何与场景可见性两方面的改进，通过接触重建与深度感知合成生成机器人兼容视频，并与机器人数据联合训练，在多个任务上取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频提供可扩展的操作数据，但人手与机器人末端执行器之间的具身差距限制了其直接使用。现有视频编辑方法虽用渲染机器人替换人手，却因交互重建不准确和合成不当，导致抓取不一致、机器人与物体遮挡不合理，因此需要从物理层面加以解决。

Method: 方法包含两个模块：一是交互感知接触重建模块，结合手-物分割与网格级接触预测，恢复稠密3D接触并转化为时序稳定的平行夹爪抓取；二是深度感知合成模块，利用场景与机器人深度信息，强制实现物理一致的机器人与物体遮挡。所得视频保留人类演示的交互结构并以机器人兼容形式呈现，再与机器人演示联合训练。

Result: 在使用相同人类视频与机器人数据的条件下，与仅机器人训练及原始Masquerade流程相比，该方法在四个RoboTwin任务和两种Diffusion Policy视觉编码器上取得最高平均成功率，尤其在分布外场景变化下增益显著。真实世界部署进一步表明，所提联合训练方法在任务几何可观测时提升了对视觉干扰物的鲁棒性，但在深度敏感抓取上仍受单相机设置限制。

Conclusion: 从交互几何与场景可见性两方面改进手-机器人视频转换，可有效弥合具身差距，提升策略学习的成功率和鲁棒性；未来需解决单相机条件下深度敏感抓取的局限。

Abstract: Human videos offer scalable manipulation data, but the embodiment gap between human hands and robot manipulators limits their direct use. Existing video-editing methods replace hands with rendered robots, yet inaccurate interaction reconstruction and compositing can produce inconsistent grasps and implausible robot-object occlusions. We address these failures from two complementary physical aspects: interaction geometry and scene visibility. First, an interaction-aware contact reconstruction module combines hand-object segmentation with mesh-level contact prediction to recover dense 3D contacts, then converts them into temporally stabilized grasps for parallel-jaw grippers. Second, a depth-aware compositing module uses scene and robot depth to enforce physically consistent robot-object occlusions. The resulting videos preserve the interaction structure of human demonstrations in a robot-compatible form and are co-trained with robot demonstrations. Using identical human videos and robot data, we compare against robot-only training and the original Masquerade pipeline. Across four RoboTwin tasks and two Diffusion Policy visual encoders, our method achieves the highest average success rates, with especially strong gains under out-of-distribution scene variation. Real-world deployment further shows that the proposed co-training approach improves robustness to visual distractors when the task geometry is observable, while performance on depth-sensitive grasps remains limited by the single-camera setup.

</details>


### [343] [Talk, Render, Act: Integrating Social Gesture and Digital Face with Synchronized Speech for Conversational Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06153)
*Jin Jiang,Kun Li,Jiancong Ma,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRABot是一个基于智能体框架的人形机器人交互系统，通过语义运动原子、语义条件组合规划器和流式面部-语音-身体集成，实现语音、面部动画与身体手势的协调统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人交互中，语音与手势生成常常缺乏视觉表现力丰富的面部，而说话人脸动画与机器人手势生成通常被分开研究，导致多模态响应不连贯。

Method: 提出TRABot智能体框架，包含运动原子构建、对话生成、运动规划和面部动画等专门智能体。构建Robot-Ready Semantic Motion Atoms，将G1重定向的BEAT2长时动作分割为具有自然手势边界、人工验证交际功能和可行轨迹的单元；引入语义条件组合规划器，根据有序语义功能序列和预估响应时长选择已批准原子，实现最长可行动作序列并考虑过渡与中性恢复；在实体G1人形上部署流式面部-语音-身体集成系统，融合流式对话音频、音频驱动面部动画与语义规划身体动作。

Result: 定量与定性实验表明，TRABot在自然度、表现力和多模态连贯性方面均优于所有对比条件，取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: TRABot通过智能体化框架与语义条件规划，实现了语音、面部与身体动作的统一实时交互，验证了多模态集成在人形机器人表达性交互中的有效性。

Abstract: Expressive humanoid interaction requires speech, facial animation, and body gestures to form a coherent response. However, many full-body humanoid robots produce speech and gestures without a visually expressive face, while talking-face animation and robot gesture generation are typically developed separately. We present Talk, Render, Act (TRABot), an agent-based framework comprising specialized agents for motion-atom construction, dialogue generation, motion planning, and facial animation. First, to produce natural and semantically meaningful gestures, we construct Robot-Ready Semantic Motion Atoms by segmenting long-form, G1-retargeted BEAT2 motion into units with natural gesture boundaries, human-verified communicative functions, and feasible trajectories. Second, to preserve semantic order and coordinate body motion with the spoken response, we introduce a Semantic-Conditioned Compositional Planner. Given an ordered semantic function sequence and an estimated response duration, the planner selects approved atoms to realize the longest feasible action sequence while accounting for transitions and neutral recovery. Finally, we deploy a Streaming Face-Speech-Body Integration system on a physical G1 humanoid, combining streaming dialogue audio, audio-driven facial animation, and semantically planned body motion in a unified real-time interaction loop. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that TRAbot achieves the best overall performance among all compared conditions in terms of naturalness, expressiveness, and multimodal coherence.

</details>


### [344] [Controllable and Photorealistic Pedestrian Risky Motion Generation for End-to-End Driving Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06171)
*Siyuan Liu,Miao Li,Haibao Yu,Haohong Lin,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ControlPed框架结合轨迹级冲突合成与3D高斯泼溅，生成逼真且运动可控的安全关键行人场景，用于评估端到端自动驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在罕见且安全关键的车辆-行人交互场景中评估端到端自动驾驶需要逼真、传感器级的场景。但基于轨迹的生成器无法合成原始视觉观测，而基于视频的方法缺乏可控性，因此存在明显空白。

Method: 提出ControlPed，结合轨迹级冲突合成与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）。基于HazardPed数据集（源自10,352个交通视频，含422条冲突轨迹、HD地图与857个标注3D人体动作），首先生成冲突轨迹，通过文本条件运动扩散将其提升为3D人体运动序列，最后利用可动画3DGS化身渲染多视角传感器观测。

Result: 在88个渲染的逼真场景中进行安全评估，七个领先的端到端驾驶模型性能严重下降，平均HDScore从88.8骤降至47.4，暴露出在危险行人行为下的主要失效模式。

Conclusion: ControlPed能生成逼真、运动可控的安全关键场景，有效揭示端到端驾驶模型在车辆-行人交互中的安全缺陷，数据集与测试基准将公开以促进安全评估。

Abstract: Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving under rare, safety-critical vehicle-pedestrian interactions requires photorealistic, sensor-level scenarios. However, trajectory-based scenario generators cannot synthesize raw visual observations, whereas video-based approaches lack controllability. To bridge this gap, we present ControlPed, a novel framework that combines trajectory-level conflict synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to generate photorealistic, motion-controllable safety-critical scenarios. Built upon HazardPed, a dataset derived from 10,352 traffic videos comprising 422 conflict trajectories, HD maps, and 857 annotated 3D human motions, ControlPed first generates conflict trajectories, lifts them into 3D human motion sequences via text-conditioned motion diffusion, and finally renders multi-view sensor observations using animatable 3DGS avatars. Safety evaluation in 88 rendered photorealistic scenarios reveals that seven leading end-to-end driving models suffer a severe performance drop, with their mean HDScore plunging from 88.8 to 47.4, exposing major failure modes under dangerous pedestrian behaviors. The dataset and testing benchmarks will be released to facilitate safety assessment of vehicle-pedestrian interactions.

</details>


### [345] [Arm-wise Compositional Generalization in Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06184)
*Zaibin Zhang,Binghao Ran,Yuhan Wu,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Junwei Jiang,Junlan Xiao,Wangcheng Shi,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Zhenfei Yin,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂组合泛化基准 ACG-Bench，并基于 π0.5 主干研究数据增强与架构策略，最终方法 AE-VLA 在仿真与真机上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协作的泛化可视为将已掌握的原子技能以新方式组合。但现有评测无法揭示在不同协调需求下，哪些训练与架构选择能支撑这种能力，因此需要一个统一的测试平台来研究技能重组。

Method: 构建 ACG-Bench，包含 8 个任务族的 23 个任务-条件对（6 个域内条件、17 个未见组合，涵盖重排序、同步、二者结合及跨任务组合）；所有方法使用相同的单臂原子提示，成功需同时满足任务目标、物理里程碑及顺序/时序约束。以 π0.5 作为统一 VLA 主干，比较数据增强与架构策略；架构研究考察臂 token 分组、技能专用 LoRA 适配器（SkillLoRA）与臂间注意力（AWA），并将这些选择组合为 AE-VLA。

Result: 仿真中 AE-VLA 泛化成功率达 21.53%，而 Single π0.5 为 2.94%、MA-VLA 为 3.06%、两个独立控制 π0.5 为 5.53%。在物理 SO101 机器人上，AE-VLA 在五个未见条件下平均成功率 39.00%，最强基线为 10.00%。

Conclusion: 技能条件化参数与注意力结构具有互补性，二者结合可显著提升双臂策略在固定训练流程之外的泛化能力，为设计双臂策略提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Generalization in multi-arm collaboration can be studied as composing familiar atomic skills in new ways across arms. However, existing evaluations offer limited insight into which training and architectural choices support this ability under different coordination requirements. We introduce \textbf{ACG-Bench}, a benchmark for \emph{Arm-wise Compositional Generalization} that provides a common testbed for studying skill recomposition in dual-arm policies. It contains 23 task--condition pairs across 8 task families, with 6 in-domain conditions and 17 unseen compositions covering reordering, synchronization, their combination, and cross-task composition. All methods receive the same per-arm atomic prompts, and success requires achieving the task goal while satisfying physical milestones and specified order or timing constraints. Using $π_{0.5}$ as a common vision-language-action backbone, we compare representative data-augmentation and architectural strategies with shared source data and a common evaluation protocol. Our architectural study examines arm-token grouping, skill-specific LoRA adapters (SkillLoRA), and arm-wise attention (AWA), highlighting the complementarity of skill-conditioned parameters and attention structure. Combining these choices yields \textbf{AE-VLA}, which achieves 21.53\% generalization success in simulation, compared with 2.94\% for Single $π_{0.5}$, 3.06\% for MA-VLA, and 5.53\% for two independently controlled $π_{0.5}$ policies. On physical SO101 robots, AE-VLA reaches 39.00\% mean success across five unseen conditions, compared with 10.00\% for the strongest baseline. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing dual-arm policies that generalize beyond fixed training routines.

</details>


### [346] [STC-MPM: Coupled Deformation, Progressive Damage, and Cut Formation in Soft-Tissue Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06198)
*Kewei Zuo,Jiahe Chen,Ziao Kuang,Junsheng Zhu,Etsuko Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STC-MPM框架，耦合有限应变变形、历史驱动连续损伤与可配置失效后处理，用于分析软组织切割中的切口形成与力学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人切割中，高顺应性目标会因刀片运动而变形或位移，渐进失效改变载荷传递和后续刀具-组织交互。传统方法将切口独立于材料失效来指定，无法描述切割形成与演化力学响应的联系。

Method: STC-MPM结合有限应变变形、历史驱动连续损伤和可配置失效后处理。损伤仅在刀刃过程区内拉伸应变历史超过材料阈值时启动；渐进退化改变应力传递后再应用失效后策略，剩余组织继续变形、移动并与刀具交互。采用延迟粒子失活的数值手术刀研究追踪插入与撤出过程。

Result: 在相同刀片运动下，降低损伤速率上限不改变首次记录的损伤时间，但会延迟并提高峰值反作用力、延迟粒子失活，并增大最大插入时的固定队列位移统计量。该对比说明失效启动后的演化如何改变后续刀具载荷与记录的材料运动。

Conclusion: STC-MPM支持无需预置切割界面的切口形成与伴随组织响应的联合分析。

Abstract: Cutting is a recurring operation in robotic au tomation, from food preparation to surgical tissue resection. For highly compliant targets, blade motion may deform or displace the material rather than advance the intended cut, while progressive failure changes load transfer and subsequent tool tissue interaction. A computational description must therefore connect cut formation to the evolving mechanical response, rather than specifying the incision independently of material failure. We present STC-MPM (Soft Tissue Cutting with the Material Point Method), a framework that couples finite-strain deformation, history-driven continuum damage, and configurable post-failure treatment. Damage initiates only when a tensile strain history exceeds a material threshold within the blade process zone. Progressive degradation changes stress transmission before the post-failure policy is applied, while the remaining tissue continues to deform, move, and interact with the tool. Numerical scalpel studies using delayed particle deactivation follow this response through insertion and withdrawal. Under identical blade motion, reducing the damage-rate limit leaves the first recorded damage time unchanged but delays and increases peak reaction force, delays particle deactivation, and increases the fixed-cohort displacement statistic at maximum insertion. The matched comparison illustrates how post-initiation failure evolution alters subsequent tool loading and recorded material motion. STC-MPM thus supports joint analysis of cut formation and accompanying tissue response without a pre-inserted cutting interface.

</details>


### [347] [Towards Quadruped-Provided Localization and Active Tracking for Micro-UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06215)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将微型无人机的定位任务完全从飞行平台卸载到装配7自由度机械臂的四足机器人上，由地面机器人充当主动视觉观测者，为仅携带IMU的27克微型无人机提供共享地图坐标系下的6自由度位姿，实现自主飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机体积小、可进入大型机器人无法到达的受限空间，但载荷能力有限，无法携带自主飞行所需的传感与计算资源，因此需要将定位任务外包给外部平台。

Method: 在四足机器人7自由度机械臂末端相机上检测无人机上的AprilTag标记，并将其观测与四足机器人自身定位融合，把无人机置于共享地图坐标系中；机械臂作为主动控制的观测者，随两机器人运动重新调整姿态以保持无人机在视野内；无人机仅携带IMU并融合外部位姿来飞向指令设定点。

Result: 实验中外部位姿精度达12-16 mm，自主飞行与动捕驱动控制的偏差在2-5 cm以内；四足机器人主动跟随无人机可将跟踪误差从11.0 cm降至6.9 cm。

Conclusion: 通过将定位栈完全卸载到具备机械臂的地面机器人并采用主动观测策略，可在极轻量微型无人机上实现接近动捕精度的自主飞行，主动跟随通过保持近距离观测有效提升标记定位精度。

Abstract: Micro unmanned aerial vehicles (micro-UAVs) are small enough to reach confined spaces that larger robots cannot access, but too small to carry the sensing and computing power required for autonomous flight. We move the localization stack entirely off the aerial platform onto a quadruped robot with a 7-degree-of-freedom (DOF) arm, which supplies the micro-UAV (27 g bare, 42 g with fiducial markers) its full 6-DOF pose. A camera at the arm's end-effector detects AprilTag fiducial markers on the drone and composes that observation with the quadruped's own self-localization to place the drone in a shared map frame, so the ground robot localizes its partner, rather than only tracking it relative to the camera. The arm acts as an actively-controlled observer, repositioning to keep the drone in view as both robots move; the drone carries only an inertial measurement unit and fuses the external pose to fly commanded setpoints. In lab flights the external pose is accurate to 12-16 mm, enough to fly the drone autonomously within 2-5 cm of motion-capture-fed control. Having the quadruped actively follow the drone reduces the tracking error from 11.0 cm to 6.9 cm by holding the camera in the close range, where the markers are most accurate.

</details>


### [348] [AUTOPILOT An Advanced Perception, Localization and Path Planning Techniques for Autonomous Vehicles Using YOLOv7 and MiDaS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06232)
*Harshkumar Devmurari,Gautham Kuckian,Prajjwal Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将 YOLO 目标检测、MiDaS 深度估计、透视变换与路径规划决策相结合，构建自动驾驶感知与定位系统，并通过仿真与实验验证其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆有望变革交通与出行方式，但其发展依赖感知、定位、决策与控制等多方面的进展。论文旨在探索一种结合目标检测与深度感知的低成本方案，以提升障碍物感知与环境定位能力。

Method: 采用 YOLO 进行目标检测，MiDaS 进行深度感知以实现障碍物定位与感知，结合透视变换处理图像视角，并通过决策模块完成路径规划；同时评估了相关技术的现状，并通过仿真与实验测试该组合系统的性能。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明，YOLO 与 MiDaS 的组合提供了一种新的、鲁棒的目标检测与深度感知系统。

Conclusion: 该研究推动了自动驾驶技术的发展，并为环境中障碍物的感知与定位提供了新颖的思路与方法。

Abstract: Self driving vehicles have emerged as a reliable technology that has the capability to transform transportation and mobility. The development of self driving cars requires significant advances in a number of areas, including perception, localization, decision making, and control. This research paper is based on the project implementation of the combination of object detection using YOLO (You Only Look Once), depth sensing using MiDaS for the localization and perception of obstacles, perspective transform, and decision making for path planning in self driving cars. The contemporary state of the technology for object detection, depth sensing, localization, and path planning evaluates the performance of the combined system through simulations and experiments. The results show that the combination of YOLO and MiDaS provides a new robust system for object detection and depth sensing. This research paper contributes to the advancement of self driving car technology and provides new and innovative approaches to the perception and localization of obstacles in the environment. Keywords: YOLO, MiDaS, perception, localization, decision making

</details>


### [349] [Encoded but Not in Control: Revealing the Grounding Gap in Vision-Language Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06235)
*Shaohan Jiang,Jiahang Cao,Qiduo He,Fengting Deng,Kun Wu,Jingkai Sun,Jiaxu Wang,Qiang Zhang,Qihao Zheng,Chunfeng Song,Ping Luo,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文通过"保持场景不变的指令干预"实验揭示：VLA策略与WAMs的任务成功并不代表真正遵循指令，模型往往依据场景而非语言选择目标；尽管语言扰动会影响动作并在线性探针中可解码，但指令token对动作生成的贡献微弱，存在"编码—执行"之间的grounding gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言条件机器人策略中，语言应决定当同一场景允许多种有效动作时该做什么。然而仅凭执行成功无法判断策略是遵循指令还是从场景推断任务，这一歧义此前缺乏系统研究，本文旨在界定并诊断该问题。

Method: 采用场景保持不变的指令干预方法，包括有效目标替换、任意名词和无关句子；在仿真与真实世界实验中评估视觉-语言-动作策略（VLA）与世界动作模型（WAMs）。分析手段包括逐层动作透镜（layerwise action lens）、线性探针、注意力分析、UMAP与共享非负矩阵分解（NMF）。

Result: (a) 当指令要求不同可见物体时，所有评估策略仍主要接近并抓取与场景关联的原始错误目标，说明成功不代表遵循指令。(b) 指令扰动确实影响任务性能，逐层动作透镜显示中间动作预测对扰动有响应，线性探针能准确恢复被指令的目标，说明语言被编码但很少决定目标选择。(c) 注意力分析显示指令token对动作生成贡献微弱，目标token注意力仍集中于原始物体，暴露目标编码与视觉grounding的错配；UMAP与共享NMF表明目标信息仍可获取，但表征日益按场景身份组织。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了被"名义成功"掩盖的grounding gap，并提供了诊断框架；提出具体进步标准：策略应能可靠遵循用户意图的有效变化，即使其与场景偏好行为相冲突。

Abstract: Instruction following is central to language-conditioned robot policies: language should determine what to do when the same scene permits multiple valid actions. Yet successful execution alone cannot establish whether a policy follows the instruction or infers the task from the scene. We study this ambiguity through scene-preserving instruction interventions, using valid target substitutions, arbitrary nouns, and unrelated sentences while holding the scene fixed. We evaluate vision-language-action (VLA) policies and world-action models (WAMs) in simulation and in real-world experiments. Our analysis addresses three questions: (a) Does task success imply instruction following? When instructions request a different visible object, all evaluated policies predominantly approach and pick up the incorrect original target associated with the scene. (b) Is this failure caused by language insensitivity? Instruction perturbations affect task performance. A layerwise action lens shows intermediate action predictions respond to these perturbations. Linear probes accurately recover instructed targets, indicating modified instructions are encoded despite rarely determining target selection. (c) Why does encoded language fail to control action? Attention analysis indicates weak instruction-token contributions to action generation. Target-token attention can remain focused on the original object, revealing a mismatch between target encoding and visual grounding. UMAP and shared non-negative matrix factorization show target information remains accessible within representations increasingly organized by scene identity. Our findings expose a grounding gap concealed by nominal success and provide a diagnostic framework. They further establish a concrete criterion for progress: policies should reliably follow valid changes in user intent, even when they conflict with scene-favored behavior.

</details>


### [350] [VLA-ZO: Fast Zeroth-Order Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06271)
*Jaemin Kim,Jiahn Kim,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 VLA-ZO 框架，通过将适应限制在动作端并复用视觉语言前缀条件状态，大幅加速 VLA 模型的零阶适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时分布漂移对 VLA 模型可靠运行很重要，但常规一阶适应超出推理平台的内存预算；零阶优化虽内存友好但查询过多导致过慢。

Method: VLA-ZO 将适应限制在动作端，冻结昂贵的视觉语言前缀，在扰动查询和优化步骤间复用其条件状态，并用调度感知预取隐藏状态传输开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 相机视角漂移上，q=16 时端到端适应时间减少 25.59 倍，q=64 时减少 32.54 倍；平均任务成功率从无适应的 48.27% 提升到 58.17% 和 63.58%。

Conclusion: 加速零阶优化使更大的查询预算变得可行，为部署平台上资源高效的 VLA 适应提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language-action (VLA) models to deployment-time distribution shifts is important for reliable robotic operation, but conventional first-order adaptation can exceed the memory budget of inference-oriented deployment platforms. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a forward-only alternative with inference-level memory, but accurate gradient estimation requires many perturbation queries, making naive ZO prohibitively slow for large VLA models. We present VLA-ZO, a framework for fast ZO adaptation that exploits the structure of VLA computation. By confining adaptation to the action side, VLA-ZO keeps the expensive vision-language prefix frozen and reuses its conditioning states across perturbation queries and optimizer steps, while schedule-aware prefetching hides state-transfer overhead. On LIBERO camera-viewpoint shifts, VLA-ZO reduces end-to-end adaptation time by 25.59$\times$ at $q=16$ and 32.54$\times$ at $q=64$ relative to baseline ZO, while improving average task success from 48.27% without adaptation to 58.17% and 63.58%, respectively. These results show that making ZO faster can make larger query budgets practical, providing a promising path toward resource-efficient VLA adaptation on deployment platforms.

</details>


### [351] [Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06280)
*Zhibin Qin,Zhenxiong Tan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAVOR框架，利用世界动作模型预测的未来帧作为锚点进行验证与在线恢复，无需修改策略即可提升机器人操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）先预测任务执行方式再解码动作，但一旦执行偏离预测，剩余动作即失效。从已处于分布外的状态重新规划很难恢复任务所需，而现有执行监控只能决定何时停止，无法决定恢复什么。

Method: FAVOR保留WAM行动前预测的未来帧作为锚点。锚点验证器将每次观测与其锚点及已执行动作对比，标记破坏任务的偏差。锚点引导恢复利用视觉语言模型将标记的锚点转化为简短纠正指令，在强化指令引导下WAM执行该指令回到预期未来，随后恢复任务。

Result: 在不修改策略的情况下，FAVOR将基础WAM的任务成功率从LIBERO上的97.85%提升至98.10%，从LIBERO-Plus上的72.60%提升至72.98%。

Conclusion: FAVOR通过将预测未来帧作为锚点用于验证与恢复，提供了轻量级的在线纠错机制，有效提升了世界动作模型的机器人操作任务成功率。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. They act by first predicting how a task should be performed and then decoding the actions from that future. However, the remaining actions are invalid once execution drifts from the prediction. Simply replanning from the already out of distribution state rarely restores what the task still requires; existing execution monitors decide when to stop, but not what to restore. We observe that the answer is already in hand: the future the WAM predicted before acting depicts exactly the states it intended to pass through. We introduce FAVOR (Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery), a lightweight framework that keeps these predicted frames as anchors and uses them for verification and recovery. An Anchor Verifier compares each observation with its anchor, together with the executed actions, to flag deviations that break the task. Anchor-Guided Recovery uses a vision-language model to turn the flagged anchor into a short corrective instruction. Under strengthened instruction guidance, the WAM executes this instruction to return to the intended future. It then resumes the task. FAVOR raises the task success of the base WAM from 97.85% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 72.60% to 72.98% on LIBERO-Plus without modifying the policy.

</details>


### [352] [GAMBIT: Learning to Plan Continuous Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06290)
*Rishabh Jain,Akmaral Moldagalieva,Lorenzo Magnino,Michael Amir,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Wolfgang Hönig,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMBIT框架，通过模仿学习与强化学习结合，学习多机器人协调的运动基元选择策略，并引入带备份轨迹的安全展开机制，在保证无碰撞的同时实现千机器人规模的高效协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协调中，个体机器人可能需要放弃局部最优行为以提升整体团队表现（类似国际象棋中的"弃子"策略）。这类自我牺牲行为在密集、交互丰富的环境中难以用人工设计的启发式规则捕捉，因此需要学习式的协调启发式方法。

Method: 针对双积分器连续动力学，GAMBIT首先通过模仿学习学习协调的运动基元选择策略，随后用强化学习对策略进行微调；此外引入带备份轨迹的安全展开机制（safeguarded rollout），以保证执行过程始终无碰撞。

Result: 实验表明GAMBIT显著优于多种基线方法，包括集中式运动规划器和分散式反应式规划器，并展现出强扩展性：可在连续域中协调超过一千个机器人，规划延迟低于几百毫秒。

Conclusion: 学习式的协调启发式（模仿学习+强化学习）配合安全保障机制，能够有效实现大规模、低延迟、无碰撞的多机器人轨迹执行，优于传统集中式与分散式规划方法。

Abstract: GAMBIT is an opening chess move in which a player sacrifices a piece, typically a pawn, to gain a positional advantage later in the game. Analogously, in multi-robot coordination, individual robots may need to forgo locally reward-maximising behaviours to improve overall team performance. Such self-sacrificial behaviours are difficult to capture with manually designed heuristics, particularly in dense, interaction-rich environments. Focusing on double-integrator continuous dynamics, this work studies how to learn such coordinated heuristics over motion primitives for multi-robot trajectory execution. Our framework, GAMBIT, first learns coordinated motion-primitive selection through imitation learning and subsequently fine-tunes the policy through reinforcement learning. We further introduce a safeguarded rollout mechanism with backup trajectories that guarantees collision-free execution at all times. Experiments demonstrate that GAMBIT substantially outperforms a range of baselines, including centralised motion planners and decentralised reactive planners, while exhibiting strong scalability. In particular, it coordinates over a thousand robots with planning latency below a few hundred milliseconds in continuous domains.

</details>


### [353] [Inspect Robots: Evaluating the Capabilities and Safety of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06306)
*Christopher Leet,Achu Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sabrina Zou,Aayushya Patel,Aditya Kumar Singh,Anish Kr Singh,Galaba Vamsi,Javin Ahuja,Sai Asish Yamani,Tushar Anand,Vedang Alle,Zihan Jack Zhang,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 Inspect Robots 的模块化开源框架，用于开发和运行具身智能体的评估，并展示了其在评估前沿语言模型驱动的机器人策略的能力与安全性方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型日益具备控制机器人硬件的能力，因此理解这些模型在具身化后的能力与安全问题，对于评估其社会影响和风险变得愈发重要。

Method: 提出 Inspect Robots 框架，提供可定制、可复用的抽象接口用于指定物理评估并分析结果，同时配套自动化评估的搭建、执行与终止的基础设施。并使用该框架评估了六个基于前沿语言模型的策略。

Result: 成功利用 Inspect Robots 评估了六个基于前沿语言模型的策略的能力与安全性；框架发布三个月内获得近 10 万次下载，早期采用度显著。

Conclusion: Inspect Robots 为具身智能体的评估提供了模块化、开源且易用的工具支持，并已获得广泛关注和应用。

Abstract: General purpose language models are increasingly able to control robotic hardware. Understanding the capabilities and safety of these models when embodied is therefore increasingly important for understanding their societal impact and risks. To this end, we introduce Inspect Robots, a modular, open-source framework for developing and running evaluations of embodied agents. Inspect Robots pairs customizable, reusable abstractions for specifying physical evaluations and analyzing their results with infrastructure that automates evaluation setup, execution and termination.
